## 1350542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん！<line-break>私たちはただ兄さんの身体を心配して…<end>"
EN: "Brother! We're only worried about your health...<end>"
KO: "오빠! 우리는 그저 오빠 몸이 걱정돼서….<end>"

## 1350543
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別にお前らがいなくても<line-break>俺はやっていける。<end>"
EN: "I can get by just fine without you two.<end>"
KO: "너희가 없어도 나는 잘 해낼 수 있어.<end>"

## 1350544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ！<line-break>私たちなんてもう要らない、<line-break>つきまとうなって言いたいわけ！？<end>"
EN: "What!? Are you saying you don't need us anymore? That you want us to stop following you around!?<end>"
KO: "뭐야! 우린 이제 필요 없으니, 따라다니지 말라는 거야!?<end>"

## 1350545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そうだな。<line-break>ロストールが滅んでエリスが死んだら、<line-break>お前らとつるむうまみもないしな。<end>"
EN: "Yes, that's right. Once Rostorl has fallen and Eris is dead, there'll be nothing in it for me, hanging around with you two.<end>"
KO: "그래, 그렇지. 로스톨이 망하고 에리스가 죽으면 너희랑 어울릴 이득도 없으니까.<end>"

## 1350546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん、それはないんじゃない！？<line-break>最近ヘンよ！　みょうに邪険で！<line-break>昔は…こんなじゃなかった！<end>"
EN: "Brother, how can you say that!? You've been acting so strange lately―so cold to us! You weren't like this before!<end>"
KO: "오빠, 그건 아니잖아!? 요즘 이상해! 묘하게 매정하고! 예전엔… 이렇지 않았어!<end>"

## 1350547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはお前らが幻想を抱いてただけさ。<line-break>俺はこういう人間だ、<line-break>勝手な妄想を押しつけるな。<end>"
EN: "You only thought I wasn't. This is the kind of man I am. Don't force your fantasies on me.<end>"
KO: "그건 너희가 멋대로 환상을 품었을 뿐이야. 난 원래 이런 인간이다. 제멋대로 망상을 들이밀지 마.<end>"

## 1350548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お兄ちゃん！！<end>"
EN: "Big brother!!<end>"
KO: "오빠!!<end>"

## 1350549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お見苦しいところをお目にかけました…<line-break>申しわけありません。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I apologize for subjecting you to such an unseemly scene.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 추한 모습을 보여 드렸습니다… 죄송합니다.<end>"

## 1350550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、助けてよ、<value:$28>！<line-break>お兄ちゃんが！　お兄ちゃんがヘンなの<line-break>ねえ、もう私、どうしていいのか…！<end>"
EN: "Please help, <value:$28>! My brother... something's wrong with him! I don't know what to do anymore...!<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 도와줘, <value:$28>! 오빠가! 오빠가 이상해. 나 이제 어떻게 해야 할지 모르겠어…!<end>"

## 1350551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼いたしました。<line-break>今、妹は動転しておりまして…<line-break>ロストールのスラムはご存じですか？<end>"
EN: "Please excuse us. My younger sister is distraught at the moment... Are you familiar with the Slums of Rostorl?<end>"
KO: "실례했습니다. 지금 제 동생이 크게 동요하고 있어서…. 로스톨의 슬럼을 아십니까?<end>"

## 1350552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、そのスラム酒場の奥の部屋にて、<line-break>のちほど事情をご説明いたします。<line-break>失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Then I will explain everything later, in the back room of the tavern in the Slums. If you will excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 슬럼가 술집 안쪽 방에서 나중에 사정을 설명드리겠습니다. 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1350553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…？<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>ホントに来てくれたの？<end>"
EN: "What...? <value:$28>, you really came?<end>"
KO: "어…? <value:$28>, 정말 와 준 거야?<end>"

## 1350554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹が無礼なことを申しまして、<line-break>まことに申しわけございません。<line-break>ですが、本当に来ていただけるとは…<line-break>兄は正直、<value:$28>様に<line-break>ご迷惑ばかりかけており<line-break>また私たちも…<end>"
EN: "Please forgive my sister's rudeness. I am truly sorry. But I never imagined you would actually come... In truth, our brother has caused you nothing but trouble, <value:$28>, and now we have as well...<end>"
KO: "동생이 무례한 말을 해서 정말 죄송합니다. 하지만 정말 와 주실 줄은…. 형님은 솔직히 <value:$28> 님께 폐만 끼쳤고, 또 저희도….<end>"

## 1350555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのお志、誠にありがとうございます。<line-break>つまらぬことを言いまして<line-break>申しわけございませんでした。<end>"
EN: "You have my deepest gratitude for your kindness. Please forgive me for saying such foolish things.<end>"
KO: "그 뜻, 진심으로 감사합니다. 쓸데없는 말을 해서 죄송했습니다.<end>"

## 1350556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、兄が<line-break>私たちを避けるようになり、<line-break>姿を見せなくなったのです。<line-break>兄はいちじるしく健康を損なっており、<line-break>ひとりでは満足に日常生活も送れません。<end>"
EN: "Recently, our brother began avoiding us and then stopped showing himself altogether. His health has deteriorated severely, and he cannot properly manage day-to-day life on his own.<end>"
KO: "최근 형이 우리를 피하기 시작해 모습을 보이지 않습니다. 형은 건강이 크게 나빠져 혼자서는 일상생활도 제대로 할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1350557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、兄は日頃から行いが悪いため、<line-break>敵は多く<line-break>味方は私たちしかいません。<line-break>しかし、私たちには唯一の肉親であり、<line-break>私たちがここまで生きて来られたのも<line-break>兄のおかげなのです。<end>"
EN: "Our brother's conduct has earned him many enemies, and my sister and I are his only allies. But he is our only other blood relative, and it is thanks to him that we have survived this long.<end>"
KO: "또한 형은 평소 행실이 좋지 않아 적이 많고, 아군은 저희뿐입니다. 하지만 저희에겐 유일한 혈육이고, 저희가 지금까지 살아올 수 있었던 것도 형 덕분입니다.<end>"

## 1350558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い<line-break>兄さんを捜して！<end>"
EN: "Please, find my brother!<end>"
KO: "부탁이야, 오빠를 찾아줘!<end>"

## 1350559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350560
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちはここに集まって<line-break>情報交換をすることにしています。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>様も<line-break>時間に余裕のある時でけっこうですので、<line-break>いらしていただけませんか？<end>"
EN: "We have decided to gather here and exchange information. <value:$28>, would you join us whenever you have time to spare?<end>"
KO: "저희는 여기 모여 정보를 교환하기로 했습니다. <value:$28>님도 시간이 여유로울 때면 괜찮으니 와주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1350561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1350562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大神官様と兄との間で<line-break>そんなことが！<end>"
EN: "So that is what happened between our brother and the High Priest!<end>"
KO: "대신관님과 형 사이에 그런 일이!<end>"

## 1350563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さんは自分が長くないと覚って、<line-break>私たちに心配かけずに死ぬために<line-break>虚無の剣を捜し出そうとしてるのね？<end>"
EN: "So he knows he doesn't have long, and he's trying to find the Sword of the Void so he can die without making us worry?<end>"
KO: "오빠는 자신에게 시간이 얼마 남지 않았다는 걸 깨닫고, 우리를 걱정시키지 않고 죽기 위해 허무의 검을 찾아내려는 거지?<end>"

## 1350564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそらく闇の神器を集めるための<line-break>シャリの罠ですね。<line-break>このままでは兄さんが危ない…<end>"
EN: "This is probably a trap laid by Shalli to gather the Dark Artifacts. At this rate, our brother is in danger...<end>"
KO: "아마 어둠의 신기를 모으기 위한 샤리의 함정이겠죠. 이대로면 오빠가 위험해….<end>"

## 1350565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "虚無の剣や、兄さんの行きそうな場所<line-break>心当たりない？<end>"
EN: "Do you know anything about the Sword of the Void, or anywhere my brother might have gone?<end>"
KO: "허무의 검이나 형이 갈 만한 곳, 짐작 가는 데 없어?<end>"

## 1350566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今…、闇の門の島…と<line-break>おっしゃいましたね。<line-break>いったい？<end>"
EN: "Just now... you said “the Island of the Dark Gate,” did you not? What did you mean?<end>"
KO: "방금… 어둠의 문 섬…이라고 하셨죠. 대체 무슨 뜻입니까?<end>"

## 1350567
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご自身でも<line-break>なぜそんなことをおっしゃったのか<line-break>おわかりにならないのですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you mean you do not even know why you said that?<end>"
KO: "본인도 왜 그런 말씀을 하셨는지 모르시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1350568
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、信じられるよ。<line-break>今、<value:$28>のソウルから<line-break>すごく懐かしい感じがしたから。<end>"
EN: "But I believe it. Just now, I felt something so familiar coming from <value:$28>'s Soul.<end>"
KO: "그래도 믿을 수 있어. 지금 <value:$28>의 소울에서 아주 그리운 느낌이 들었거든.<end>"

## 1350569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この感じは…まるで…<end>"
EN: "This feeling... It is almost as though...<end>"
KO: "이 느낌은… 마치….<end>"

## 1350570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手がかりはなし、か。<line-break>だけど、こうしちゃいられないわ。<line-break>行こう、ヴァイ！<end>"
EN: "So we have no leads. But we can't just sit around. Come on, Vai!<end>"
KO: "단서는 없나. 하지만 이러고 있을 순 없어. 가자, 바이!<end>"

## 1350571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か情報は？<end>"
EN: "Any news?<end>"
KO: "무슨 정보라도 있어?<end>"

## 1350572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…<line-break>こちらも収穫なしです。<end>"
EN: "I see... We haven't learned anything either.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까… 이쪽도 수확이 없습니다.<end>"

## 1350573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3え？<line-break>私の顔…、うれしそうに<line-break>見えますか…。<line-break>…困りました。<line-break>感情を押し殺すのは得意だったのに、<line-break>隠せないなんて。<end>はあ…<line-break>でも、私なんて…。<line-break> <line-break>いえ…なんでも…ございません。<line-break>だいそれた望みが胸に生まれて<line-break>苦しいだけです。<end><value:$28>様<line-break>あの…<line-break> <line-break>…なんでもございません。<line-break>こういう気持ちははじめてなので<line-break>どう処理してよいのか、とまどいます。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3What? Do I... look happy? This is troubling. I used to be so good at suppressing my feelings, yet now I cannot even hide them.<end>Ah... But someone like me... No... it is nothing. It is only that a presumptuous hope has arisen in me, and it pains me.<end><value:$28>, I... No, it is nothing. I have never felt this way before, and I am at a loss as to what to do with it.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3네? 제 얼굴이… 기뻐 보이나요…. …곤란하군요. 감정을 억누르는 건 자신 있었는데 숨길 수 없다니.<end>하아… 하지만 저 같은 사람이…. 아뇨… 아무것도… 아닙니다. 분에 넘치는 바람이 마음속에 생겨서 괴로울 뿐입니다.<end><value:$28> 님, 저기…. …아무것도 아닙니다. 이런 마음은 처음이라 어떻게 해야 할지 당황스럽습니다.<end>"

## 1350574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に<value:$28>様には<line-break>何とお礼を言ってよいやら。<line-break> <line-break>あなた様が来てくださることが<line-break>どれだけ私たち姉妹の<line-break>救いになっていることか…。<end>"
EN: "I truly do not know how to thank you, <value:$28>. Your coming here means more to my sister and me than I can say...<end>"
KO: "정말 <value:$28> 님께 뭐라 감사드려야 할지 모르겠습니다. 당신이 와 주시는 게 저희 자매에게 얼마나 큰 구원이 되는지…<end>"

## 1350575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3<value:$28>様、<line-break>私より，ヴィアに話しかけて<line-break>あげてくださいませんでしょうか。<line-break>ヴィアはお兄ちゃんっ子で<line-break>いつも夜中になると泣いているのです。<line-break>私は姉ですからだいじょうぶですが…<end>元気づけてくださって<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>お心遣い、うれしい…です。<end>兄のことを心配してくださるなんて、<line-break><value:$28>様は<line-break>おやさしい方なのですね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3<value:$28>, would you please talk to Via instead of me? She is very attached to our brother, and she cries every night. I am the elder sister, so I will be all right...<end>Thank you for trying to cheer me up. Your thoughtfulness means a great deal... to me.<end>It is very kind of you to worry about our brother, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3<value:$28>님, 저보다 비아에게 말을 걸어 주시지 않겠습니까. 비아는 오빠를 무척 따르는 아이라 밤마다 울고 있습니다. 저는 언니니까 괜찮지만…<end>기운을 북돋아 주셔서 감사합니다. 배려해 주셔서… 기쁩니다.<end>오빠를 걱정해 주시다니, <value:$28>님은 참 다정한 분이시군요.<end>"

## 1350576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3あんな兄ですが<line-break>私たち姉妹にとっては<line-break>かけがえのない存在なのです。<end>心配です。<line-break>兄は私たちを助けるため、<line-break>重い呪いを受け、廃人同様なのです。<end><value:$28>様、<line-break>兄のためにご迷惑をおかけして<line-break>申しわけございません。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Whatever else he may be, our brother is irreplaceable to us.<end>I am worried. Our brother suffered a terrible curse to save us, and it has left him scarcely more than a living shell.<end><value:$28>, I am sorry our brother has caused you so much trouble.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3그런 오빠지만 우리 자매에게는 둘도 없는 소중한 존재입니다.<end>걱정됩니다. 오빠는 우리를 구하기 위해 무거운 저주를 받아 폐인이나 다름없는 상태입니다.<end><value:$28>님, 오빠 일로 폐를 끼쳐 드려 죄송합니다.<end>"

## 1350577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3…私さ、<line-break><value:$28>に会えるのが<line-break>すごくうれしいんだよね。<line-break>大好きなお兄ちゃんがいなくなって<line-break>それどころじゃないはずなのに…。<line-break>自分で自分にへこむよ。<end>…今まで、お兄ちゃんより<line-break>大事な人が現れるなんて<line-break>考えたこともなかったんだ。<line-break>今は<line-break>考えちゃいけない<line-break>…って思ってる。<end>お兄ちゃんにしろ<line-break><value:$28>にしろ<line-break>気楽だよね、男って。<line-break>女がどんな気分でいるのかなんて<line-break>おかまいなしなんだよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3You know... seeing <value:$28> makes me so happy. My beloved brother is missing, so I shouldn't even be thinking about that... I feel awful about myself for it.<end>Until now, I never imagined anyone could become more important to me than my brother. Right now, though, I think I shouldn't be thinking about that...<end>Whether it's my brother or <value:$28>, men have it easy. They don't care how a woman might feel.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3…나 있잖아, <value:$28>를 만나는 게 정말 기뻐. 좋아하는 오빠가 없어져서 이럴 때가 아닌데…. 나 자신한테 실망하게 돼.<end>…지금까지 오빠보다 소중한 사람이 나타날 거라고는 생각해 본 적도 없어. 지금은 그런 생각을 하면 안 돼…라고 생각하고 있어.<end>오빠도 그렇고 <value:$28>도 그렇고 남자들은 참 속 편하지. 여자가 어떤 기분인지 전혀 신경 쓰지 않잖아.<end>"

## 1350578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>ホント救われてるわ、私たち。<line-break>なんか、家族みたい。<line-break>同い年くらいのはずなのに<line-break>なんだろう、姉さんみたいな存在だよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. You've really been a lifeline for us. It almost feels like we're family. We must be about the same age, but somehow, you're like an older sister to me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 고마워. 정말 큰 도움을 받고 있어, 우리. 뭔가 가족 같아. 비슷한 나이일 텐데도뭐랄까, 언니 같은 존재야.<end>"

## 1350579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3私より、ヴァイに話しかけてあげてよ。<line-break>あの子、本心を隠して<line-break>いつも自分だけガマンしちゃうんだ。<line-break>だけど、ホントにつらいのは<line-break>私みたいにすぐ泣いちゃう子じゃなくて<line-break>きっとヴァイみたいな子だから…。<end>兄さんは、お兄ちゃんって<line-break>呼ばれるのが嫌いでさ、<line-break>そう呼ぶといつも怒ってた。<line-break>ゴメン。つまんない思い出話して<line-break>だけど不思議。<value:$28>には<line-break>こんな話が自然にできちゃうんだ。<end>お兄ちゃんは私たちを助けるため<line-break>重い呪いを受けて身体がボロボロなんだ。<line-break>ものすごく心配だよ。<line-break>ゴメンね、こんな話して<line-break>でも、こんな話できるの<line-break><value:$28>だけなんだ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Go talk to Vai instead of me. She hides how she really feels and keeps everything bottled up. I cry at the drop of a hat, but I don't think people like me are the ones who suffer most. It's people like Vai...<end>My brother hated being called “big brother.” He'd always get angry when I called him that. Sorry. That's a boring old story. Still, it's strange. Talking to <value:$28> about things like this just comes naturally.<end>My brother took on a terrible curse to save us, and it wrecked his body. I'm so worried about him. Sorry for talking about all this, but <value:$28> is the only one I can tell.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3나보다 바이한테 말 좀 걸어 줘. 그 애는 속마음을 숨기고 늘 혼자 참아 버리거든. 하지만 정말 괴로운 건 나처럼 금방 울어 버리는 애가 아니라 바이 같은 애일 테니까….<end>오빠는 '오빠'라고 불리는 걸 싫어해서 그렇게 부르면 늘 화냈어. 미안, 재미없는 추억 얘기나 해서. 그런데 신기해. <value:$28>에게는 이런 얘기도 자연스럽게 하게 되네.<end>오빠는 우리를 구하려고 무거운 저주를 받아 몸이 엉망이야. 정말 걱정돼. 미안해, 이런 얘기 해서. 하지만 이런 얘기를 할 수 있는 건 <value:$28>뿐이야.<end>"

## 1350581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あ、お兄ちゃん、<line-break>戻ってきてくれたんだ！<end>あ、<value:$28>、<line-break>戻ってきてくれたんだ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Oh, big brother! You came back!<end>Oh, <value:$28>! You came back!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0아, 오빠, 돌아와 줬구나!<end>아, <value:$28>, 돌아와 줬구나!<end>"

## 1350582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、なんか邪険にするし<line-break>顔も見せてくれないしさ、<line-break>心配したんだよ。<end>"
EN: "You've been so cold lately, and you haven't even come around. I was worried.<end>"
KO: "요즘 왠지 차갑게 대하고 얼굴도 보여 주지 않아서 걱정했잖아.<end>"

## 1350583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントのスラムで<line-break>別に私たちなんかいらないと言って<line-break>姿を消したあと…<line-break>ヴィアは毎日、夜が来ると<line-break>泣いていたんですよ。<line-break>察してあげてください。<end>"
EN: "After you disappeared in the Slums of Ancient, saying you had no need for the likes of us... Via cried every night. Please try to understand.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 빈민가에서 우리 따윈 필요 없다고 말하고 사라진 뒤… 비아는 매일 밤이 되면 울었어요. 알아주세요.<end>"

## 1350584
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、いいよ<line-break>だって、これからはまた、<line-break>いっしょに旅ができるんだからさ。<end>"
EN: "That's enough. We can travel together again now.<end>"
KO: "이제 됐어. 어차피 이제부터는 다시, 같이 여행할 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 1350585
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご用がおありのときは<line-break>猫屋敷…で呼び出す、ですか？<line-break>ええと、猫屋敷というと…？<end>"
EN: "If you need anything, you'll summon us... at Cat Manor, is that it? Um... what is Cat Manor?<end>"
KO: "용건이 있을 때는 고양이 저택…에서 불러낸다고요? 저기, 고양이 저택이라면…?<end>"

## 1350586
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ、忘れたの？<line-break>ずっと、そうだったでしょ？<line-break>ヘンだよ。<end>"
EN: "Vai, did you forget? That's how it's always been, remember? That's strange.<end>"
KO: "바이, 잊었어? 계속 그랬잖아? 이상해.<end>"

## 1350587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうでした…ね。<line-break>そう、ずっとそうだった。<line-break>…気がします。ヘンですね、私。<end>"
EN: "Yes... that's right. Yes, that's how it always was... I think. I'm strange, aren't I?<end>"
KO: "…그랬…지요. 그래, 계속 그랬던… 것 같아요. 이상하네요, 나.<end>"

## 1350588
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0じゃ、お兄ちゃん、<line-break>私、呼び出してくれるの待ってるから。<end>じゃあね、<value:$28>、<line-break>私、呼び出してくれるの待ってるから。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0All right, big brother. I'll be waiting for you to summon me.<end>See you, <value:$28>. I'll be waiting for you to summon me.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그럼 오빠, 난 불러 주길 기다리고 있을게.<end>그럼 안녕, <value:$28>, 난 불러 주길 기다리고 있을게.<end>"

## 1350589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜだ？　なぜ…俺を救った？<end>"
EN: "Why? Why did you save me?<end>"
KO: "왜지? 왜… 나를 구했지?<end>"

## 1350590
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人が人を救うことに理由がいるのかい？<line-break>なぁ？<line-break><value:$28>。 <end>"
EN: "Does a person need a reason to save someone? Isn't that right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "사람이 사람을 구하는 데 이유가 필요한가? 그렇지? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1350591
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺の母はダルケニスだった。<line-break>そして、俺が幼いうちに母は<line-break>行方知れずになった。<line-break>義母はダルケニスの俺に<line-break>よくしてくれ、俺を当主にまでした。<line-break>実の子のエストを差し置いてな。<end>"
EN: "My mother was Darkenith. She disappeared when I was still young. My stepmother treated me well despite my being Darkenith, and even made me head of the family instead of her own son, Est.<end>"
KO: "…내 어머니는 다르케니스였다. 그리고 내가 어릴 때 행방불명이 됐지. 새어머니는 다르케니스인 나에게 잘해 주었고 나를 당주 자리까지 올려 줬어. 친아들 에스트를 제쳐 두고 말이야.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1350594
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1350595
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界を消せば同じことだよね。<line-break>ティアナ。<end>"
EN: "It'll make no difference once the world is gone, will it, Tiana?<end>"
KO: "세계를 없애면 똑같은 거잖아. 티아나.<end>"

## 1350596
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。その前に…。<line-break>レムオン様、<line-break>あなたにお礼をしないと。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. But first...Lord Lemghon, I must thank you.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 그 전에… 레무온 님, 당신께 감사부터 해야겠군요.<end>"

## 1350597
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当然よね。<line-break>私って「王女」でなければ<line-break>空っぽだったんですもの。<line-break>でもね、ゼネテス、<line-break>あなたはもう私を拒絶できないわ。<line-break>私を傷つけることもできやしない。<end>"
EN: "Of course. Without being a “princess,” I was nothing. But you see, Xenetes, you can no longer reject me. You can't hurt me, either.<end>"
KO: "당연하겠지. 난 '왕녀'가 아니면 텅 비어 있었으니까. 하지만 제네테스, 이제 당신은 날 거부할 수 없어. 나를 상처 입히지도 못해.<end>"

## 1350598
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様は<line-break>激務で心身ともにすり減らしている。<line-break>若い娘だ、恋の１つでもしていたかろうに。<end>"
EN: "The demands of her position have worn Lady Khryuseis down in body and spirit. She's still a young woman. She must have wished for the chance to fall in love.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님은 격무로 몸과 마음이 닳아가고 있어. 젊은 아가씨인데 연애 한 번쯤은 해 보고 싶었겠지.<end>"

## 1350599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らにつらくあたるときもあるが仕方ない。<line-break>この解放軍を、リベルダム陥落後、<line-break>１人で支えてきたようなものだからな。<end>"
EN: "She may be harsh with us at times, but who can blame her? Ever since Liberdam fell, she's practically held the Liberation Army together single-handedly.<end>"
KO: "우리에게 매섭게 대할 때도 있지만 어쩔 수 없지. 리벨덤 함락 뒤 이 해방군을 사실상 혼자 떠받쳐 왔으니까.<end>"

## 1350600
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec135-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク、ボリューム４終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질라크 볼륨 4 끝<end>"

## 1360000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク、ボリューム６開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질라크 볼륨 6 시작<end>"

## 1360001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜エルアザル、何百年も前に<line-break>世界を滅亡の危機に追いやったけど<line-break>賢王アルキュオネに倒された…。<end>"
EN: "The Evil Dragon Elazar drove the world to the brink of destruction centuries ago, only to be defeated by Wise King Alcyone...<end>"
KO: "사룡 엘아자르. 수백 년 전 세계를 멸망 직전까지 몰아넣었지만 현왕 알큐오네에게 쓰러졌어….<end>"

## 1360002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知ってる？<line-break>ホントにこいつを倒したのは別の人間。<line-break>忘れられたかわいそうな剣士。<line-break>彼は強大な力を与える代わりに<line-break>世界から存在を忘却の彼方に消し去る<line-break>闇の神器、虚無の剣を抜いたのさ。<end>"
EN: "Did you know? It was actually defeated by someone else―a poor swordsman who was forgotten. He drew the Sword of the Void, a Dark Artifact that grants immense power in exchange for erasing its wielder's very existence from the world.<end>"
KO: "알아? 사실 이 녀석을 쓰러뜨린 건 다른 사람이야. 잊힌 불쌍한 검사. 그는 강대한 힘을 주는 대신 세계에서 존재 자체를 망각 속으로 지워 버리는 어둠의 신기, 허무의 검을 뽑았지.<end>"

## 1360003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れられた剣士ミラクの<line-break>失われた伝承など知っている俺を、<line-break>誰だと思っている？<end>"
EN: "Who do you think I am? I even know the lost legend of Mirak, the Forgotten Swordsman.<end>"
KO: "잊힌 검사 미라크의 사라진 전승까지 아는 나를 누구라고 생각하는 거지?<end>"

## 1360004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もとは施文院最強の告死天使。<line-break>今は堕ちて、自分のためだけに<line-break>どんな汚い仕事もこなすケチな悪党…<end>"
EN: "Once the mightiest of the Angels of Death in the Inscription Order. Now fallen, a petty villain who'll do any dirty job for personal gain...<end>"
KO: "원래 시문원 최강의 고사천사. 지금은 타락해서 자기만을 위해 어떤 더러운 일도 해내는 보잘것없는 악당….<end>"

## 1360005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな君がなんでここに来るの？<line-break>ご親切にも、うかつな冒険者に<line-break>この屍竜に近づくなと警告するため？<end>"
EN: "So what brings you here? Did you kindly come to warn some careless adventurer not to go near this Corpse Dragon?<end>"
KO: "그런 네가 왜 여기 왔지? 친절하게도 덜렁대는 모험가들에게 이 시룡에게 다가가지 말라고 경고하러?<end>"

## 1360006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここには黄泉の乙女像がある。<line-break>ガッポリ賞金のかかったデカイ山だ。<line-break>ケチな小悪党にはぴったりだろう？<end>"
EN: "The Statue of the Underworld Maiden is here. It's a big score with a hefty reward attached―perfect for a petty little crook, wouldn't you say?<end>"
KO: "여기엔 황천의 소녀상이 있어. 상금이 두둑하게 걸린 큰 건수지. 쩨쩨한 소악당한테 딱 어울리지?<end>"

## 1360007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。エルファスの呪いの具合はどう？<line-break>ホントはもう歩くのすら困難なはずさ。<line-break>そんな身体で屍竜に挑む気だったの？<end>"
EN: "Heh. How is Elfas's curse treating you? You should barely be able to walk by now. Were you really going to take on the Corpse Dragon in that condition?<end>"
KO: "후후. 엘파스의 저주 상태는 어때? 사실은 이제 걷는 것조차 힘들 텐데. 그런 몸으로 시룡에게 도전할 생각이었어?<end>"

## 1360008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うだろう？<line-break>思い出すため、だろう？<end>"
EN: "No, that's not it. You came here to remember, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "아니잖아? 떠올리기 위해서잖아?<end>"

## 1360009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "思い出そうとしていることすら<line-break>思い出せないだろうけど<line-break>僕は知っているよ。<end>"
EN: "You probably can't even remember that you're trying to remember. But I know.<end>"
KO: "떠올리려 했다는 사실조차 기억하지 못하겠지만 나는 알고 있어.<end>"

## 1360010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは忘れてしまった君の願い。<end>"
EN: "It's the wish you've forgotten.<end>"
KO: "그건 네가 잊어버린 소원이다.<end>"

## 1360011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや、またもやおジャマ虫の登場だ。<line-break>霊峰トールのノトゥーン神殿においで。<line-break>そこで話の続きをしよう？　くすくす。<end>"
EN: "Oh, my. Another pest has come buzzing in. Come to Notun Temple on Sacred Mount Thor. Let's continue our little talk there, shall we? Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 또 훼방꾼이 등장했네. 영봉 톨의 노툰 신전에 와. 거기서 이야기의 계속을 하자고? 후후.<end>"

## 1360012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす。<line-break>君の願いはなんなのか？<line-break>それを探りに来てくれたんだね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. What is it you wish for? You've come to find out, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "후후. 네 소원이 무엇인지, 그걸 알아보러 와 준 거구나.<end>"

## 1360013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあまず質問。<line-break>そしてこれは君の願いを知るヒントだよ。<line-break>なんで君はゼネテスが嫌いなの？<end>"
EN: "Then let's start with a question. It'll give you a clue to what you truly wish for. Why do you hate Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "그럼 먼저 질문. 그리고 이건 네 소원을 알기 위한 힌트야. 왜 넌 제네테스를 싫어해?<end>"

## 1360014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "虫が好かない。<line-break>それだけだ。<line-break>理由などあるものか。<end>"
EN: "He rubs me the wrong way. That's all. Why should there be a reason?<end>"
KO: "마음에 안 들어. 그뿐이야. 이유 따위 있을 리 없잖아.<end>"

## 1360015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。理由はあるよ。<line-break>思い出せないだけ。<line-break>じゃあ、次。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. There is a reason. You just can't remember it. Next question.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 이유는 있어. 기억이 안 날 뿐이지. 그럼 다음.<end>"

## 1360016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで君は生きることに執着するの？<line-break>そんなになってまで<line-break>呪いで身体はズタズタだろう？<end>"
EN: "Why are you so determined to stay alive? Even like this, with the curse tearing your body apart?<end>"
KO: "왜 넌 사는 데 그렇게 집착하지? 그런 꼴이 되면서까지. 저주 때문에 몸은 이미 만신창이잖아?<end>"

## 1360017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死よりもつらい痛みが君を襲ってるのに<line-break>君は平気なふりしてまだ生きようとする。<line-break>まるで君が嫌いなゼネテスみたいだ。<end>"
EN: "You're in pain worse than death, yet you pretend nothing's wrong and keep struggling to live. Just like that Xenetes you hate so much.<end>"
KO: "죽음보다 괴로운 고통이 널 덮치는데도 넌 아무렇지 않은 척하며 아직 살아가려고 해. 꼭 네가 싫어하는 제네테스 같군.<end>"

## 1360018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死ぬのが恐いからさ。<line-break>あいつは関係ない。<end>"
EN: "Because I'm afraid to die. He has nothing to do with it.<end>"
KO: "죽는 게 무서워서야. 그 녀석은 상관없어.<end>"

## 1360019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ！　関係あるさあ！<line-break>なんの感情も持たない告死天使の君を<line-break>ここまでにしてくれた彼の影響だよ。<end>"
EN: "Ohoho, ahahaha! He has everything to do with it! You're one of the Angels of Death who once felt nothing at all. He's the reason you've come this far.<end>"
KO: "우후후 아하하! 관계있지! 아무 감정도 없던 고사천사인 너를 이렇게 만든 그의 영향이야.<end>"

## 1360020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次！　ドンドンいこう！　ねえ？<line-break>君ははるか昔にエルアザルを倒した<line-break>忘れられた剣士に誰の姿を重ねてるの？<end>"
EN: "Next! Let's keep them coming! Now, whose face do you see in the Forgotten Swordsman who defeated Elazar so long ago?<end>"
KO: "다음! 계속 가자! 있잖아, 넌 아주 오래전에 엘아자르를 쓰러뜨린 잊힌 검객에게 누구의 모습을 겹쳐 보고 있어?<end>"

## 1360021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ？　バロルを倒したネメアは<line-break>どうして世界を救った勇者と<line-break>賞賛されることを嫌がるの？　ねえ？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, why does Nemea, who defeated Balor, hate being praised as the Hero who saved the world? Hmm?<end>"
KO: "있잖아? 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 네메아는 왜 세계를 구한 용사라고 칭송받는 걸 싫어하는 거야? 응?<end>"

## 1360022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはネメアめは勇者ではないからだ。<line-break>奴も忘れてはいるが<line-break>真に世界を救った者は別にいる。<end>"
EN: "Because that wretch Nemea is no Hero. He may have forgotten it himself, but someone else truly saved the world.<end>"
KO: "그건 네메아가 용사가 아니기 때문이다. 녀석도 잊고 있지만 진정으로 세계를 구한 자는 따로 있다.<end>"

## 1360023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界を滅ぼそうとする魔王バロルと<line-break>ネメアの決戦のとき、別の場所で<line-break>世界を救うための戦いがあった。<end>"
EN: "While Nemea fought his final battle against Balor, Demon King, who sought to destroy the world, another battle to save it was being fought elsewhere.<end>"
KO: "세계를 멸망시키려는 마왕 발로르와 네메아의 결전이 벌어질 때, 다른 곳에서는 세계를 구하기 위한 싸움이 있었다.<end>"

## 1360024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス、貴様、そして<line-break>忘れられた大いなるソウルの持ち主は、<line-break> <line-break>姉さんが施した封印を<line-break>破ろうとしていた。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes, you, and the forgotten bearer of the great Soul were trying to break the seal my sister had placed.<end>"
KO: "제네테스, 네놈, 그리고 잊힌 위대한 소울의 주인은 누님이 건 봉인을 깨뜨리려 하고 있었다.<end>"

## 1360025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その封印こそ、バロルに不死の力を与え<line-break>バロルの精神を操って世界を消そうと<line-break>しむけていた元凶だったからね。<end>"
EN: "Because that very seal was the root of it all. It gave Balor immortality, controlled Balor's mind, and drove Balor to erase the world.<end>"
KO: "그 봉인이야말로 발로르에게 불사의 힘을 주고 발로르의 정신을 조종해 세계를 없애게 만들던 원흉이었으니까.<end>"

## 1360026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、考えてもごらんよ。<line-break>君らの貧弱な魔力じゃ、とうてい<line-break>イズの封印を解けるはずもない。<line-break>…でも、ひとつだけ手はあったんだ。<line-break>闇の神器の力を借りるって奥の手がね。<end>"
EN: "But think about it. With your feeble magic, you could never have broken Iz's seal. There was one way, though: a last resort. You could borrow the power of a Dark Artifact.<end>"
KO: "하지만 생각해 봐. 너희의 빈약한 마력으로는 도저히 이즈의 봉인을 풀 수 없어. …그래도 한 가지 방법은 있었지. 어둠의 신기의 힘을 빌린다는 비장의 수가 말이야.<end>"

## 1360027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつは言った。…この虚無の剣を<line-break>大いなるソウルを持つ自分が抜けば<line-break>自分の存在は忘却の彼方へ消え去る。<line-break>だけど大いなるソウルと闇の神器の力で<line-break>この封印を断つことができる。<line-break>自分が好きな世界を救えるんだ、と。<end>"
EN: "That's what was said: “If I, the bearer of the great Soul, draw this Sword of the Void, my existence will vanish beyond all memory. But with the power of the great Soul and the Dark Artifact, I can cut through this seal. I can save the world I love.”<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 말했다. …이 허무의 검을 큰 소울을 가진 자신이 뽑으면 자신의 존재는 망각 저편으로 사라진다. 하지만 큰 소울과 어둠의 신기 힘으로 이 봉인을 끊을 수 있다. 자신이 좋아하는 세계를 구할 수 있다고.<end>"

## 1360028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は止めたよね？<line-break>だけど、普段生きることの大切さを<line-break>偉そうに説いてたゼネテスは…。<end>"
EN: "You tried to stop it, didn't you? But Xenetes, who was always so self-righteous about how precious life was...<end>"
KO: "넌 말렸지? 그런데 평소엔 살아가는 것의 소중함을 잘난 듯 떠들던 제네테스는….<end>"

## 1360029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴は止めなかった。<line-break>あいつの意志を…大事にする…と言って<line-break>そんな…偽善…俺は…俺は…！<end>"
EN: "He didn't try to stop it. Said he would... respect that choice... Such... hypocrisy... I... I...!<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 말리지 않았어. 그 사람의 의지를… 소중히 한다면서…. 그런… 위선… 나는… 나는…!<end>"

## 1360030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の痛みはものすごかったんだね。<end>"
EN: "You must have been in terrible pain.<end>"
KO: "네 고통은 정말 엄청났구나.<end>"

## 1360031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の君を作ったふたりのうちのひとりが<line-break>そのときしていた悲しい目に<line-break>気づけないほどつらかったんだね。<end>"
EN: "So much pain that you never noticed the sorrow in the eyes of one of the two people who made you what you are today.<end>"
KO: "지금의 너를 만든 두 사람 중 한 명이 그때 짓고 있던 슬픈 눈빛조차 알아차리지 못할 만큼 괴로웠던 거구나.<end>"

## 1360032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしてツェラシェルは世界が滅ぶとも<line-break>自分の利益のためだけに生きると誓い<line-break>ゼネテスは誰とも組まなくなったとさ。<end>"
EN: "And so Zeraschel swore to live for nothing but personal gain, even if the world perished, while Xenetes never joined forces with anyone again.<end>"
KO: "그리고 체라셸은 세계가 멸망하더라도 자기 이익만을 위해 살겠다고 맹세했고, 제네테스는 누구와도 손잡지 않게 됐다는 이야기지.<end>"

## 1360033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、すべては忘却の彼方…。<line-break>みーんな、その存在を忘れてしまった。<line-break>世界を救うために消滅した者のことを。<line-break>僕だけだよ。この話を覚えていたのは。<end>"
EN: "And then everything passed into oblivion... Everyone forgot that person had ever existed―the one who vanished to save the world. I was the only one who remembered this story.<end>"
KO: "그리고 모든 건 망각의 저편으로… 모두가 그 존재를 잊어버렸다. 세계를 구하기 위해 소멸한 자를. 이 이야기를 기억한 건 나뿐이야.<end>"

## 1360034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君が意識下で死を恐れる理由はこれ。<line-break>自分が負った大事な人を失うという傷を<line-break>大事な妹たちに負わせるのが恐いんだ。<end>"
EN: "That's why, deep down, you're afraid to die. You're afraid your beloved younger sisters will suffer the same wound you did: losing someone precious to them.<end>"
KO: "네가 무의식중에 죽음을 두려워하는 이유는 이거야. 소중한 사람을 잃은 상처를 네 소중한 여동생들에게도 입힐까 두려운 거지.<end>"

## 1360035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、エルファスの呪いにより<line-break>君は死のあぎとに捕らえられつつあるよ。<line-break>どうする？<end>"
EN: "But Elfas's curse is slowly dragging you into the jaws of death. What will you do?<end>"
KO: "하지만 엘파스의 저주 때문에 넌 죽음의 아가리에 붙잡히고 있어. 어떻게 할래?<end>"

## 1360036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の願いをかなえる答えはひとつだけ。<line-break>ヒントは、闇の神器、虚無の剣。<line-break>僕は知ってる、あれは確実だ。<end>"
EN: "There's only one answer that can grant your wish. Here's a hint: the Dark Artifact, the Sword of the Void. I know it will work. Without fail.<end>"
KO: "네 소원을 이루는 답은 하나뿐이야. 힌트는 어둠의 신기, 허무의 검. 나는 알고 있어. 그건 확실해.<end>"

## 1360037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "使用者のソウルが大きすぎた失敗例が<line-break>たった２件だけあるけど、<line-break>君程度の小悪党ならだいじょうぶ。<end>"
EN: "There have only been two failures, both because the user's Soul was too great. A petty villain like you will be fine.<end>"
KO: "사용자의 소울이 너무 커서 실패한 사례가 딱 두 건뿐인데, 너 정도의 소악당이면 괜찮아.<end>"

## 1360038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、君の希望はすぐそこだよ？<line-break>思い出しているんだろう？<line-break>おいで、安らかな絶望のほうへ。<end>"
EN: "There now. Your hope is right before you. You remember, don't you? Come. Embrace peaceful despair.<end>"
KO: "자, 네 희망은 바로 앞이야. 기억하고 있지? 이리 와, 편안한 절망으로.<end>"

## 1360039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わー、<value:$28>だ。<line-break>なんでも暴力で解決しようって姿勢<line-break>よくないと思うよ？<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's <value:$28>. You really shouldn't try to solve everything with violence, you know.<end>"
KO: "와, <value:$28>이다. 뭐든 폭력으로 해결하려는 태도는 좋지 않다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1360040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人はね、ココロがあって生きてるんだ。<line-break>そのココロの底の願いがかなうってのに<line-break>ジャマばっかしてさ。おしおきだよ！<end>"
EN: "People live because they have hearts, you know. The wish at the bottom of that heart is about to come true, and all you do is get in the way. Time for your punishment!<end>"
KO: "사람은 말이야, 마음이 있어서 살아가는 거야. 그 마음 깊은 곳의 소원이 이루어진다는데 자꾸 방해만 하고. 벌 받아야지!<end>"

## 1360041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴なら、行ったよ。<end>"
EN: "The one you mean already left.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석이라면 갔어.<end>"

## 1360042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど…闇の神器を使って<line-break>逆に封印をほどこしておったか。<end>"
EN: "I see... You used a Dark Artifact to place a seal on it instead.<end>"
KO: "그렇군… 어둠의 신기를 사용해 반대로 봉인을 걸어 둔 건가.<end>"

## 1360043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "開け放つものである私の力が満ちても<line-break>ウルグ様復活の門が開かないのも道理だ。<line-break>しかし…いつの間に？<end>"
EN: "No wonder the gate to Lord Ulugh's resurrection remained closed, even with my power to open it at its height. But... when did you manage it?<end>"
KO: "열어젖히는 자인 내 힘이 가득 찼는데도 울그 님 부활의 문이 열리지 않는 것도 당연하군. 하지만… 언제부터?<end>"

## 1360044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは認識しえまい。<line-break>私もこの世界の存在だ。<line-break>この虚無の剣の魔力からは逃れられぬ。<end>"
EN: "You could not have perceived it. I, too, am a being of this world. I cannot escape the power of the Sword of the Void.<end>"
KO: "그것은 인식할 수 없을 것이다. 나도 이 세계의 존재다. 이 허무의 검의 마력에서는 벗어날 수 없어.<end>"

## 1360045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まあよい、闇の門を開け放ち<line-break>幾度となく繰り返されてきた茶番、<line-break>光と闇の戦いの幕をまた開けよう。<end>"
EN: "No matter. I shall throw open the Dark Gate and raise the curtain once more on that farce repeated through the ages: the battle between Light and Darkness.<end>"
KO: "…좋다. 어둠의 문을 활짝 열고 수없이 반복되어 온 희극, 빛과 어둠의 싸움의 막을 다시 올리자.<end>"

## 1360046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうはさせん。<line-break>これはあいつがいたという証だ。<end>"
EN: "I won't let you. This is proof of that person's existence.<end>"
KO: "그렇게는 못 해. 이건 그 녀석이 있었다는 증거다.<end>"

## 1360047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院の秘術か。しかもかなり高度だ。<line-break>これを使える者がこの時代にも<line-break>まだいたとはな。<line-break>いや、きれいに一撃をもらったよ。<line-break>本来なら魔人の生という永遠の怠惰から<line-break>私は解放されていたことだろう。<end>"
EN: "A secret art of the Inscription Order? And a highly advanced one at that. I never imagined anyone in this age could still wield it. I must admit, that was a clean hit. Ordinarily, it would have freed me from the eternal indolence of a demon's existence.<end>"
KO: "시문원의 비술인가. 게다가 상당히 고도의 술법이군. 이걸 쓸 수 있는 자가 이 시대에도 아직 있었다니. 아니, 제대로 한 방 먹었어. 원래라면 마인으로서의 삶이라는 영원한 나태에서 나는 해방되었겠지.<end>"

## 1360048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかしどうしたことだ。<line-break>その肉体の衰えは<line-break>まるで死体だ。技の威力も皆無だな。<end>"
EN: "But what has happened to you? That body has withered to the state of a corpse, and your technique has lost all its power.<end>"
KO: "그런데 이게 어찌 된 일이지. 그 육체의 쇠약함은 마치 시체 같군. 기술의 위력도 전혀 없어.<end>"

## 1360049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこらのザコならいざ知らず<line-break>破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士筆頭<line-break>ヴァシュタールを倒すには不足だ。<end>"
EN: "That might suffice against common rabble, but not against Vashtar, foremost among the Knights of the Round Table serving Ulugh, God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "잡졸이라면 몰라도 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 수장 바슈타르를 쓰러뜨리기엔 부족하다.<end>"

## 1360050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん！<end>"
EN: "Brother!<end>"
KO: "형!<end>"

## 1360051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザコがもう２匹。<end>"
EN: "Two more weaklings.<end>"
KO: "잡졸이 두 마리 더.<end>"

## 1360052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐっ！<line-break>動けない！<end>"
EN: "Ghh! I can't move!<end>"
KO: "큭! 움직일 수 없어!<end>"

## 1360053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほほう、貴様も消えたがっているのか。<line-break>この２匹の女どもに、心の痛みを<line-break>与えぬため、という理由はくだらんが。<end>"
EN: "Oh? So you wish to disappear as well. Though wanting to spare these two women the pain is a pitiful reason.<end>"
KO: "호오, 네놈도 사라지고 싶은 모양이군. 이 두 여자에게 마음의 고통을 주지 않기 위해서라는 이유는 시시하지만.<end>"

## 1360054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなら虚無の剣を使うまでもないな。<line-break>私が貴様の前にこの女どもを始末しよう。<line-break>そうすれば痛むべき心もなくなる。<end>"
EN: "Then there is no need for the Sword of the Void. I shall dispose of these women before your eyes. Then there will be no heart left to ache.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 허무의 검을 쓸 필요도 없겠군. 내가 네 앞에서 이 여자들을 처리하지. 그러면 아플 마음도 사라질 테니.<end>"

## 1360055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ！！<end>"
EN: "Stop!!<end>"
KO: "그만둬!!<end>"

## 1360056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>お前まで、どうしてここに！<line-break>危険だ！　逃げろ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Why are you here too? It's dangerous! Run!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 너까지 왜 여기에! 위험해! 도망쳐!<end>"

## 1360057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ。またザコかと思えば<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主ではないか。<line-break>そうか、今回の茶番の主役は貴様か。<line-break>あるいは、貴様なら<line-break>この永遠に繰り返される輪廻に<line-break>終止符を打ってくれるのかな？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I took you for more rabble, but you possess the Infinite Soul. So you are the protagonist of this farce. Perhaps you might even bring this eternally recurring cycle to an end.<end>"
KO: "훗. 또 잡졸인가 했더니 무한의 소울을 가진 자로군. 그런가, 이번 촌극의 주역은 네놈인가. 어쩌면 네놈이라면 영원히 반복되는 이 윤회에 종지부를 찍어 줄 수 있을까?<end>"

## 1360058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあいい<line-break>時間は限りなくある。<line-break>退屈しのぎに試してみてもいいだろう。<line-break>いくぞ、<value:$28>とやら。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I have all the time in the world. You may prove an amusing diversion. Come, <value:$28>, or whatever your name is.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 좋다. 시간은 무한히 있으니 심심풀이로 시험해 봐도 되겠지. 간다, <value:$28>이라 했나.<end>"

## 1360059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の茶番の主人公は<line-break>別の人間に務めてもらうのだな。<line-break>どうせ代役は用意されているのだろう？<end>"
EN: "It seems another human will have to play the lead in this farce. There is a replacement waiting in the wings anyway, is there not?<end>"
KO: "이번 희극의 주인공은 다른 인간에게 맡기게 되는 건가. 어차피 대역은 준비되어 있겠지?<end>"

## 1360060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "茶番、茶番とうるさいんだよ。<line-break>人間の生き様は、かけがえのない<line-break>それぞれに尊いもんなんだ！<end>"
EN: "Enough about your damned farce! Every human life is unique and precious in its own right!<end>"
KO: "촌극, 촌극 좀 그만해! 인간의 삶은 하나뿐인, 저마다 소중한 거라고!<end>"

## 1360061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その気…<line-break>貴様、さっきの戦いの間に<line-break>虚無の剣を盗みおったか？<end>"
EN: "That aura... Did you steal the Sword of the Void during the battle just now?<end>"
KO: "그 기운… 네놈, 아까 싸우는 동안 허무의 검을 훔쳤느냐?<end>"

## 1360062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きるのに飽き飽きしてる？<line-break>消えたい？<line-break>上等だ。消してやるぜ！<end>"
EN: "Sick of living? Want to disappear? Fine. I'll wipe you out!<end>"
KO: "사는 데 질렸다고? 사라지고 싶어? 좋아. 없애 주마!<end>"

## 1360063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめて、お兄ちゃん！<line-break>虚無の剣を抜かないで！<end>"
EN: "No, Brother! Don't draw the Sword of the Void!<end>"
KO: "그만해, 오빠! 허무의 검을 뽑지 마!<end>"

## 1360064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は、俺はもっと生きたかったぜ！<line-break>今は言える！　もっと、もっと<line-break><value:$28>と…いっしょに…！<end>"
EN: "I... I wanted to live longer! I can say it now! I wanted more time―much more time―with <value:$28>...!<end>"
KO: "난, 난 더 살고 싶었어! 지금은 말할 수 있어! 더, 더 <value:$28>과… 함께…!<end>"

## 1360065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…たち…<line-break>どうしてこんなところに…<line-break>いるの…？<end>"
EN: "Why... are we... in a place like this...?<end>"
KO: "우리… 왜 이런 곳에… 있는 거지…?<end>"

## 1360066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>虚無の剣を手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> obtained the Sword of the Void.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 허무의 검을 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1360067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよ、たぶんそれよ。<line-break>ええと…、闇の神器…の…剣。<line-break>その探索でここに来たのよ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, that must be it. Um... the sword... one of the Dark Artifacts. We came here looking for it.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아마 그거야. 음… 어둠의 신기…의… 검. 그걸 찾으러 여기 온 거야.<end>"

## 1360068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、旅を続けよっか。<line-break>お兄ちゃん！<end>"
EN: "Okay, let's keep going, Brother!<end>"
KO: "그럼 여행 계속하자. 오빠!<end>"

## 1360069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。<line-break>なんでお兄ちゃんなんて呼んだんだろ。<line-break><value:$28>は女の子なのにね。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Why did I call you Brother? You're a girl, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아…. 왜 오빠라고 불렀지. <value:$28>은 여자아이인데.<end>"

## 1360070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに<line-break>私たちは天涯孤独、ふたりっきり。<line-break>兄なんていないでしょう、ヴィア。<end>"
EN: "Besides, we have no one in the world but each other. We don't have a brother, Via.<end>"
KO: "게다가 우리는 천애고아, 둘뿐이잖아. 오빠 같은 건 없잖아, 비아.<end>"

## 1360071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよね。<line-break>どうしたんだろ。ヘンだよ。<line-break>さっきから頭がボーっとしてて。<end>"
EN: "You're right. What's wrong with me? This is strange. My head's been all foggy for a while.<end>"
KO: "그러게. 왜 이러지. 이상해. 아까부터 머리가 멍해서.<end>"

## 1360072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうなさったのですか<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Is something wrong, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이신가요, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1360073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私も同じだ。<end>"
EN: "I feel it too.<end>"
KO: "…나도 마찬가지야.<end>"

## 1360074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>私も何か心に穴が開いたような、<line-break>そんな気がします。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I feel as though there's a hole in my heart too.<end>"
KO: "네. 저도 마음에 구멍이 뚫린 듯한 기분이 듭니다.<end>"

## 1360075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "旅にご一緒させていただくときは<line-break>いつも通り猫屋敷で呼び出していただく<line-break>…ということですね。了解です。<end>"
EN: "So whenever you wish me to join you on your journey, you will summon me at Cat Manor as usual. Understood.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하게 될 때는 평소처럼 고양이 저택에서 불러주시면 된다… 그런 말씀이군요. 알겠습니다.<end>"

## 1360076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは<value:$28>様<line-break>失礼させていただきます。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall take my leave, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>님, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1360077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、退屈しのぎにはなった。<line-break>だが、私の存在を消すには<line-break>どうにも力不足のようだ。<end>"
EN: "I see. You did relieve my boredom. But it seems you still lack the power to erase my existence.<end>"
KO: "과연, 심심풀이 정도는 됐군. 하지만 내 존재를 지워 버리기엔 아무래도 힘이 부족한 모양이다.<end>"

## 1360078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、ズルいー！<line-break>またお兄ちゃんとふたりっきりで！<line-break>交代してよ、ヴァイ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, that's not fair! You get to be alone with Big Brother again! Switch with me, Vai!<end>"
KO: "아, 치사해ー! 또 오빠랑 둘만 있고! 교대해, 바이!<end>"

## 1360079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、妹のわがまま、<line-break>まことに申しわけございませんが<line-break>聞いていただけませんでしょうか。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I sincerely apologize for my younger sister's selfish request, but might I ask you to indulge her?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 제 동생의 제멋대로인 부탁이라 송구하지만 들어주시겠습니까.<end>"

## 1360080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>では、失礼いたします<line-break><value:$28>様<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Then, if you'll excuse me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 그럼 실례하겠습니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1360081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お兄ちゃん、なんて呼び方<line-break><value:$28>様に失礼です。<line-break>おやめなさいね、ヴィア。<end>"
EN: "Calling <value:$28> “Big Brother” is disrespectful. Please stop, Via.<end>"
KO: "오빠라고 부르는 건 <value:$28>님께 실례야. 그만하렴, 비아.<end>"

## 1360082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だって、私、<line-break>地上で一番大切な人を<line-break>お兄ちゃんって呼びたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "But I want to call the person I care about most in the whole world “Big Brother.”<end>"
KO: "…왜냐하면 난 지상에서 가장 소중한 사람을 오빠라고 부르고 싶어.<end>"

## 1360083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでだろう？　わからない。<line-break>すごいヘンだよね、<line-break>言われてみると。<end>"
EN: "Why? I don't know. Now that you mention it, it is really strange, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "왜일까? 모르겠어. 듣고 보니 정말 이상하네.<end>"

## 1360084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイと生まれたときからふたりきり。<line-break>家族もなかったし、お兄ちゃんって<line-break>存在にあこがれてたのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Ever since I was born, it's been just Vai and me. We didn't have any family, so maybe I longed for a big brother...<end>"
KO: "바이와 태어날 때부터 둘뿐이었어. 가족도 없었고, 오빠라는 존재를 동경했던 걸까….<end>"

## 1360085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フシギだよ。<line-break><value:$28>をお兄ちゃんて呼ぶと<line-break>胸がギュッとするんだ。<end>"
EN: "It's strange. When I call <value:$28> “Big Brother,” my chest feels all tight.<end>"
KO: "신기해. <value:$28>를 오빠라고 부르면 가슴이 꽉 조이는 느낌이야.<end>"

## 1360086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何これ？<line-break>闇の王女の置きみやげ？<line-break>それとも東方の博士の忘れ物？<end>"
EN: "What is this? A parting gift from the Princess of Darkness? Or something the Scholar from the East left behind?<end>"
KO: "이게 뭐지? 어둠의 공주가 두고 간 선물? 아니면 동방의 박사가 놓고 간 물건?<end>"

## 1360087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Anyway, I'll go in and help, so cover me!<end>"
KO: "아무튼 내가 구하러 들어갈 테니 지원 부탁해!<end>"

## 1360088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ！<end>"

KO: "바이!<end>"

## 1360089

EN: "We did it! Big brother!<end>"

## 1360090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…じゃなくて、兄さん。<end>"

KO: "…아니, 형.<end>"

## 1360091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360092

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お兄ちゃんは<line-break>ヴァイのことだと必死だもんね。<line-break>ヴァイは万事ひかえめでかわいげが…。<end>"

KO: "오빠는 바이 일이라면 필사적이니까. 바이는 뭐든 얌전하고 귀여운 데가….<end>"

## 1360095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わからないの。<end>"
EN: "I don't know.<end>"
KO: "모르겠어.<end>"

## 1360097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔、こんなことがあったような気がして。<line-break>なんか、大事なものを…<line-break>忘れてるような気がして…。<end>"
EN: "I feel as though something like this happened long ago. As though I've forgotten... something important...<end>"
KO: "예전에 이런 일이 있었던 것 같은 기분이 들어. 뭔가 소중한 걸… 잊고 있는 것 같아서….<end>"

## 1360098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントだ…。<line-break>なぜ…、涙が止まらないんだろう…。<end>"
EN: "It's true... Why can't I stop crying...?<end>"
KO: "정말이야…. 왜… 눈물이 멈추지 않는 거지….<end>"

## 1360099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかりするのだ！<line-break>あれを探しに来た子供は<line-break>どっちへ行った。<end>"
EN: "Stay with me! Which way did the child who came looking for it go?<end>"
KO: "정신 차려라! 그것을 찾으러 온 아이는 어느 쪽으로 갔지?<end>"

## 1360100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イ…イズキヤルを操って<line-break>坑道…へ…。<line-break>封士様が追っていかれました…。<end>"
EN: "The child... took control of Izkiyal and went into the mine tunnels... The Sealer went after them...<end>"
KO: "이… 이즈키얄을 조종해서 갱도…로…. 봉사님께서 뒤쫓아 가셨습니다….<end>"

## 1360101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんということだ！<line-break>ここにあれがあることが知れれば<line-break>大国があれを手に入れようと…。<end>"
EN: "This is disastrous! If word gets out that it is here, the great powers will try to seize it...<end>"
KO: "이럴 수가! 여기에 그것이 있다는 사실이 알려지면 대국들이 그것을 손에 넣으려고….<end>"

## 1360102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、放っておいては<line-break>きゃつめが目覚めかねん。<line-break>どうすればよい？<end>"
EN: "No. If we leave this be, that wretch may awaken. What are we to do?<end>"
KO: "아니, 내버려 두면 그놈이 깨어날지도 모른다. 어찌하면 좋겠느냐?<end>"

## 1360103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を貸しましょうか<end>"
EN: "Shall I lend a hand?<end>"
KO: "힘을 빌려드릴까요<end>"

## 1360104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かかわるのは避けよう<end>"
EN: "Let's stay out of this.<end>"
KO: "관여하는 건 피하자<end>"

## 1360105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む？<line-break>よそ者には関係ないこと。<line-break>行かれよ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? This is no concern of an outsider. Be on your way.<end>"
KO: "음? 외지인과는 상관없는 일이다. 가시오.<end>"

## 1360106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、こちらは<line-break><value:$28>様にて<line-break>封士様の知己です。信頼に足る方かと。<end>"
EN: "No. This is <value:$28>, an acquaintance of the Sealer and, I believe, worthy of your trust.<end>"
KO: "아뇨, 이분은 <value:$28> 님으로 봉사님과 아는 사이입니다. 믿을 만한 분일 겁니다.<end>"

## 1360108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は生意気な魔道士が参り<line-break>聖光石の鉱脈があるはずだ<line-break>教えろ、と申したのです。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, an insolent young mage came here and demanded that we tell him where to find the vein of Holy Light Stone he claimed must exist.<end>"
KO: "사실 건방진 마도사가 찾아와 성광석 광맥이 있을 테니 가르쳐 달라고 했습니다.<end>"

## 1360109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おおかた、聖光石の力で<line-break>魔道力を増幅させ<line-break>悪事を働こうという手合いでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Most likely, the mage intends to use the Holy Light Stone to amplify magical power for some wicked purpose.<end>"
KO: "십중팔구 성광석의 힘으로 마도력을 증폭시켜 악행을 벌이려는 부류겠지요.<end>"

## 1360110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々が知らぬと拒否しますと<line-break>イズキヤルを操り、その記憶から<line-break>聖光石の鉱脈を探り出したのです。<line-break>ほんの小僧のくせに使い手で<line-break>神官どもを蹴散らして<line-break>イズキヤルとともに鉱脈へ向かいました。<end>"
EN: "When we denied knowing anything and refused to tell him, the boy took control of Izkiyal and used its memories to locate the vein of Holy Light Stone. Though only a whelp, he was skilled enough to rout the priests before heading for the vein with Izkiyal.<end>"
KO: "우리가 모른다며 거절하자 이즈키얄를 조종해 그 기억에서 성광석 광맥을 찾아낸 겁니다. 어린 녀석 주제에 솜씨가 좋아 신관들을 쓸어버리고 이즈키얄와 함께 광맥으로 향했습니다.<end>"

## 1360112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を貸してくださるのですか！？<line-break>こちらです！<end>"
EN: "You'll help us!? This way!<end>"
KO: "힘을 빌려주시는 겁니까!? 이쪽입니다!<end>"

## 1360113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "立てるか？<end>"
EN: "Can you stand?<end>"
KO: "일어설 수 있어?<end>"

## 1360114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くぞ！<end>"
EN: "Let's go!<end>"
KO: "간다!<end>"

## 1360116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいや！　この聖光石の鉱脈があれば<line-break>白ヤギおじいさんの願いがかなうよ。<line-break>これで同胞種族は救われるってね。<end>"
EN: "Amazing! With this vein of Holy Light Stone, Grandpa White Goat's wish will come true. It'll save his people, he says.<end>"
KO: "굉장해! 이 성광석 광맥만 있으면 흰염소 할아버지의 소원이 이루어질 거야. 이걸로 동족들이 구원받을 수 있겠네.<end>"

## 1360117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、やめるんじゃ！<line-break>この鉱山の奥には……！<end>"
EN: "S-stop! Deep within this mine...!<end>"
KO: "그, 그만두게! 이 광산 깊숙한 곳에는……!<end>"

## 1360118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすくす、眠ってるんだよね？<line-break>邪竜イシュバアルが。<line-break>あの破壊神のしもべが。<line-break>聖光石の強大な力を求め<line-break>採掘を始める愚か者がいたら<line-break>邪竜が目覚め、世界は滅んでしまう。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. It's sleeping down there, isn't it? Evil Dragon Ishbaal, servant of the God of Destruction. If some fool starts mining in pursuit of the Holy Light Stone's immense power, the Evil Dragon will awaken and destroy the world.<end>"
KO: "후후, 잠들어 있는 거지? 사룡 이슈바알이. 저 파괴신의 종이. 성광석의 강대한 힘을 탐내 채굴을 시작하는 어리석은 자가 나타나면 사룡이 깨어나 세계는 멸망해 버려.<end>"

## 1360119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、何百年もウソをついて<line-break>鉱脈があるのを秘密にして、<line-break>このへんぴな町を守ってきたんだよね。<line-break>でも、人にウソをつくなって教える<line-break>神官様が何百年もウソをついてきたのは<line-break>許せないよなあ。どうしよっかなぁ？<end>"
EN: "That's why you've spent centuries lying, keeping the vein secret to protect this remote little town. But a priest who teaches people not to lie has been lying for centuries. That's unforgivable, isn't it? Now, what should I do?<end>"
KO: "그래서 수백 년이나 거짓말을 하면서 광맥이 있다는 걸 숨기고 이 외딴 마을을 지켜 온 거지. 그런데 사람한텐 거짓말하지 말라고 가르치는 신관님들이 수백 년 동안 거짓말해 왔다니 용서가 안 되네. 어떻게 할까아?<end>"

## 1360120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここでイズキヤルに大暴れさせちゃおう。<line-break>そんで邪竜イシュバアルを目覚めさせ<line-break>アルノートゥンに裁きを！　アハハ！<end>"
EN: "I'll make Izkiyal run wild right here. Then Evil Dragon Ishbaal will awaken and pass judgment upon Alnortun! Ah ha ha!<end>"
KO: "여기서 이즈키얄가 마음껏 날뛰게 하자. 그리고 사룡 이슈바알을 깨워 알노툰에 심판을! 아하하!<end>"

## 1360121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　イズキヤルの身体は<line-break>僕が完全に操作してるはずなのに<line-break>まだ抵抗するの、イズキヤル？<end>"
EN: "What? I should have complete control over Izkiyal's body. Are you still resisting, Izkiyal?<end>"
KO: "어라? 이즈키얄의 몸은 내가 완전히 조종하고 있을 텐데 아직도 저항하는 거야, 이즈키얄?<end>"

## 1360122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　イオ…ンズ…　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　オレヲ…タオシテ…クレ…　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Io...ns... Please... defeat me...<end>"
KO: "이오…즈… 나를… 쓰러뜨려… 줘….<end>"

## 1360123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはできん！<end>"
EN: "I cannot do that!<end>"
KO: "그럴 수는 없다!<end>"

## 1360124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　マタ…ヒトトカイブツガ…ニクミ…アウ　　<line-break>　　　　　ソレハ…カナシイ…タエキレ…ナイ<end>"
EN: "Humans and monsters will... hate each other again... It's too sad... I can't bear it...<end>"
KO: "또… 인간과 괴물이… 서로 미워해… 그건… 슬퍼… 견딜 수… 없어.<end>"

## 1360125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　タノム…イオンズ…　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　オレヲ…タオセ…　<line-break>　　　　　　ソシテ…コノコゾウカラ…　<line-break>　　　　　オレヲ…カイホウシテ…クレ…！　<end>"
EN: "Please... Ions... Defeat me... Then... free me from this brat...!<end>"
KO: "부탁한다… 이온즈…. 나를… 쓰러뜨려라…. 그리고… 이 꼬맹이에게서… 나를… 해방시켜… 줘…!<end>"

## 1360126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できん！<line-break>そんなことはできん！！<end>"
EN: "I cannot! I cannot do such a thing!!<end>"
KO: "못 해! 그런 건 못 한다!!<end>"

## 1360127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "倒すもよし、負けて殺されるもよし。<line-break>ただね、選択の時は来たんだ。<line-break> <line-break>僕だって忙しいんだから。<line-break>サッサと決断してよね。<end>"
EN: "Win, or lose and be killed. Either way, the time has come to choose. I'm busy too, you know. Hurry up and decide.<end>"
KO: "쓰러뜨려도 좋고, 져서 죽어도 좋아. 다만 선택할 때는 왔어. 나도 바쁘니까 얼른 결정해.<end>"

## 1360128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勝った事にする<end>"
EN: "Call it a win<end>"
KO: "이긴 걸로 한다<end>"

## 1360129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "됐어<end>"

## 1360130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　コレデ…イイ…　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "This... is how it should be...<end>"
KO: "이걸로… 됐어…<end>"

## 1360131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "偉そうなことを言っておっても<line-break>このザマじゃ。<line-break>結局、イズキヤルを倒してしもうた…。<end>"
EN: "For all my grand words, look at me now. In the end, I struck Izkiyal down...<end>"
KO: "잘난 척 떠들어 봐도 이 꼴이지. 결국 이즈키얄을 쓰러뜨리고 말았어….<end>"

## 1360132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　　これでいいのだ　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　友よ…　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "This is how it should be, my friend...<end>"
KO: "이걸로 됐다, 친구여….<end>"

## 1360133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イズキヤル…か？<end>"
EN: "Izkiyal... is that you?<end>"
KO: "이즈키얄…인가?<end>"

## 1360134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　そうだ、イオンズ　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　俺だ、イズキヤルだ　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Yes, Ions. It's me, Izkiyal.<end>"
KO: "그래, 이온즈. 나다, 이즈키얄이다.<end>"

## 1360135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　俺たち、いにしえの怪物　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　…ティラの子らは、死んで　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　母のもとに帰るのだ　　　　<line-break> <line-break>　　　　　　　俺も母のもとに帰る　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　お前とすごした日々や　　　　<line-break>　　　　　アルノートゥンで過ごした　　　　<line-break>　　　　　やさしい思い出と一緒にな　　　　<end>"
EN: "We ancient monsters... the children of Tira, return to our mother when we die. I, too, will return to her, taking with me the days I spent with you and the gentle memories I made in Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "우리들 고대의 괴물… 티라의 아이들은 죽으면 어머니 곁으로 돌아간다. 나도 어머니 곁으로 돌아간다. 너와 함께 지낸 나날과 알노툰에서 보냈던 따뜻한 추억을 품고 말이야.<end>"

## 1360136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　その思い出は母ティラとひとつになり、　<line-break>　　　　怒り、傷つく母に慰めを与えるだろう　<line-break>　　　　母の心は癒され、生まれくる弟や妹は、　<line-break>　　　　少しだけ、やさしくなっているだろう　　<end>"
EN: "Those memories will become one with Mother Tira and bring comfort to our angry, wounded mother. Her heart will heal, and the younger brothers and sisters yet to be born will be a little kinder.<end>"
KO: "그 추억은 어머니 티라와 하나가 되어 분노하고 상처 입은 어머니를 위로하리라. 어머니의 마음은 치유되고, 태어날 남동생과 여동생들은 조금 더 상냥해져 있으리라.<end>"

## 1360137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　イオンズ…　　　<line-break>　　　　　憎しみは憎しみしか生まない　　　<line-break>　　　　人間と兄弟たちの憎しみの連鎖を　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　断ち切ってくれ　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　俺の妹や…弟たちが　　　<line-break>　　　　　　俺のような…幸せな日々を　　　<line-break>　　　　　過ごせる…時代が…来るように…　　　<end>"
EN: "Ions... Hatred only breeds hatred. Please break the cycle of hatred between humanity and my siblings, so that an age may come when my younger sisters and brothers can know... the same happy days... that I did...<end>"
KO: "이온즈… 증오는 증오밖에 낳지 않아. 인간과 형제들의 증오의 연쇄를 끊어 줘. 내 여동생과… 남동생들이 나처럼… 행복한 나날을 보낼 수 있는… 시대가… 오도록….<end>"

## 1360138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イズキヤル…<end>"
EN: "Izkiyal...<end>"
KO: "이즈키얄…<end>"

## 1360139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　イオンズ…、タノム…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　オレハ…オレヲ…モウ、トメラレナイ<line-break>　　　　オマエノテデ…オワラセテクレ…　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　トモ…ヨ…　<end>"
EN: "Ions... please... I can't... hold myself back any longer. End this... with your own hands... my friend...<end>"
KO: "이온즈… 부탁한다…. 나는… 이제… 나 자신을 멈출 수 없어. 네 손으로… 끝내 줘…. 친구…여….<end>"

## 1360140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オオオオ…ッ！！<end>"

KO: "오오오오…!!<end>"

## 1360141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
