KO: "<value:$28>, 부탁이네. 나도 함께 여행에 데려가 주게.<end>"

## 1360142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…<line-break>ありがとうな。<end>"
EN: "I see... Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나… 고맙다.<end>"

## 1360143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえねえ、そんなに急いでどこ行くの？<line-break>ちょっと僕につきあってよ。<line-break>いいもの見せて上げるからさ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, where are you going in such a hurry? Keep me company for a bit. I've got something good to show you.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 그렇게 급하게 어디 가? 잠깐 나랑 어울려 줘. 좋은 걸 보여 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 1360144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚いてる？<line-break>倒したはずなのに？<line-break>ははっ、僕が甦らせたのさ。<end>"
EN: "Surprised? You thought you'd killed it, didn't you? Ha! I brought it back to life.<end>"
KO: "놀랐어? 분명 쓰러뜨렸는데? 하하, 내가 되살렸지.<end>"

## 1360145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪竜エルアザルだよ。<line-break>死竜の洞窟で眠っていたのを、<line-break>僕が起こしてあげたんだ。<line-break>ここにエサがあるよー、ってね。<line-break>アハハハハ。<end>"
EN: "It's Elazar, the Evil Dragon. It was sleeping in Dead Dragon Cave, so I woke it up. Told it, “There's food over here!” Ahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "사룡 엘아자르야. 사룡의 동굴에서 잠들어 있던 걸, 내가 깨워 줬지. 여기 먹이가 있다고 말이야. 아하하하하.<end>"

## 1360146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、こいつを倒さないと、<line-break>この先には進めないよ。<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Now, if you don't defeat this thing, you can't go any farther. <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "자, 이 녀석을 쓰러뜨리지 않으면 앞으로 갈 수 없어. <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1360148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我はイシュバアル…、すなわち破滅…。<line-break>破壊神により生まれ…、<line-break>この世界に死をもたらす者なり…。<end>"
EN: "I am Ishbaal... I am ruin itself. Born of the God of Destruction, I am the one who shall bring death to this world...<end>"
KO: "나는 이슈바알… 즉 파멸…. 파괴신에게서 태어나… 이 세계에 죽음을 가져오는 자….<end>"

## 1360149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぬか…、我が破壊を望まず、<line-break>立ちはだからんとする者は。<end>"
EN: "So it is you... the one who rejects the destruction I bring and would stand in my way.<end>"
KO: "네놈인가… 나의 파괴를 바라지 않고 가로막으려는 자는.<end>"

## 1360150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、この恐怖の存在を<line-break>目の前にしても、立ち向かう？<end>"
EN: "Even with this terrifying being before you, you're still going to fight?<end>"
KO: "넌 이 공포의 존재를 눈앞에 두고도 맞설 거야?<end>"

## 1360151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、さすがだよ。<line-break>それでこそ、だよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I expected no less. That's more like it.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 역시 대단해. 그래야지.<end>"

## 1360152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、こいつを倒さないと、<line-break>この先には進めないよ。<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Now, if you don't defeat this thing, you can't go any farther. <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "자, 이 녀석을 쓰러뜨리지 않으면 앞으로 갈 수 없어. <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1360154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の世界で、私は知った。<line-break>光も闇も、何もかもを無に帰する虚無。<line-break>その存在を。<line-break>虚無の子シャリよ。<line-break>お前は何を望んでいる？<line-break>すべてを無に帰することか？<end>"
EN: "In the world of darkness, I learned of the Void: that which returns light, darkness, and all else to nothing. Shalli, Child of the Void, what is it you desire? To return everything to nothing?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 세계에서 나는 알았다. 빛도 어둠도 모든 것을 무로 돌리는 허무. 그 존재를. 허무의 아이 샤리여. 너는 무엇을 원하는가? 모든 것을 무로 돌리는 것인가?<end>"

## 1360155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うよ、ネメア。<line-break>僕は虚無から生まれたけど、<line-break>僕に命を吹き込んだのは、別のものさ。<end>"
EN: "No, Nemea. I was born from the Void, but something else breathed life into me.<end>"
KO: "아니야, 네메아. 나는 허무에서 태어났지만 내게 생명을 불어넣은 건 다른 것이야.<end>"

## 1360156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、その存在自体が、<line-break>謎めいていた。もしかしたら、<line-break>敵ではないのかもしれぬ。<line-break>だが、お前の思い通りにはさせん。<line-break>この大地は、ここに生まれ、<line-break>ここで育ち、死にゆく、我らのものだ。<end>"
EN: "Your very existence was shrouded in mystery. I thought perhaps you might not be our enemy. But I will not let you have your way. This land belongs to us, who are born here, grow here, and die here.<end>"
KO: "너는 존재 자체가 수수께끼였다. 어쩌면 적이 아닐지도 모르지. 하지만 네 뜻대로 하게 둘 수는 없다. 이 대지는 여기서 태어나 자라고 죽어 가는 우리들의 것이다.<end>"

## 1360157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、ネメア。<line-break>もう思い通りになってるんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>僕は、あと少しで、<line-break>この時代での役割を終える。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh, Nemea. I've already had my way. My role in this age will soon be over.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 네메아. 이미 내 뜻대로 되고 있어. 나는 이제 조금만 있으면 이 시대에서의 역할을 끝낸다.<end>"

## 1360158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね。<line-break>またあとで会おうね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Bye for now. I'll see you again later, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕. 나중에 또 만나자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1360159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ケリュネイア。<line-break>どこまでついて来るつもりだ？<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia... How far do you intend to follow me?<end>"
KO: "…케류네이아. 대체 어디까지 따라올 생각이지?<end>"

## 1360160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気付いていたの…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>行き着くところまでよ。<line-break>たとえ、そこが地獄でも、<line-break>どこまでもついていくわ。<end>"
EN: "You knew I was here... I'm going with you to the very end. Even if the road leads to hell, I'll follow you all the way.<end>"
KO: "알고 있었어…. 갈 데까지 가는 거야. 설령 그곳이 지옥이라도 어디까지든 따라갈게.<end>"

## 1360161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア。<line-break>お前はここに残れ。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia. Stay here.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아. 너는 여기 남아라.<end>"

## 1360162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤよ！<line-break>ついていく！<line-break>　<line-break>足手まといにはならないわ！<line-break>私だって魔王を倒した勇者のひとり。<line-break>閃光のケリュネイアよ！<end>"
EN: "No! I'm coming with you! I won't hold you back! I'm one of the Heroes who defeated the Demon King. I am Ceryneia the Flash!<end>"
KO: "싫어! 따라갈 거야! 짐은 안 될 거야! 나도 마왕을 쓰러뜨린 용사 중 하나, 섬광의 케류네이아라고!<end>"

## 1360163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからこそ、だ。<line-break>お前にはここで、次々わいてくる<line-break>ザコどもをくいとめてもらう。<line-break>そして、<value:$28>の<line-break>仲間たちを呼び出してもらう。<line-break>これができるのはお前だけだ。<end>"
EN: "That's exactly why. Stay here and hold off the small fry that keep pouring in. Then summon <value:$28>'s companions. You're the only one who can do it.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 네가 필요한 거다. 여기서 계속 몰려드는 졸개들을 막아 줘. 그리고 <value:$28>의 동료들을 불러내라. 이걸 할 수 있는 건 너뿐이야.<end>"

## 1360164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難しい仕事だ。<line-break>できるか、ケリュネイア？<end>"
EN: "It will not be easy. Can you do it, Ceryneia?<end>"
KO: "어려운 일이다. 할 수 있겠나, 케류네이아?<end>"

## 1360165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったわ、兄さん。<line-break>勇者ネメアの妹の名に恥じない<line-break>働きをしてみせる。<end>"
EN: "All right, brother. I'll prove myself worthy of the name of Nemea the Hero's sister.<end>"
KO: "알겠어, 오빠. 용자 네메아의 여동생이라는 이름에 부끄럽지 않게 해내겠어.<end>"

## 1360166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、私はここにいるわ。<line-break>仲間を呼び出したかったら<line-break>私に話しかけて。<line-break>あとは、たのんだわ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'll remain here. If you want to summon your companions, come and speak to me. The rest is up to you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 난 여기 있어. 동료를 부르고 싶으면 나한테 말 걸어. 나머지는 부탁할게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1360167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、時に猶予はない。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, there is no time to spare.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 시간이 없다.<end>"

## 1360168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、行こう。<line-break>すべてを終わらせに。<end>"
EN: "Then let us go and put an end to everything.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가자. 모든 걸 끝내러.<end>"

## 1360169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おつかれさま。<line-break>連戦でいい顔つきになってきたよ。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>ここで君を待っていたのは<line-break>君におとぎ話を聞いてほしかったからさ。<line-break>舞台はアルノートゥンだよ。<end>"
EN: "Good work. All those battles are starting to give you the look of a seasoned fighter, <value:$28>. I was waiting here because I wanted you to hear a little fairy tale. It takes place in Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "수고했어. 연전 덕분에 얼굴이 제법 좋아졌네, <value:$28>. 여기서 널 기다린 건 동화를 하나 들려주고 싶었기 때문이야. 무대는 알노툰이야.<end>"

## 1360170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し前、封士イオンズという男がいた。<line-break>彼は怪物を退治したり封印しても、<line-break>憎悪が残ってしまうことを悩んでいた。<line-break>で、彼はイズキヤルという怪物と<line-break>戦ったとき、殺したり封印したりせず<line-break>忍耐強く説得を続けたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Not long ago, there was a man named Ions, a Sealer. It troubled him that hatred remained even after he defeated monsters or sealed them away. So when he fought a monster named Izkiyal, he neither killed nor sealed it away. Instead, he patiently kept trying to persuade it.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 이온즈라는 봉사가 있었어. 그는 괴물을 쓰러뜨리거나 봉인해도 증오가 남는 걸 고민했지. 그래서 이즈키얄라는 괴물과 싸웠을 때 죽이거나 봉인하지 않고 끈질기게 설득했어.<end>"

## 1360171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その説得は功を奏し、イズキヤルは<line-break>小さくなって、自分の力を封じ、<line-break>アルノートゥンの町に住むことにした。<line-break>町の人々も、徐々にイズキヤルに<line-break>親しみ、なじんできた。<line-break>怪物と人間、両者の間に交流が生まれた。<end>"
EN: "The persuasion paid off. Izkiyal made itself small, sealed away its own power, and settled in the town of Alnortun. Little by little, the townspeople warmed to Izkiyal and grew used to having it around. And so, monster and human began to interact.<end>"
KO: "그 설득은 효과가 있었고 이즈키얄은 몸을 작게 하고 자신의 힘을 봉인한 채 알노툰 마을에 살기로 했다. 마을 사람들도 조금씩 이즈키얄에게 정을 붙였고, 괴물과 인간 사이에 교류가 생겨났다.<end>"

## 1360172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけどイズキヤルみたいなティラの子は<line-break>いわば暴風雨や地震や洪水のような<line-break>自然の暴力の現れだからね。<line-break>思うままに暴れたいという思いは<line-break>どうしても本能として心の底に残ってた。<line-break>そこに悪い魔法使いが現れたんだ。<end>"
EN: "But a child of Tira like Izkiyal is a manifestation of nature's violence, much like a storm, an earthquake, or a flood. Deep down, the instinctive urge to run wild at will never quite went away. Then an evil sorcerer appeared.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이즈키얄 같은 티라의 아이는 폭풍이나 지진, 홍수 같은 자연의 폭력 그 자체야. 마음껏 날뛰고 싶다는 욕구가 본능으로 깊이 남아 있었지. 그때 나쁜 마법사가 나타났어.<end>"

## 1360173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ大変！　悪い魔法使いに<line-break>暴れたいという本能を呼び覚まされ<line-break>イズキヤルは暴れ出したよ。<line-break>そこに<value:$28>という<line-break>勇者が通りかかったんだけど、<line-break>無視して行っちゃったんだ。ひどいよね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, what a disaster! The evil sorcerer awakened Izkiyal's instinctive urge to run wild, and it began to rampage. Just then, a hero named <value:$28> happened along, but walked right past and ignored the whole thing. Awful, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "큰일이야! 나쁜 마법사가 날뛰고 싶은 본능을 깨워서 이즈키얄가 난동을 부리기 시작했어. 마침 <value:$28>라는 용사가 지나갔는데 무시하고 가 버렸지 뭐야. 너무하지?<end>"

## 1360174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イオンズはイズキヤルを追いつめた。<line-break>だけど、どうしても倒せなかったんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>イズキヤルはいわば彼の親友だったし、<line-break>イズキヤルの意志で暴れてたんじゃない。<line-break>そう、イオンズにはわかっていたからね。<end>"
EN: "Ions cornered Izkiyal, but he simply couldn't defeat it. Izkiyal was, in a way, his closest friend, and the rampage wasn't Izkiyal's own choice. Ions knew that.<end>"
KO: "이온즈는 이즈키얄를 궁지로 몰아넣었어. 하지만 끝내 쓰러뜨릴 수 없었지. 이즈키얄는 말하자면 그의 절친한 친구였고, 자기 의지로 날뛰는 것도 아니었거든. 그래, 이온즈는 그걸 알고 있었으니까.<end>"

## 1360175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イズキヤルも、彼を殺したくなかった。<line-break>だけど、<line-break>イズキヤルは本能にあらがえなかった。<end>"
EN: "Izkiyal didn't want to kill him, either. But it could not resist its own instincts.<end>"
KO: "이즈키얄도 그를 죽이고 싶지 않았어. 하지만 이즈키얄은 본능에 거스를 수 없었지.<end>"

## 1360176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イズキヤルは絶望を知り、<line-break>闇に墜ちましたとさ。<end>"
EN: "And so Izkiyal learned despair and fell into darkness.<end>"
KO: "이즈키얄는 절망을 알고 어둠에 빠졌답니다.<end>"

## 1360177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おしまい。おしまい。<line-break>じゃ、またあとで。<line-break>死んじゃったりしないでね。<end>"
EN: "The end, the end. See you later. Don't go dying on me, all right?<end>"
KO: "끝. 끝이야. 그럼 나중에 봐. 죽거나 하지는 마.<end>"

## 1360178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あっ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…앗. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1360179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いた？<line-break>アルノートゥンのジラークが死んだわ。<line-break>コーンス族を人間から守ろうとしてね。<end>"
EN: "Have you heard? Zsilak of Alnortun is dead. He was trying to protect the Konsu people from humans.<end>"
KO: "들었어? 알노툰의 지라크가 죽었어. 콘스족을 인간에게서 지키려다가.<end>"

## 1360180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…<line-break>弱い者を守るために戦ってきた。<line-break>大事なものを守るために剣を振るってた。<line-break>…そう信じてた。<end>"
EN: "I... I fought to protect the weak. I wielded my sword to protect what mattered to me. Or so I believed.<end>"
KO: "나… 약한 사람을 지키려고 싸워 왔어. 소중한 걸 지키려고 검을 휘둘렀어. …그렇게 믿었어.<end>"

## 1360181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このリベルダムの破壊だって…。<end>"
EN: "Even the destruction of Liberdam...<end>"
KO: "이 리벨덤의 파괴도….<end>"

## 1360182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど結局ゼネテスは戦争を挑んだ。<line-break>ゼネテスはゼネテスの大事なものを<line-break>守るために戦うことを選んだ。<end>"
EN: "But in the end, Xenetes started a war. He chose to fight for what mattered to him.<end>"
KO: "하지만 결국 제네테스는 전쟁을 걸었어. 제네테스는 자신에게 소중한 것을 지키기 위해 싸우는 길을 택한 거야.<end>"

## 1360183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラークも同じ。<line-break>争いでは悲しみしか得られないのに<line-break>どうしても争いは地上からなくならない。<end>"
EN: "Zsilak was the same. Conflict brings nothing but sorrow, yet it never disappears from the world.<end>"
KO: "지라크도 마찬가지야. 싸움에선 슬픔밖에 얻을 수 없는데도 어째서 싸움은 지상에서 사라지지 않는 걸까.<end>"

## 1360184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大事なものを守りたい者同士が<line-break>相手の大事なものを壊しあい続けるの。<line-break>ねえ！　なにが正しいの？　答えてよ！<end>"
EN: "People who want to protect what matters to them keep destroying what matters to each other. Tell me! What's right? Answer me!<end>"
KO: "소중한 걸 지키고 싶은 사람들끼리 서로 상대의 소중한 걸 계속 부숴. 있잖아! 뭐가 옳은 거야? 대답해 줘!<end>"

## 1360185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…開け放つもの。<end>"
EN: "...The one who sets things free.<end>"
KO: "…열어젖히는 자.<end>"

## 1360186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おとぎ話の魔人よ。<line-break>魂と引き替えに心の底のわだかまりを<line-break>開け放ってくれるの…<end>"
EN: "The demon from the fairy tale. In exchange for your soul, it sets free everything festering deep inside you...<end>"
KO: "동화에 나오는 마인이야. 영혼과 맞바꾸어 마음 깊은 곳의 응어리를 풀어 준다는…<end>"

## 1360187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、何もわからないの。<line-break>何が正しくて、何を正義と呼ぶのか？<line-break>わからないのに…。<line-break>剣を振り続けるなんてできないの。<end>"
EN: "I don't understand anything anymore. What's right? What can even be called justice? I don't know... I can't go on wielding my sword without knowing.<end>"
KO: "이제 아무것도 모르겠어. 무엇이 옳고 무엇을 정의라 부르는지? 모르면서… 계속 검을 휘두를 수는 없어.<end>"

## 1360188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そして私は<line-break>剣を振り続けることしか<line-break>してこなかったの。<end>"
EN: "And all I've ever done is wield my sword.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 나는 계속 검을 휘두르는 것밖에는 해 오지 않았어.<end>"

## 1360189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>、大変だ。<line-break>お前にカルラ将軍から<line-break>出頭命令が来てるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, this is serious. General Karula has ordered you to report in.<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>, 큰일이다. 카를라 장군에게서 출두 명령이 왔어.<end>"

## 1360190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい？<line-break>思い当たるふしはないってかい？<line-break>冗談言うなよ。<line-break>お前ほどの人間なら<line-break>カルラ将軍に目をつけられる理由なんて<line-break>腐るほどあるだろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "What, you can't think why? Don't make me laugh. Someone like you must've given General Karula more reasons to keep an eye on you than you can count.<end>"
KO: "이봐? 짚이는 데가 없다고? 농담하지 마. 너 정도 되는 인간이면 카를라 장군에게 찍힐 이유쯤은 썩어 넘치잖아.<end>"

## 1360191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかくロセン総督府に出頭しろってさ。<line-break>総督府は、元王宮のあの建物だ。<line-break>確かに伝えたぜ。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, you're to report to the Rocen Governor-General's Office. You know the building―the former royal palace. There, message delivered.<end>"
KO: "아무튼 로센 총독부에 출두하래. 총독부는 옛 왕궁 건물이야. 분명히 전했다.<end>"

## 1360192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>、大変だ。<line-break>お前にカルラ将軍から<line-break>出頭命令が来てるぞ。<end>"

KO: "어이, <value:$28>, 큰일이다. 카를라 장군에게서 출두 명령이 왔어.<end>"

## 1360197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかくロセン総督府に出頭しろってさ。<line-break>総督府は、元王宮のあの建物だ。<line-break>確かに伝えたぜ。<end>"

## 1360198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>だな。<line-break>聞いている。<line-break>通るがいい。<end>"
EN: "You are <value:$28>, I presume. I have been informed. You may pass.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>군. 얘기는 들었다. 지나가도 좋다.<end>"

## 1360199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less>50貴公が、ゼネテスの副官、<line-break><value:$28>か。<end>貴公が、ゼネテスの副官、<line-break><value:$2B>の<line-break><value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less>50You are Xenetes's adjutant, <value:$28>, are you?<end>You are Xenetes's adjutant, <value:$2B> <value:$28>, are you?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less>50귀공이 제네테스의 부관,<value:$28>인가.<end>귀공이 제네테스의 부관,<value:$2B>의<value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 1360200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、只者ではないな。<line-break>カルラ様から話は聞いている。<line-break>中へ入るがいい<end>"
EN: "I see. You are no ordinary person. Lady Karula has told me about you. You may enter.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 보통내기가 아니야. 카를라 님께 얘기는 들었다. 안으로 들어가라.<end>"

## 1360201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひさしぶり、<value:$28>。<line-break>いやー、感動の再会だねー。<line-break>感動、感動～。<end>"
EN: "Long time no see, <value:$28>. What a touching reunion! I'm moved. Truly moved.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이야, <value:$28>. 이야, 감동의 재회네. 감동, 감동~.<end>"

## 1360202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれから、いろいろあったよねー。<line-break>あんたがゼネテス側について<line-break>あたしの敵にまわったりさー。<end>"
EN: "A lot's happened since then, hasn't it? Like you siding with Xenetes and turning against me.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤로 이런저런 일이 많았지~. 네가 제네테스 편에 붙어서 내 적이 되기도 했고 말이야~.<end>"

## 1360203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、ぜんぜん根に持ってないから<line-break>うん、ぜんぜん。ホントよ。<line-break>だから、安心してよね。<end>"
EN: "But I don't hold a grudge. Not at all. Really. So don't worry.<end>"
KO: "나, 전혀 뒤끝 없어. 응, 전혀. 진짜야. 그러니까 안심해.<end>"

## 1360204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君に来てもらったのは他でもない。<line-break>国計に関わる大事があってのことだ。<end>"
EN: "I summoned you here for one reason: a matter of grave importance to the state.<end>"
KO: "자네를 부른 건 다름이 아니야. 국정에 관계된 중요한 일이 있어서다.<end>"

## 1360205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国を脅かす存在…。<line-break>つまり君には今日ここで消えてもらう。<line-break>すでに我が精鋭がこの部屋を囲んでいる。<end>"
EN: "You pose a threat to the Dyneskal Empire. Which means you must disappear here today. My finest troops have already surrounded this room.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국을 위협하는 존재…. 즉, 넌 오늘 여기서 사라져 줘야겠어. 이미 내 정예가 이 방을 포위하고 있다.<end>"

## 1360206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なーんてさ、どっかの宰相みたいに<line-break>インケンなことは考えてないから。<line-break>安心しちゃって。<line-break>あたしが青竜将軍カルラ。<line-break>ま、これまでのことは水に流して<line-break>サクサク行こうよ。とりあえずは、さ。<end>"
EN: "Or so some chancellor might say. I'm not plotting anything that underhanded, so relax. I'm Karula, the Azure Dragon General. Let's put everything that's happened behind us and get on with things. For now, anyway.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 어느 재상처럼 음침한 짓은 생각 안 하니까 안심해. 내가 청룡장군 카를라야. 자, 지금까지 일은 물에 흘려버리고 척척 가자고. 일단은 말이지.<end>"

## 1360207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちの副官。アイリーンのことで<line-break>ちょっと話があるのさ。<line-break>あんたにすごい関係あること。<end>"
EN: "I want to talk about my adjutant, Irene. It's got a lot to do with you.<end>"
KO: "우리 부관, 아이린에 관해 좀 할 말이 있어. 너와 아주 관련 있는 이야기야.<end>"

## 1360208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンが先のロストール戦で<line-break>あんたに戦いを挑んだでしょ？<line-break>んで、あんたは逃げた。<line-break>それから、どーも、落ち込んでてさ。<end>"
EN: "Irene challenged you during the recent battle at Rostorl, remember? And you ran away. She's been rather down ever since.<end>"
KO: "아이린이 지난 로스톨 전투에서 너한테 싸움을 걸었지? 그런데 네가 도망쳤잖아. 그 뒤로 계속 풀이 죽어 있더라고.<end>"

## 1360209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのコ、根がマジメだし、<line-break>思い詰めるとヤバいかなーって<line-break>思ってたら、失踪しちゃった。<end>"
EN: "She's serious by nature, so I was starting to worry she might do something drastic if she brooded over it too long. Then she disappeared.<end>"
KO: "그 애, 원래 진지한 성격이라 너무 몰아붙이면 위험하겠다 싶었는데, 실종돼 버렸어.<end>"

## 1360210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で…調べによると、彼女は失踪する直前に<line-break>リベルダムであんたと接触してんのよ。<line-break>ほれ、何があったか白状しなさい？<end>"
EN: "According to our investigation, she met with you in Liberdam just before she vanished. Come on, confess. What happened?<end>"
KO: "그래서… 조사해 보니 그녀는 실종되기 직전에 리벨덤에서 당신과 접촉했더라고. 자, 무슨 일이 있었는지 털어놔 봐.<end>"

## 1360211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむふむ、そんな話を…<line-break>ちょーっと期待してた展開では<line-break>なかったなあ。<end>"
EN: "Mm-hmm. So that's what happened... Not quite the story I was hoping for.<end>"
KO: "흠흠, 그런 이야기였구나… 내가 조금 기대했던 전개는 아니었네.<end>"

## 1360212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあまあ、冗談はおいといて。<line-break>アイリーンの行方を追えるのは<line-break><value:$28>だけみたいやね。<end>"
EN: "All right, jokes aside. It looks like <value:$28> is the only one who can track Irene down.<end>"
KO: "자자, 농담은 그만하고. 아이린의 행방을 쫓을 수 있는 건 <value:$28>뿐인 것 같네.<end>"

## 1360213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失踪原因はあんた、とまでは言わないけど<line-break>どうも関わりがあるみだいだし<line-break>ま、キッカケは確実にあんただしね。<line-break>それに、あんたなら<line-break>魔人にも知り合いがいるみたいだしさ。<line-break>アイリーンは魔人の話してたんでしょ？<end>"
EN: "I won't go so far as to say you're the reason she disappeared, but you do seem to be involved. And you were definitely what set it off. Besides, you apparently even know demonfolk. Irene talked about demonfolk, didn't she?<end>"
KO: "실종 원인이 너라고까지 하진 않겠지만 아무래도 관련은 있는 것 같고, 계기가 너였던 건 확실하잖아. 게다가 넌 마인과도 아는 사이가 있는 모양이고. 아이린이 마인 얘기를 했던 거지?<end>"

## 1360214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、冒険者<value:$28>に<line-break>騎士アイリーン・エルメスの<line-break>捜索を依頼します。<line-break>条件はアイリーンを生きている状態で<line-break>ロセン総督府に連れ戻し、<line-break>青竜軍に復帰させること。<end>"
EN: "I hereby commission the adventurer <value:$28> to find the knight Irene Hermes. The terms are that you bring Irene back alive to the Rocen Governor-General's Office and return her to service in the Azure Dragon Army.<end>"
KO: "그럼 모험자 <value:$28>에게 기사 아이린 에르메스의 수색을 의뢰합니다. 조건은 아이린을 살아 있는 상태로 로센 총독부에 데려와 청룡군에 복귀시키는 것입니다.<end>"

## 1360215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "報酬は錬剛石５個。<line-break>文句ある？<end>"
EN: "Your reward will be five Tempering Stones. Any complaints?<end>"
KO: "보수는 연강석 5개. 불만 있어?<end>"

## 1360216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、契約成立ってことで。<line-break>途中報告一切不要。結果をみせてよね。<line-break>以上。<end>"
EN: "Then we have a deal. No progress reports. Just show me the results. That's all.<end>"
KO: "그럼 계약 성립인 걸로. 중간 보고는 일절 필요 없어. 결과로 보여 줘. 이상.<end>"

## 1360217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "開け放つものって魔人がいるかって？<line-break>破壊神ウルグ様の円卓騎士筆頭<line-break>ヴァシュタールがそうだが、どうしたよ<end>"
EN: "You want to know if there's a demon called the Opener? That would be Vashtar, foremost among the Knights of the Round Table serving Lord Ulugh, God of Destruction. Why do you ask?<end>"
KO: "열어젖히는 자라는 마인이 있냐고? 파괴신 울그 님의 원탁기사 필두 바슈타르가 그렇긴 한데, 왜?<end>"

## 1360219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな伝説ができてたのは知ってたが…<line-break>ヴァシュタールの老いぼれに<line-break>何を聞こうってんだ？<line-break>伝説は伝説に過ぎない。<line-break>あいつはそんな親身な奴じゃないぜ？<line-break>最近はめっきりやる気もなくしてる。<end>"
EN: "I knew that legend had been making the rounds, but what do you expect to learn from an old wreck like Vashtar? A legend's just a legend. He's not the helpful sort, you know. Lately, he's lost all motivation.<end>"
KO: "그런 전설이 생긴 건 알고 있었지만… 바슈타르 그 늙은이에게 뭘 들으려는 거지? 전설은 전설일 뿐이야. 녀석은 그렇게 친절한 놈도 아니라고. 요즘은 완전히 의욕도 잃었고.<end>"

## 1360220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度も繰り返される光と闇の戦いに<line-break>やれ飽きただの、もう消えたいだの。<line-break>あきらめてオトナになれよって思うぜ。<line-break>そんな奴より、俺に聖杯を持ってきたら<line-break>騎士どころか貴族にも王様にもして…<end>"
EN: "He keeps whining that he's sick of the endless cycle of battles between light and darkness, that he wants to disappear. I say he should accept it and grow up. Forget him. Bring me the Grail, and I'll make you not just a knight, but a noble or even a king...<end>"
KO: "몇 번이고 반복되는 빛과 어둠의 싸움이 질렸다느니, 이제 사라지고 싶다느니. 포기하고 어른이 되라고 말해 주고 싶군. 그런 녀석보다 내게 성배를 가져오면 기사뿐 아니라 귀족도 왕도 만들어 줄 텐데…<end>"

## 1360221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァシュタールの居場所？<line-break>ああ、だいたいの見当ならつくぜ。<line-break>ヴァシュタールの柩って遺跡だ。<line-break>趣味悪いだろ？　普通、自分の寝床に<line-break>柩なんてつけるか？<line-break>まあ、そういう奴だ。<end>"
EN: "Where is Vashtar? Oh, I have a pretty good idea. There's a ruin called Vashtar's Coffin. Awful taste, isn't it? Who calls his own bed a coffin? Well, that's the sort of man he is.<end>"
KO: "바슈타르가 있는 곳? 아아, 대충 짐작은 가. 바슈타르의 관이라는 유적이야. 취향 나쁘지? 보통 자기 잠자리에 관 같은 이름을 붙이냐? 뭐, 그런 녀석이야.<end>"

## 1360222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブルの南西に偽りの森ってあるだろ？<line-break>その奥にオズワルドっていう町がある。<line-break>そこにある遺跡だよ。<line-break>でも、もう町の人間のソウルを奪って<line-break>闇の門を開けにいってるかもな。<line-break>奴がいなかったからって文句はなしだぜ？<end>"
EN: "You know the Forest of Deceit southwest of Noble? There's a town called Oswald deep inside it. The ruin is there. But Vashtar may already have stolen the townspeople's Souls and gone off to open the Dark Gate. Don't come complaining to me if he's not there, all right?<end>"
KO: "노블 남서쪽에 거짓의 숲이 있지? 그 안쪽에 오즈월드라는 마을이 있어. 거기 있는 유적이야. 하지만 이미 마을 사람들의 소울을 빼앗고 어둠의 문을 열러 갔을지도 몰라. 녀석이 없었다고 불평하진 마?<end>"

## 1360223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ、この世界から争いが、<line-break>そして悲しみがなくならないのか…<line-break>それがつらいのか、女よ？<end>"
EN: "Does it pain you, woman, that conflict and sorrow never vanish from this world?<end>"
KO: "왜 이 세계에서 싸움이, 그리고 슬픔이 사라지지 않는가… 그게 괴로운가, 여자여?<end>"

## 1360224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしさに毒されているのだな、女。<line-break>やさしさという名の欲に。<end>"
EN: "You have been poisoned by kindness, woman―by the desire that calls itself kindness.<end>"
KO: "친절함에 중독됐군, 여자. 친절함이라는 이름의 욕망에.<end>"

## 1360225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "争いはなくならない。それが摂理だ。<line-break>勝者は傲り、敗者は傷つき悲しむ。<line-break>そのゆがみの総和が創造の絶対値なのだ。<line-break>安寧…完全に均質化された世界は、<line-break>人らしく意志する者にとって<line-break>動きのない死。<end>"
EN: "Conflict will never end. That is the natural order. The victors grow arrogant; the defeated are wounded and grieve. The sum of those distortions is creation's absolute value. For anyone possessed of a human will, peace―a perfectly uniform world―is motionless death.<end>"
KO: "싸움은 사라지지 않는다. 그것이 섭리다. 승자는 교만해지고 패자는 상처받고 슬퍼한다. 그 왜곡의 총합이 창조의 절대값이다. 안녕… 완전히 균질화된 세계는 인간답게 의지를 지닌 자에게 움직임 없는 죽음이다.<end>"

## 1360226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怠惰なる死を恐れた大いなる意志は<line-break>この永遠に続く争いの摂理を作った。<line-break> <line-break>お前が怒る不当な勝利も、<line-break>お前が嘆く不当な敗北も、<line-break>すべてはその中で踊っているにすぎない。<end>"
EN: "The Great Will, fearing the idle death of stasis, created this law of eternal conflict. The unjust victories that enrage you and the unjust defeats you lament are merely dancing within it.<end>"
KO: "나태한 죽음을 두려워한 거대한 의지는 이 영원히 이어지는 싸움의 섭리를 만들었다. 네가 분노하는 부당한 승리도, 네가 한탄하는 부당한 패배도 모두 그 안에서 춤추고 있을 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1360227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛みは実感するだろう。<line-break>だが、勝利は迷妄。<line-break>敗北は虚妄にすぎぬ。<line-break>真に勝利する方法はひとつ。<line-break>争いを楽しんで踊るだけだ。<end>"
EN: "Pain, at least, is real. But victory is a delusion, and defeat no more than an illusion. There is only one way to achieve true victory: delight in the conflict and dance.<end>"
KO: "고통은 실감하겠지. 하지만 승리는 미망, 패배는 허망일 뿐이다. 진정 이기는 방법은 하나. 싸움을 즐기며 춤추는 것뿐이다.<end>"

## 1360228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな理屈じゃ救われない！<line-break>争いで傷つく人の悲しみは<line-break>どうなるの！<end>"
EN: "That reasoning won't save anyone! What about the grief of those hurt by the fighting?<end>"
KO: "그런 논리로는 구원받지 못해! 싸움으로 상처받는 사람들의 슬픔은 어떻게 할 건데!<end>"

## 1360229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愛ゆえに、守りたいがゆえに、戦う。<line-break>それがバカげていることに気付いたか？<line-break>それは評価しよう。<end>"
EN: "You fight out of love, out of the desire to protect. Have you realized how foolish that is? For that, I give you credit.<end>"
KO: "사랑하기 때문에, 지키고 싶기 때문에 싸운다. 그게 어리석다는 걸 깨달았나? 그 점은 인정하지.<end>"

## 1360230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、お前の傲慢さはいただけない。<line-break>お前の中にある、やさしさから生まれた<line-break>弱者への支配欲ともいうべき憐れみはな。<end>"
EN: "But I cannot abide your arrogance: the pity born of your kindness, which might better be called a desire to dominate the weak.<end>"
KO: "하지만 네 오만함은 좋지 않다. 네 안의 상냥함에서 태어난, 약자를 지배하려는 욕망이라고도 할 수 있는 그 연민 말이다.<end>"

## 1360231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の近しい者は<line-break>今、大きな運命の波にあらがい<line-break>未来のために道を切り開こうとしている。<line-break>その者は大いなる痛みを経験するだろう。<line-break>それは大いなる痛みにふさわしい。<line-break>大事をなすからだ。<end>"
EN: "Someone close to you is even now struggling against a great tide of destiny, trying to carve a path toward the future. That person will endure great pain―pain befitting one who is to accomplish something momentous.<end>"
KO: "네 가까운 이는 지금 거대한 운명의 파도에 맞서 미래를 위해 길을 열려 하고 있다. 그자는 큰 고통을 겪게 될 것이다. 큰일을 이루려 하니 그에 걸맞은 큰 고통이지.<end>"

## 1360232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…違う、そうじゃない。悲しいの<line-break>…なぜ彼にだけ痛みを背負わせるの？<line-break>なぜ私はその痛みから彼を守れないの？<end>"
EN: "No... That's not it. I'm sad. Why must he bear all that pain alone? Why can't I protect him from it?<end>"
KO: "…아니야, 그런 게 아니야. 슬픈 거야… 왜 그 사람에게만 고통을 짊어지게 해? 왜 나는 그 고통에서 그를 지켜 주지 못해?<end>"

## 1360233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしいのだな。だがそれはお門違いだ。<line-break>守ることのできぬお前を恨むなど<line-break>その者は思いつきもすまい。<line-break>ただ、お前とともに歩いた時間を<line-break>ときに懐かしく思うだけだ。<line-break>そして、残酷だが、そこまでだ。<end>"
EN: "You are kind. But that kindness is misplaced. It would never occur to that person to resent you for being unable to offer protection. There would only be an occasional fond remembrance of the time spent walking beside you. And, cruel though it is, that is all.<end>"
KO: "상냥하군. 하지만 그건 번지수를 잘못 짚었다. 지키지 못한 너를 원망한다는 생각은 그 사람에게 떠오르지도 않을 거다. 다만 너와 함께 걸었던 시간을 때때로 그리워할 뿐이다. 그리고 잔혹하지만, 거기까지다.<end>"

## 1360234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の近しい者は敗れ、命を落とした。<line-break>お前はそれを守りきれなかったことを<line-break>嘆いている。<line-break>大義のかかった戦いではなかった。<line-break>つまらぬ原因で起ったそんな戦いで<line-break>なぜ命を落とさねばならなかった？　と…<end>"
EN: "Someone close to you was defeated and died. You grieve because you could not protect that person to the end. It was not a battle fought for any great cause. Why did that person have to die in a battle sparked by something so trivial? That is what you ask...<end>"
KO: "네 가까운 이는 패배해 목숨을 잃었다. 너는 그를 지키지 못한 것을 슬퍼하고 있지. 대의가 걸린 싸움도 아니었다. 하찮은 원인으로 벌어진 그런 싸움에서 왜 목숨을 잃어야 했느냐고….<end>"

## 1360235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦いの大義名分と命とは別の価値だ。<line-break>そして、どんなささいな戦いであれ<line-break>戦いに敗れれば失うものがある。<end>"
EN: "The cause invoked to justify a battle and the lives at stake have separate worth. And however trivial the battle, defeat always costs something.<end>"
KO: "싸움의 대의명분과 목숨은 서로 다른 가치다. 그리고 아무리 사소한 싸움이라도 패하면 잃는 것이 있다.<end>"

## 1360236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でも、そうじゃない。悲しいの。<line-break>…なぜ彼だけが痛みを背負ったの？<line-break>なぜ私はその痛みから守れなかったの？<end>"
EN: "But that's not it. I'm sad. Why was he the only one made to bear the pain? Why couldn't I protect him from it?<end>"
KO: "…하지만 그런 게 아니야. 슬픈 거야. …왜 그 사람만 고통을 짊어진 거야? 왜 나는 그 고통에서 지켜 주지 못했어?<end>"

## 1360237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、その者がここにいたとしても…<line-break>守れなかったお前を恨むなど<line-break>その者は思いつきもすまい。<line-break>ただ、お前とともに同じ時を<line-break>歩めなかったと、残念に思うだけだ。<line-break>そして、残酷だが、そこまでだ。<end>"
EN: "Even if that person were here... it would never occur to that person to resent you for failing to offer protection. There would only be regret at not having walked through the same span of time beside you. And, cruel though it is, that is all.<end>"
KO: "설령 그 사람이 여기 있다 해도… 지키지 못한 너를 원망한다는 생각은 하지도 않을 거다. 그저 너와 같은 시간을 함께 걷지 못한 것을 아쉬워할 뿐이지. 그리고 잔혹하지만 거기까지다.<end>"

## 1360238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わから…ない…。<end>"
EN: "I... don't understand...<end>"
KO: "모르…겠어….<end>"

## 1360239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "理解したくないことは<line-break>理解できない。<line-break>そういうものだ。<line-break>わからなくなったら尋ねることだ。<line-break>世界にお前以外に他人がいるのは<line-break>そのためなのだから。<end>"
EN: "What you do not wish to understand, you cannot understand. That is how it is. When you lose your way, ask someone. That is why there are other people in this world besides you.<end>"
KO: "이해하고 싶지 않은 것은 이해할 수 없다. 그런 법이다. 모르겠으면 물어봐라. 세상에 너 말고 다른 사람이 있는 건 그 때문이니까.<end>"

## 1360240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この者がお前と戦うのを拒んだのも<line-break>憐れみや、恨みからではない。<line-break>そうしたいからそうしただけだ。<line-break>もう一度戦って問うてみるがいい。<line-break>何度問うても同じこと。<line-break>その者はただ、自由にするだけだ。<end>"
EN: "This person refused to fight you not out of pity or resentment, but simply because that was what this person wished to do. Fight once more and ask again, if you wish. No matter how many times you ask, the answer will be the same. This person will simply act freely.<end>"
KO: "이 사람이 너와 싸우길 거부한 것도 연민이나 원한 때문이 아니다. 그러고 싶어서 그랬을 뿐이지. 다시 싸워 물어봐. 몇 번을 물어도 같을 거다. 그 사람은 그저 자유롭게 할 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1360241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これもひとつの終わり。<line-break>ひとつの結論。<line-break>ひとつの帰結、だ。<end>"
EN: "This too is an ending. A conclusion. An outcome.<end>"
KO: "이것도 하나의 끝. 하나의 결론. 하나의 귀결이다.<end>"

## 1360242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は価値を優先させた。<line-break>確かに、他者と共存するためには<line-break>共通価値を作り、遵守することが必要だ。<line-break>魔人に魂を売ったこの薄汚い女に<line-break>お前たちは制裁を加えなければならない。<line-break>それが正しい。お前は価値に従ったのだ。<end>"
EN: "You placed shared values above all else. Certainly, coexistence requires people to establish common values and abide by them. You must punish this filthy woman who sold her soul to the demonfolk. That is right. You acted according to those values.<end>"
KO: "넌 가치를 우선했다. 확실히 타인과 공존하려면 공통의 가치를 만들고 지킬 필요가 있지. 마인에게 영혼을 판 이 더러운 여자에게 너희는 제재를 가해야 한다. 그게 옳아. 넌 가치에 따른 거야.<end>"

## 1360243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、そうすることで<line-break>自ら意志することを放棄していないか？<line-break>お前自身はどこに生きているのだ？<line-break>なんにせよ戦わねばならないようだ。<line-break>お前たちの価値に従えば<line-break>魔人は倒さねばならぬ存在なのだろう？<end>"
EN: "But in doing so, have you not forsaken your own will? Where in all this do you yourself live? In any case, it seems we must fight. By your values, the demonfolk are beings who must be destroyed, are they not?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇게 함으로써 스스로 뜻하는 것을 포기하고 있지는 않나? 너 자신은 어디에 살아 있는 거지? 어쨌든 싸워야 할 모양이군. 너희의 가치관대로라면 마인은 쓰러뜨려야 할 존재겠지?<end>"

## 1360244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずはお前の勝利だ。<line-break>お前ほどの力を持った相手とは<line-break>ここ数千年の間戦っていないな。<line-break>だが、残念ながら私を完全に<line-break>消し去ってくれるほどの力はないようだ。<line-break>さらばだ。時間のムダだったな。<end>"
EN: "For now, the victory is yours. I have not fought anyone with power to rival yours in several thousand years. Unfortunately, you do not seem powerful enough to erase me completely. Farewell. What a waste of time.<end>"
KO: "우선은 네 승리다. 너만큼 힘을 가진 상대와는 지난 수천 년 동안 싸워본 적이 없군. 하지만 안타깝게도 나를 완전히 소멸시킬 정도의 힘은 없는 모양이다. 잘 있어라. 시간 낭비였군.<end>"

## 1360246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "価値を優先させるなら、<line-break>この者はお前と戦い、<line-break>勝利せねばならなかった。<end>"
EN: "Had this person placed shared values first, fighting and defeating you would have been necessary.<end>"
KO: "가치를 우선한다면 이 자는 너와 싸워 이겨야 했다.<end>"

## 1360247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きるとは意志すること。<line-break>命とは意志して動くこと。<line-break>価値、そして夢とはその道標を持つこと。<line-break>それ以上ではない。<line-break>価値や夢がとかく独り歩きしがちだが<line-break>この者は意志することを優先させたのだ。<end>"
EN: "To live is to exert one's will. Life is will set in motion. Values and dreams serve as guideposts, nothing more. Values and dreams often take on lives of their own, but this person placed personal will first.<end>"
KO: "산다는 것은 의지를 갖는 것. 생명이란 의지를 갖고 움직이는 것. 가치와 꿈은 그 길잡이를 갖는 것. 그 이상이 아니다. 가치나 꿈이 흔히 독주하지만 이 사람은 의지를 갖는 것을 우선했다.<end>"

## 1360248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意志すること…生きることを…。<end>"
EN: "To exercise the will... to live...<end>"
KO: "의지를 갖는 것… 살아가는 것을….<end>"

## 1360249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな女よ、お前の答えはそこにある。<line-break>そしてお前はそれを絶対に理解できない。<line-break>だが、感じることくらいならできよう。<line-break>感じるためにすべきことは<line-break>自分で考えるがいい。<end>"
EN: "Foolish woman, your answer lies there. You will never be able to understand it. But perhaps you can at least feel it. Decide for yourself what you must do to feel it.<end>"
KO: "어리석은 여자여, 네 답은 바로 거기에 있다. 그리고 넌 절대로 그것을 이해하지 못할 것이다. 다만 느끼는 정도라면 가능하겠지. 느끼기 위해 무엇을 해야 할지는 스스로 생각해라.<end>"

## 1360250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "繰り返され代わり映えせぬ光と闇の戦い<line-break>その主役がどうしたことだ？<line-break>魔人とは戦えと、教えられなかったか？<end>"
EN: "What has become of the central figure in this endlessly repeated, unchanging battle between light and darkness? Were you not taught to fight the demonfolk?<end>"
KO: "되풀이되어 달라지는 것 없는 빛과 어둠의 싸움. 그 주역이 어쩐 일이냐? 마인과는 싸우라고 배우지 않았나?<end>"

## 1360251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0これが意志する男というものか。<line-break>なるほどお前の未来は見えぬな。<line-break>それはお前の前に道がないからだ。<end>これが意志する女というものか<line-break>美しいぞ…もっと顔を見せてくれ…<line-break>これは美しい女だ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0So this is a man who exercises his own will. No wonder I cannot see your future: there is no path laid out before you.<end>So this is a woman who exercises her own will. Beautiful... Let me see more of your face. What a beautiful woman.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0이것이 의지를 지닌 남자라는 것인가. 과연 네 미래가 보이지 않는군. 네 앞에 정해진 길이 없기 때문이다.<end>이것이 의지를 지닌 여자라는 것인가. 아름답군… 얼굴을 더 보여 다오…. 참으로 아름다운 여자다.<end>"

## 1360252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が存在する、そのあとの世界が<line-break>退屈なものでないかもしれぬ。<line-break>私も消えるにはおよばぬか…<line-break>さらばだ。<line-break>お前に勝利する必要のないことは<line-break>時に幸福な事実だ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps the world that follows your arrival in it will not be so dull. Perhaps there is no need for me to disappear... Farewell. Sometimes, having no need to defeat you is a happy thing.<end>"
KO: "네가 존재하는 그 이후의 세계는 지루하지 않을지도 모르겠군. 나도 사라질 필요는 없나…. 잘 있어라. 네게 승리할 필요가 없다는 건 때로 행복한 사실이지.<end>"

## 1360253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…<line-break>私、私…、あなたと…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I... I... with you...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 나, 나는… 당신과….<end>"

## 1360254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "So, you want to resign as a knight and vice commander of the Azure Dragon Army so you can travel with <value:$28>. Is that it?<end>"
KO: "…그래서 <value:$28>과 여행하려고 기사와 청룡군 부장을 그만두고 싶다는 거군.<end>"

EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "네!<end>"

## 1360256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムダに元気のよい返事でよろしい。<end>"
EN: "Good. An unnecessarily enthusiastic answer.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없이 힘찬 대답이라 좋군.<end>"

## 1360257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…で、三流冒険者さんよ。<line-break>この落とし前どうつけてくれんの？<line-break>依頼はアイリーンを連れ帰る、だよ？<line-break>アイリーン、帰ってこないじゃん。<line-break>それでも報酬払わないとダメなわけ？<end>"
EN: "Now then, third-rate adventurer. How are you going to make this right? The job was to bring Irene back, remember? Well, she isn't coming back. Do I still have to pay you?<end>"
KO: "…그래서 삼류 모험가 씨. 이 책임은 어떻게 질 거야? 의뢰는 아이린을 데려오는 거였지? 아이린은 안 돌아오잖아. 그래도 보수를 줘야 해?<end>"

## 1360258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "払わないとダメ<end>"
EN: "You still have to pay.<end>"
KO: "안 내면 안 돼<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、いいか。<line-break>アイリーンの無事がわかっただけでもさ。<line-break>せんべつっつーことで。<end>"
EN: "Oh, all right. At least now I know Irene's safe. Consider it a parting gift.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐나. 아이린이 무사하다는 걸 안 것만으로도. 전별금이라고 생각해.<end>"

## 1360262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "보상이 필요 없다는 건 알겠는데 그 손가락은 뭐야? 대신 나더러 따라오라는 거야?<end>"

## 1360263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "安！　そんなんじゃ釣り合わないよ。<line-break>あたしを仲間にしたいんなら<line-break>世界の一つでもくんないと。<end>"
EN: "Cheap! That's nowhere near enough. If you want me to join you, you'll have to give me a whole world.<end>"
KO: "싸잖아! 그 정도론 수지가 안 맞아. 날 동료로 삼고 싶으면 세계 하나쯤은 줘야지.<end>"

KO: "좋아<end>"

## 1360266

## 1360267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしみたいな奴のところにはさ、<line-break>ウソついて夢見せて笑わせてやろうって<line-break>ささやく悪魔は来ないと思ってた。<line-break>そしたら、世界をくれるって言う<line-break>バカが来たよ。<line-break>アハハハ！<end>"
EN: "I never thought a devil would come to someone like me, whispering lies to give me a dream and make me laugh. Instead, along comes an idiot offering me the world. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "나 같은 녀석한테는 거짓말로 꿈을 보여 주고 웃게 해 주겠다며 속삭이는 악마 따윈 안 올 줄 알았어. 그런데 세계를 주겠다는 바보가 왔지. 아하하하!<end>"

## 1360268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいよ！<line-break>なんだか、面白そうだしね。<line-break>力、貸しましょ。<end>"
EN: "All right! It sounds like it might be fun. I'll lend you my strength.<end>"
KO: "좋아! 왠지 재미있을 것 같고. 힘을 빌려줄게.<end>"

## 1360269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をするときは<line-break>猫屋敷で呼び出してくれるのね。<line-break>待ってるわん。<end>"
EN: "When you want me to travel with you, just summon me at Cat Manor. I'll be waiting, woof!<end>"
KO: "같이 여행할 때는 고양이 저택에서 불러 주는 거지. 기다리고 있을게 멍.<end>"

## 1360270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーんだ、それじゃダメだわ。<line-break>ちょっと期待したのにな。<end>"

KO: "뭐야, 그럼 안 되겠네. 조금 기대했는데.<end>"

## 1360271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "開け放つもの、ヴァシュタールか…。<line-break>ソウルと引き替えに開け放って<line-break>もらおっかな、あたしの心の底のアレ。<end>"
EN: "Vashtar, the Opener... Maybe I'll trade my Soul and have him open up that thing at the very bottom of my heart.<end>"
KO: "열어젖히는 자, 바슈타르라…. 소울과 맞바꿔 열어 달라고 해볼까, 내 마음 밑바닥의 그걸.<end>"

## 1360274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ……、ムリか。<line-break>あたしみたいな<line-break>夢を信じられない奴のところには…。<line-break>ウソついてだまして夢見せて<line-break>笑わせてやろうって悪魔も<line-break>やってこないんだろうなー。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Guess not. No devil would come to someone like me, someone who can't believe in dreams... Not even one willing to lie and deceive me, just to give me a dream and make me laugh.<end>"
KO: "후후…… 무리인가. 나처럼 꿈을 믿지 못하는 녀석한테는…. 거짓말로 속여서 꿈을 보여 주고 웃게 해 주려는 악마조차 안 오는 모양이네.<end>"

## 1360275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、がんばってくれい。<line-break>こんなあたしだけど、<line-break>あんたの幸せを遠くで祈ってるよ…。<end>"
EN: "Well, do your best. For what it's worth, I'll be praying for your happiness from afar...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 힘내. 이런 나지만 멀리서 네 행복을 빌고 있을게….<end>"

## 1360276

## 1360278

## 1360279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360281

## 1360282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、あなたは、心をなくした人形…。<line-break>忘却の仮面の騎士だもの。<line-break>そう言うでしょうね。<line-break>でも、仮面の下のあなたが<line-break>そんなことを言うとは<line-break>思ってなかったのだけれど？<end>"
EN: "Heh. You're a puppet without a heart, the knight in the Mask of Oblivion. Of course you'd say that. But I never expected to hear it from the person beneath the mask, you know?<end>"
KO: "후후, 당신은 마음을 잃은 인형…. 망각의 가면을 쓴 기사니까 그렇게 말하겠지. 하지만 가면 아래의 당신이 그런 말을 할 줄은 몰랐어.<end>"

## 1360283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気付いておられたのですか…。<line-break>私の精神と記憶が戻りつつあるのを…。<end>"
EN: "You knew...? That my mind and memories were returning...<end>"
KO: "…알고 계셨습니까… 제 정신과 기억이 돌아오고 있다는 걸…<end>"

## 1360284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、それらを取り戻してなお、<line-break>今の私は仮面の騎士サイフォスです。<line-break>それ以外の誰でもありません。<line-break>…それとも、<line-break>あなたが愛してくださっているのは<line-break>ロイという男なのですか？<end>"
EN: "Even with my mind and memories restored, I am still Xiphos, the masked knight. I am no one else. Or is it the man named Roy whom you love?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그것들을 되찾고도 지금의 저는 가면의 기사 사이포스입니다. 그 외 누구도 아닙니다. …아니면 당신이 사랑하는 건 로이라는 남자인 겁니까?<end>"

## 1360285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね。<line-break>わかったわ。<line-break>ありがとう。私のサイフォス。<end>"
EN: "Yes... I understand. Thank you, my Xiphos.<end>"
KO: "…그래. 알았어. 고마워. 나의 사이포스.<end>"

## 1360286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイア様、<line-break>ここは私がくいとめます。<line-break>お逃げください。<end>"
EN: "Lady Argyleshire, I'll hold the enemy here. Please, escape.<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어 님, 여기는 제가 막겠습니다. 도망치십시오.<end>"

## 1360287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたを置いていく気はないわ。<line-break>あなたは私の一番のお気に入りの<line-break>オモチャなの。<line-break>見てなさい。<line-break>精霊の生命を、魔法の火種程度に考えて<line-break>次々と奪っていった人間たちよ…。<end>"
EN: "I won't leave you behind. You're my favorite toy. Now watch, you humans who took the spirits' lives one after another, treating them as no more than kindling for your magic...<end>"
KO: "널 두고 갈 생각은 없어. 넌 내가 가장 좋아하는 장난감이야. 잘 봐. 정령의 생명을 마법의 불씨 정도로 여기고 차례차례 빼앗아 간 인간들이여….<end>"

## 1360288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は精霊たちの死の苦しみから<line-break>生まれた魔人…。<line-break>　<line-break>すべての生きとし生けるものに<line-break>死の苦しみを教えてあげるわ！<end>"
EN: "I am a demon born of the agony of dying spirits... I will teach every living thing the agony of death!<end>"
KO: "나는 정령들의 죽음의 고통에서 태어난 마인…. 모든 살아 있는 것들에게 죽음의 고통을 가르쳐 주겠어!<end>"

## 1360289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "サイ…フォス…。<end>"
EN: "Xi... phos...<end>"
KO: "사이… 포스….<end>"

## 1360290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "サイフォス…<line-break>あなただけを行かせない…<line-break>私も…。<end>"
EN: "Xiphos... I won't let you go alone... I'll come too...<end>"
KO: "사이포스… 너만 보내진 않아… 나도….<end>"

## 1360291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、あなたたちも死ぬの…。<line-break>みんな、無に帰すの…。<line-break>ウフフフ…。<end>"
EN: "And all of you will die as well... Everyone will return to nothingness... Ahahaha...<end>"
KO: "그리고 너희도 죽는 거야…. 모두 무로 돌아가는 거야…. 후후후….<end>"

## 1360292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　すべてを…無に帰すの？　　　　　　<line-break>　　あなたの…サイフォスへの気持ちも？<end>"
EN: "Will you return everything... to nothing? Even your feelings for Xiphos?<end>"
KO: "모든 것을… 무로 돌릴 거야? 네… 사이포스를 향한 마음도?<end>"

## 1360293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の…気持ち…？<end>"
EN: "My... feelings...?<end>"
KO: "내… 마음…?<end>"

## 1360294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　何もかも無に帰すことはないわ…。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　私の中で眠りなさい。<line-break>　　　　　その気持ちと一緒に…。<end>"
EN: "You needn't reduce everything to nothing... Sleep within me, together with those feelings...<end>"
KO: "모든 것이 무로 돌아가지는 않아…. 내 안에서 잠들렴. 그 마음과 함께….<end>"

## 1360295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "サイフォス…。<end>"
EN: "Xiphos...<end>"
KO: "사이포스….<end>"

## 1360296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今の感じ…。<line-break>姉さんか？<end>"
EN: "That feeling just now... Sis?<end>"
KO: "…방금 그 느낌…. 누나인가?<end>"

## 1360297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仮面が…<line-break>忘却の仮面がとれたのか…。<end>"
EN: "The mask... The Mask of Oblivion came off...<end>"
KO: "가면이… 망각의 가면이 벗겨진 건가….<end>"

## 1360298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…セラ。<end>"
EN: "Sera...<end>"
KO: "…세라.<end>"

## 1360299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ロイ。<end>"
EN: "Roy...<end>"
KO: "…로이.<end>"

## 1360300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、行こう。<line-break>姉さん。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Sis. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "자, 가자. 누나.<end>"

## 1360301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして…！<line-break>姉さん！<end>"
EN: "Why...?! Sis!<end>"
KO: "어째서…! 언니!<end>"

## 1360302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いて…、セラ。<line-break>アーギルシャイアは精霊の<line-break>断末魔の化身なの。　　　　　　<line-break>古代魔法文明から今に至るまで、<line-break>人々は魔法を使い、その源である<line-break>精霊たちの命を奪っていったわ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, Sera... Argyleshire is the embodiment of the spirits' dying screams. From the ancient magical civilization to this very day, people have used magic, taking the lives of the spirits from which it draws its power.<end>"
KO: "들어 봐… 세라. 아르길샤이어는 정령들의 단말마가 형상화된 존재야. 고대 마법문명부터 지금까지 사람들은 마법을 쓰며 그 근원인 정령들의 생명을 빼앗아 왔어.<end>"

## 1360303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな精霊たちの苦しみを背負った<line-break>アーギルシャイアの心は<line-break>憎悪と虚無で冷たく渦巻いていた…。<line-break>彼女に体を乗っ取られた私の心は<line-break>その渦に呑みこまれて<line-break>消えそうだったの…。だけど…。<end>"
EN: "Burdened with all the spirits' suffering, Argyleshire's heart was a cold maelstrom of hatred and the Void... When she took over my body, my heart was swallowed by that maelstrom and nearly vanished... But...<end>"
KO: "그런 정령들의 고통을 짊어진 아르길샤이어의 마음은 증오와 허무로 차갑게 소용돌이치고 있었어…. 그녀에게 몸을 빼앗긴 내 마음은 그 소용돌이에 삼켜져 사라질 것 같았어…. 하지만….<end>"

## 1360304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、彼女のロイへの想いは<line-break>凍てついた心の中の唯一の温もりとして<line-break>私の心を包んでくれたわ。<line-break>そして、その想いだけは、<line-break>彼女にとってたったひとつの温もりは、<line-break>私の心の中に残っているの…。<end>"
EN: "But her feelings for Roy were the only warmth in that frozen heart, and they enfolded mine. Those feelings―the one warmth she possessed―still remain within me...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그녀의 로이에 대한 마음은 얼어붙은 마음속 유일한 온기로 내 마음을 감싸 줬어. 그리고 그 마음만은, 그녀에게 단 하나뿐인 온기는 내 마음속에 남아 있어….<end>"

## 1360305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…セラ、私は言ったはずだ。<line-break>お前が追いつけない地平まで<line-break>お前の姉を奪って逃げると。<end>"
EN: "Sera, I told you. I would steal your sister away and flee beyond any horizon you could reach.<end>"
KO: "…세라, 나는 말했을 텐데. 네가 따라잡을 수 없는 지평선까지 네 누나를 빼앗아 달아나겠다고.<end>"

## 1360306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さようなら、セラ。<line-break>悲しい魔人と仮面の騎士の想いを胸に、<line-break>私たち……行くわ。<end>"
EN: "Goodbye, Sera. We carry the feelings of a sorrowful demon and a masked knight in our hearts... and now we're going.<end>"
KO: "…잘 있어, 세라. 슬픈 마인과 가면의 기사의 마음을 가슴에 품고 우리… 갈게.<end>"

## 1360307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何をしている、<line-break>置いていくぞ。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing? I'm leaving you behind.<end>"
KO: "…뭐 하고 있나, 두고 간다.<end>"

## 1360319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがこの物語の行く末を<line-break>見極めたいのなら、お行きなさい。<line-break>私は、待ちます。<end>"
EN: "If you wish to see how this story ends, then go. I will wait.<end>"
KO: "당신이 이 이야기의 끝을 확인하고 싶다면 가세요. 저는 기다릴게요.<end>"

## 1360320

JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>私もともに<line-break>旅をさせてもらえないだろうか？<end>"

KO: "<value:$28>, 나도 함께 여행하게 해주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1360321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml

## 1360332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞いて…、セラ。<line-break>アーギルシャイアは精霊の<line-break>断末魔の化身なの。　　　　　　<line-break>古代魔法文明から今に至るまで、<line-break>人々は魔法を使い、その源である<line-break>精霊たちの命を奪っていったわ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, Sera... Argyleshire is the embodiment of the spirits' dying screams. From the ancient magical civilization to this very day, people have used magic, taking the lives of the spirits from which it draws its power.<end>"
KO: "들어 봐… 세라. 아르길샤이어는 정령들의 단말마가 형상화된 존재야. 고대 마법문명부터 지금까지 사람들은 마법을 쓰며 그 근원인 정령들의 생명을 빼앗아 왔어.<end>"

## 1360333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな精霊たちの苦しみを背負った<line-break>アーギルシャイアの心は<line-break>憎悪と虚無で冷たく渦巻いていた…。<line-break>彼女に体を乗っ取られた私の心は<line-break>その渦に呑みこまれて<line-break>消えそうだったの…。だけど…。<end>"
EN: "Burdened with all the spirits' suffering, Argyleshire's heart was a cold maelstrom of hatred and the Void... When she took over my body, my heart was swallowed by that maelstrom and nearly vanished... But...<end>"
KO: "그런 정령들의 고통을 짊어진 아르길샤이어의 마음은 증오와 허무로 차갑게 소용돌이치고 있었어…. 그녀에게 몸을 빼앗긴 내 마음은 그 소용돌이에 삼켜져 사라질 것 같았어…. 하지만….<end>"

## 1360334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、彼女のロイへの想いは<line-break>凍てついた心の中の唯一の温もりとして<line-break>私の心を包んでくれたわ。<line-break>そして、その想いだけは、<line-break>彼女にとってたったひとつの温もりは、<line-break>私の心の中に残っているの…。<end>"
EN: "But her feelings for Roy were the only warmth in that frozen heart, and they enfolded mine. Those feelings―the one warmth she possessed―still remain within me...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그녀의 로이에 대한 마음은 얼어붙은 마음속 유일한 온기로 내 마음을 감싸 줬어. 그리고 그 마음만은, 그녀에게 단 하나뿐인 온기는 내 마음속에 남아 있어….<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はあの日、この忘却の仮面の力で　<line-break>デスギガースを倒した。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、その副作用で記憶を封じられ<line-break>魔人のしもべとなってしまったのだ。<line-break>彼女、アーギルシャイアのな…。<end>"
EN: "That day, I used the power of the Mask of Oblivion to defeat Death Gigas. But its aftereffects sealed away my memories, and I became the servant of a demon―Argyleshire...<end>"
KO: "나는 그날 이 망각의 가면의 힘으로 데스기가스를 쓰러뜨렸다. 하지만 그 부작용으로 기억이 봉인되어 마인의 하인이 되고 말았지. 그녀, 아르길샤이어의….<end>"

## 1360355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなったな。<line-break>お前と刃を交えることができて、<line-break>お前の力を感じた。うれしかったぞ。<line-break>これは、お前が守ってくれ。<end>"

## 1360372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "束縛の腕輪は、<line-break>使えば使うほど、その所有者と<line-break>対象を縛りつけていきます。<line-break>それがどういう結果をもたらすのか？<line-break>考えたことはないのですか？<end>"
EN: "The more the Bracelet of Binding is used, the more tightly it binds its owner and its target together. Have you never considered what that might lead to?<end>"
KO: "속박의 팔찌는 쓰면 쓸수록 소유자와 대상을 서로 묶어 갑니다. 그것이 어떤 결과를 가져오는지 생각해 본 적 없습니까?<end>"

## 1360373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私に…<line-break>そのような価値があるのでしょうか？<end>"

KO: "저에게… 그런 가치가 있을까요?<end>"

KO: "저에게… 아니… 아무것도 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1360375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "束縛の腕輪は、闇の神器です。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>そのような危険なものなのに、<line-break>あなたはなぜそれを<line-break>手放そうとしないのですか？<end>"

KO: "속박의 팔찌는 어둠의 신기입니다. 그렇게 위험한 물건인데도 당신은 왜 그걸 놓으려 하지 않는 겁니까?<end>"

## 1360376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break>このようなときに来られるとは…。<line-break><line-break>私の体は間もなく崩れ落ちます。 <line-break>そのような姿を見せたくはありません。<line-break>お引取りください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... To think you would come at a time like this. My body will soon collapse. I do not want you to see me that way. Please, go.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 이런 때 오시다니… 제 몸은 곧 무너져 내릴 겁니다. 그런 모습을 보여드리고 싶지 않습니다. 돌아가 주세요.<end>"

## 1360378

## 1360379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは…束縛の腕輪。<line-break>私の命をこの世に縛り付ける、<line-break>闇の神器…。<end>"
EN: "That is... the Bracelet of Binding. A Dark Artifact that binds my life to this world...<end>"
KO: "그건… 속박의 팔찌. 내 목숨을 이 세상에 붙들어 매는 어둠의 신기….<end>"

## 1360380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを手にして、<line-break>私の前に現れたのは、<line-break>どういう理由からなのですか？<line-break>…亡き長老の代わりに、あなたが私を<line-break>束縛しようというのですか？<end>"
EN: "Why have you come before me with that in your hands? Do you mean to bind me in the late elder's place?<end>"
KO: "그걸 손에 들고 제 앞에 나타난 건 무슨 이유입니까? …죽은 장로 대신 당신이 저를 속박하려는 겁니까?<end>"

## 1360381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者のあなたにとって私は、<line-break>精霊神への道を閉ざす障害に<line-break>すぎないはずです。<line-break>それなのに…<end>"
EN: "To an adventurer like you, I should be nothing more than an obstacle barring the way to the Spirit God. And yet...<end>"
KO: "모험자인 당신에게 저는 정령신으로 가는 길을 막는 장애물에 불과할 텐데요. 그런데도….<end>"

## 1360382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。　　　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>わかりません。私には…。<end>"
EN: "...I don't understand. I...<end>"
KO: "………………. 모르겠습니다. 저로서는….<end>"

## 1360383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…以前、長老が力を<line-break>解放してしまったせいで、<line-break>束縛の腕輪の力が弱まっています。<end>"
EN: "When the elder released the power before, it weakened the Bracelet of Binding.<end>"
KO: "…전에 장로가 힘을 해방해 버린 탓에 속박의 팔찌의 힘이 약해져 있습니다.<end>"

## 1360384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "腕輪の力が無いまま<line-break>時を重ねれば…私の体は<line-break>崩れ落ちるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "If the bracelet remains powerless, my body will eventually collapse.<end>"
KO: "팔찌의 힘이 없는 채로 시간이 흐르면… 제 몸은 무너져 내릴 겁니다.<end>"

## 1360385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここには、私に残された<line-break>ただひとつの役目があります。<line-break>　<line-break>その時が来るまで…<line-break>私はここにいます。<end>"
EN: "There is one final duty left for me here. Until the time comes, I will remain.<end>"
KO: "여기에는 제게 남겨진 단 하나의 역할이 있습니다. 그때가 올 때까지… 저는 여기 있겠습니다.<end>"

## 1360386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、それは<line-break>そう遠い未来ではないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "And I do not believe that time is far off.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그것은 그리 먼 미래가 아닐 겁니다.<end>"

## 1360387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで私の役目は…。<line-break>あとは…終わらせるだけ…。<end>"
EN: "Now my part is... All that remains is to bring it to an end...<end>"
KO: "이걸로 내 역할은…. 이제… 끝내기만 하면 돼….<end>"

## 1360388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私のことは心配に及びません。<line-break>どうかお気をつけて、<line-break><value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to worry about me. Please take care, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "제 걱정은 하지 않으셔도 됩니다. 부디 조심하세요, <value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1360389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1360390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスが永久に失われたとき、<line-break>私はその鍵としての、ラドラスの<line-break>巫女としての役割を終えました。<line-break>私の最後の役割は火の精霊神<line-break>ウルカーンの封印…<end>"
EN: "When Ladras was lost forever, my duty as its key and its priestess came to an end. My final duty is to keep Ulkarne, the fire spirit god, sealed away...<end>"
KO: "라드라스가 영원히 사라졌을 때 저는 그 열쇠로서, 라드라스의 무녀로서 역할을 끝냈습니다. 제 마지막 역할은 불의 정령신 울칸의 봉인….<end>"

## 1360391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それこそ…ここでかろうじて<line-break>生きている私の、<line-break>ただひとつの存在理由…。<end>"
EN: "That is the one reason I remain here, barely alive...<end>"
KO: "그것이야말로… 여기서 간신히 살아 있는 나의 단 하나뿐인 존재 이유….<end>"

## 1360392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "教えてください…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>役目でもなく、価値でもない…<line-break>なら、人とは、何のために<line-break>生きるべきものなのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Please, tell me... If neither duty nor worth gives life its purpose, then what should a person live for?<end>"
KO: "가르쳐 주세요…. 역할도 아니고 가치도 아니라면… 사람은 무엇을 위해 살아야 하는 걸까요?<end>"

## 1360393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>わかりません。 <line-break>やはり、私には…。<end>"
EN: "...I don't know. I still cannot understand...<end>"
KO: "………………. 모르겠습니다. 역시 저로서는…<end>"

## 1360394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…胸の中で何かが、<line-break>芽生えているような気がします。<line-break>言葉では表せない何かが…。<end>"
EN: "And yet... I feel something beginning to grow within me. Something I cannot put into words...<end>"
KO: "하지만… 가슴속에서 무언가 싹트고 있는 것 같아요. 말로 표현할 수 없는 무언가가….<end>"

## 1360395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はわからなくなりました。<line-break>巫女としての役割を果すことが<line-break>正しいのか？<line-break>あなたから感じる不思議な力…。<line-break>その可能性を信じることが正しいのか？<end>"
EN: "I no longer know what is right. Should I fulfill my duty as a priestess? Or should I trust in what may come of the strange power I sense in you?<end>"
KO: "저는 알 수 없게 되었습니다. 무녀로서 역할을 다하는 게 옳은지, 당신에게서 느껴지는 이상한 힘… 그 가능성을 믿는 게 옳은지.<end>"

## 1360396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれにせよ、私の命は<line-break>あなたに委ねられています。<line-break>私はあなたを拒むことはできないのです。<line-break>ならば、私にできることは、<line-break>あなたの意思と可能性を<line-break>信じることだけです。<end>"
EN: "Either way, my life is in your hands. I cannot refuse you. All I can do is believe in your will and your potential.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 제 목숨은 당신에게 맡겨져 있습니다. 저는 당신을 거부할 수 없습니다. 그렇다면 제가 할 수 있는 건 당신의 의지와 가능성을 믿는 것뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1360397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お気をつけて、<value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "Please take care, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "조심히 가세요, <value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1360398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1360399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愛する…人のため？<end>"
EN: "For someone you love...?<end>"
KO: "사랑하는… 사람을 위해?<end>"

## 1360400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。　　　　　　<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>人を愛する…ということを、<line-break>私は、理解していません…。<end>"
EN: "... I do not understand... what it means to love someone.<end>"
KO: "………………. 사람을 사랑한다는 걸 저는 이해하지 못하고 있습니다….<end>"

## 1360401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長老、シェムハザは、かつて私に…<line-break>そう言いましたが、<line-break>今の、この私には…。<line-break>私にとっては、理解できないこと。<line-break>あなたの答えは、<line-break>今の私には重過ぎました。<end>"
EN: "Elder Shemhaza once told me... the same thing. But as I am now, it is beyond my understanding. Your answer is more than I can bear now.<end>"
KO: "장로 셈하자는 예전에 제게… 그렇게 말했습니다만, 지금의 저로서는…. 저에게는 이해할 수 없는 일입니다. 당신의 대답은 지금의 저에겐 너무 무거웠습니다.<end>"

## 1360402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さようなら、<line-break><value:$28>様…。<line-break>私の死を、苦いものにした人…。<end>"
EN: "Farewell, <value:$28>... You who made death bitter for me...<end>"
KO: "안녕히, <value:$28> 님…. 제 죽음을 쓰디쓰게 만든 사람….<end>"

## 1360403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>あなたは、いつでも変わらない…<line-break>そして、いつでも不思議な人でした。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You never changed. And you were always a mystery to me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신은 언제나 변함없고… 언제나 신기한 사람이었어요.<end>"

## 1360404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "価値を失ったはずの道具である私を、<line-break>何度も訪ねて来てくれたあなたに、<line-break> <line-break>胸に芽生えた警戒心が、<line-break>徐々に期待へと変わってゆくのを、<line-break>自覚せずにはいられませんでした。<end>"
EN: "I was only a tool that should have lost all value, yet you came to see me time and again. I could not help but realize that the wariness growing within me was slowly turning to expectation.<end>"
KO: "가치를 잃은 도구였던 저를 몇 번이나 찾아와 준 당신에게, 가슴에 싹튼 경계심이 서서히 기대감으로 변해 가는 걸 깨닫지 않을 수 없었습니다.<end>"

## 1360405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうです、私も、自分のことを<line-break>意識できるように変わったのです。<line-break>あなたに触れるうちに…。<line-break>そして、それこそが、今、<line-break>自分の身を保つための、<line-break>かけがえのない力の源となっています。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Through my contact with you, I too changed and became aware of myself. And now, that awareness is the irreplaceable source of the strength sustaining my existence.<end>"
KO: "그래요. 저도 제 자신을 의식할 수 있도록 변했어요. 당신과 접하면서…. 그리고 그것이야말로 지금 제 존재를 유지하게 해 주는 무엇과도 바꿀 수 없는 힘의 원천이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 1360406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "巫女として、私は力を尽くしました。<line-break>そして、あなたを阻止することが<line-break>できないと知りました。<line-break>ならば、私にできることは、<line-break>あなたの意思が正しいのだと<line-break>信じることだけです。<end>"
EN: "As a priestess, I did everything in my power. And I learned that I could not stop you. So all I can do is believe that what you intend to do is right.<end>"
KO: "무녀로서 저는 최선을 다했습니다. 그리고 당신을 막을 수 없다는 걸 알게 되었습니다. 그렇다면 제가 할 수 있는 건 당신의 의지가 옳다고 믿는 것뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1360407

KO: "조심히 가세요, <value:$28> 님….<end>"
