KO: "<value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1360409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何者にも束縛されずに<line-break>生きるということとは、<line-break>どういうことなのでしょうか。        <line-break>あなたの心に触れてから、私は、<line-break>そんな生き方があるらしいということを<line-break>知ったようです。<end>"
EN: "What does it mean to live free of all bonds? Since touching your heart, I feel I have come to understand that such a life may be possible.<end>"
KO: "어떤 것에도 얽매이지 않고 산다는 건 어떤 걸까요. 당신의 마음에 닿고 나서 저는 그런 삶의 방식도 있는 것 같다는 걸 알게 됐습니다.<end>"

## 1360410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…。<end>"
EN: "And yet...<end>"
KO: "하지만….<end>"

## 1360411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "束縛されないことばかりに<line-break>一生懸命になるということは、　　<line-break> <line-break>そのこと自体に束縛されている<line-break>ということではないのでしょうか…？<end>"
EN: "If one devotes oneself entirely to being free of all bonds, does that not become a bond in itself?<end>"
KO: "속박되지 않으려고만 필사적인 건, 그 자체에 속박되어 있다는 뜻 아닐까요…?<end>"

## 1360412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、自由な未来を夢見るということに<line-break>束縛されているのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Am I bound by my own dream of a free future?<end>"
KO: "저는 자유로운 미래를 꿈꾸는 것에 속박되어 있는 걸까요?<end>"

## 1360413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様には<line-break>教えていただきたいことが<line-break>たくさんあります。<end>"
EN: "There is so much I would like you to teach me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님께 배우고 싶은 게 정말 많습니다.<end>"

## 1360414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…。<end>"
EN: "Um...<end>"
KO: "저기….<end>"

## 1360415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "御迷惑をおかけすると思いますが…。<line-break>よろしくお願いいたします。<end>"
EN: "I fear I may be a burden, but... I hope you will be patient with me.<end>"
KO: "폐를 끼칠 것 같습니다만…. 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1360417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ。他でもありませぬ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>我が心の師であるアンギルダン殿と、<line-break>今こうしてひとつの旗印のもと、<line-break>旅をすることができる喜びを…<end>"
EN: "Yes, sir. I merely wished to tell you what a joy it is to travel as we do now, under one banner with Angeerdan, my mentor in spirit...<end>"
KO: "예. 다름이 아닙니다. 제 마음의 스승인 앙길단 님과 지금 이렇게 하나의 깃발 아래 함께 여행할 수 있다는 기쁨을…<end>"

## 1360418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、待った。<line-break>そういう話ならば、ちょうど<line-break><value:$28>も来たことじゃ。<line-break>パーティーのリーダーである<line-break><value:$28>に言ったら<line-break>どうかな。<end>"
EN: "Ah, hold on. If that's what this is about, <value:$28> has arrived at just the right time. Why not say it to <value:$28>, our party leader?<end>"
KO: "어이, 잠깐. 그런 이야기라면 마침 <value:$28>도 왔으니 파티 리더인 <value:$28>에게 말하는 게 어떻겠나.<end>"

## 1360419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしとしちゃ、おぬしのような<line-break>いい歳の男に礼など言われるのは、<line-break>ちょいと照れくさいぞ。<end>"
EN: "To be honest, it's a little embarrassing to have a grown man like you thanking me.<end>"
KO: "나로선 자네 같은 다 큰 남자에게 감사 인사를 받는 게 좀 쑥스럽구먼.<end>"

## 1360420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいや、そこをあえて<line-break>言わせていただきますぞ、<line-break>アンギルダン殿！<line-break>あなたに言われた一言、<line-break>このイオンズの胸に、今でも熱く熱く<line-break>焼きついておるのですから！<end>"
EN: "No, I must insist on saying it, Angeerdan! The words you spoke to me still burn fiercely in this Ions's heart, seared there to this day!<end>"
KO: "아니오, 그래도 굳이 말씀드리겠습니다, 앙길단 공! 당신께 들은 그 한마디가 이 이온즈의 가슴에 지금도 뜨겁게, 뜨겁게 새겨져 있으니까요!<end>"

## 1360421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう興奮なさるな。<line-break>そろそろイオンズ殿も<line-break>胸の病が気になる歳じゃろうに。<line-break>ま、しかし、言われてみれば…<line-break>わしは一体、どんな大層なことを<line-break>言ったのかな？<end>"
EN: "Don't get so worked up. You're about the age when you ought to start worrying about your heart, Sir Ions. Still, now that you mention it... what grand words did I say, exactly?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 흥분하지 마시오. 슬슬 이온즈 공도 가슴병이 신경 쓰일 나이일 텐데. 뭐, 그러고 보니… 내가 대체 무슨 대단한 말을 했던가?<end>"

## 1360422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官になったおぬしと同じくらい、<line-break>わしもいろいろと険しい人生の峠を<line-break>越えてきたもんで、忘れてしまった。<end>"
EN: "I've had just as many trials in my life as you have in becoming a priest, so I've forgotten.<end>"
KO: "신관이 된 그대만큼이나 나도 여러 험한 인생의 고비를 넘어왔더니 잊어버렸네.<end>"

## 1360423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、一理ありますな。<line-break>…よし、<value:$28>、<line-break>ちょいとこっちへ来てくれい。<end>"
EN: "A fair point. Very well. <value:$28>, come over here a moment.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 일리가 있네. …좋아, <value:$28>, 잠깐 이리 와 보게.<end>"

## 1360424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "喝！　若かりし日のアンギルダン殿の<line-break>役をやってもらうのじゃ！<line-break>光栄と思ってわしと対峙せよっ！<end>"
EN: "Hah! You shall play Angeerdan in his younger days! Count it an honor, and face me!<end>"
KO: "얍! 젊은 시절 앙길단 공 역할을 해 줘야겠다! 영광으로 알고 나와 맞서라!<end>"

## 1360425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…はあ？<end>"
EN: "What...?<end>"
KO: "…하아?<end>"

## 1360426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あの頃、ここアルノートゥンで<line-break>編成された魔物退治の傭兵部隊で、<line-break>わしとアンギルダン殿は出会った。<line-break>当時のわしは、何をも恐れぬ無頼漢…<line-break>大金を稼げることによだれを垂らし、<line-break>血沸き肉踊らせる有り様。<end>"
EN: "Back then, Angeerdan and I met in a mercenary company raised here in Alnortun to hunt Monsters. In those days, I was a fearless rogue, drooling at the thought of riches and spoiling for bloodshed.<end>"
KO: "…그 무렵 여기 알노툰에서 편성된 마물 토벌 용병부대에서 나와 앙길단 공은 처음 만났지. 당시 나는 아무것도 두려워하지 않는 무뢰한… 큰돈을 벌 수 있다는 생각에 군침을 흘리고 피가 끓어오르던 놈이었다.<end>"

## 1360427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほうほう。<end>"
EN: "Go on.<end>"
KO: "호오, 호오.<end>"

## 1360428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一方、似たような命知らずで<line-break>あふれかえる傭兵部隊にありながら、<line-break> <line-break>剣を抱いてむっつり険しい顔で<line-break>黙りこくっている赤い鎧の男がおった。<line-break>…それが、アンギルダン殿じゃった。<end>"
EN: "The company was overflowing with equally reckless men, yet among them was a man in red armor, clutching his sword and brooding in grim silence. That man was Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "한편 비슷한 목숨 아까운 줄 모르는 자들로 넘쳐나는 용병부대 안에서 검을 끌어안고 험악한 얼굴로 말없이 있는 붉은 갑옷의 남자가 있었지. …그게 앙길단 공이었네.<end>"

## 1360429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは彼に興味を覚えたが、<line-break>よく話す間もなく、<line-break>魔物の侵攻が始まった。<end>"
EN: "He caught my interest, but before we had much chance to speak, the Monsters attacked.<end>"
KO: "나는 그에게 흥미를 느꼈지만 제대로 이야기도 나누기 전에 마물의 침공이 시작되었다.<end>"

## 1360430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハブで大発生した<line-break>アンデッドどもの大群じゃ。<line-break>わしは、一も二もなく突撃した！<line-break>うおおおおおっ！<end>"
EN: "It was a vast horde of undead that had appeared in Ahab. Without a moment's hesitation, I charged! Raaaaagh!<end>"
KO: "아합에서 대량 발생한 언데드 무리다. 나는 앞뒤 가리지 않고 돌격했지! 우오오오오!<end>"

## 1360431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええい、空気の読めぬ奴じゃ！<line-break>おぬしは今、アンデッド！<end>"
EN: "Blast it, can't you take a cue? You're one of the undead now!<end>"
KO: "에잇, 눈치 없는 녀석! 너는 지금 언데드다!<end>"

## 1360432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、魔物どもは手ごわかった。<line-break>わしらは散り散りになり、<line-break>気付けばふたりきりじゃった。<line-break>わしは、恐怖にとり憑かれた。<line-break>死神と呼ぶにも等しい、<line-break>すさまじい恐怖心に…。<end>"
EN: "But the Monsters were formidable. Our company was scattered, and before I knew it, only the two of us remained. Terror took hold of me―a fear so dreadful it might as well have been Death itself.<end>"
KO: "하지만 마물들은 강했다. 우리는 뿔뿔이 흩어졌고 정신을 차리니 둘뿐이었지. 나는 공포에 사로잡혔다. 사신이라 불러도 될 정도로 끔찍한 공포에….<end>"

## 1360433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世に神はいないのか？<line-break>助けを求めている者を、<line-break>神は、見殺しにするのか？<line-break>信仰心の欠片もない無頼のくせに、<line-break>いざ自分が死に臨むとなれば、<line-break>神の名を引き合いに出す。<end>"
EN: "Was there no god in this world? Would a god simply stand by and let one pleading for help die? Faithless rogue that I was, the moment death stared me in the face, I invoked the name of god.<end>"
KO: "이 세상에 신은 없는가? 도움을 구하는 자를 신은 죽게 내버려 두는가? 믿음이라고는 한 조각도 없는 무뢰한 주제에 막상 자신이 죽음을 앞두면 신의 이름을 들먹인다.<end>"

## 1360434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは、とんでもないうつけ者だった。<line-break>やけになって飛び出し、<line-break>神を呪って死のうとした…。<end>"
EN: "I was an utter fool. In despair, I rushed out, meaning to curse god as I died.<end>"
KO: "나는 정말 어리석은 놈이었다. 자포자기해 뛰쳐나와 신을 저주하며 죽으려 했지….<end>"

## 1360435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その時じゃ。アンギルダン殿は、<line-break>刃を抜いてわしの前に立ちはだかった！<line-break>そして…こう言われたのじゃ。<line-break>信じているぞ…とな。<end>"
EN: "That was when Angeerdan drew his blade and barred my way! Then he said... “I believe.”<end>"
KO: "그때였지. 앙길단 공은 칼을 뽑고 내 앞을 막아섰다! 그리고… 이렇게 말했지. 믿고 있다…고.<end>"

## 1360437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ま、何と言うか…さすがはわしが惚れた<line-break>大人物じゃな。この大器に影響され、<line-break>わしは神官に鞍替えしたわけじゃ。<line-break>"
EN: "...Well, what can I say? He's every inch the great man I fell for. Inspired by a man of such caliber, I changed course and became a priest."
KO: "……………….  뭐랄까… 역시 내가 반한큰 인물이군. 이 큰 그릇에 영향을 받아, 나도 신관으로 갈아탄 거지."

## 1360438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン殿！<end>"
EN: "Angeerdan!<end>"
KO: "앙길단 공!<end>"

## 1360439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、うむっ！<line-break>い、いや、かたじけない…。<line-break>あまり日差しが暖かかったので、つい。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Ah, yes! N-no, forgive me... The sunlight was so warm that I drifted off.<end>"
KO: "으, 으음! 아, 아니, 고맙네…. 햇볕이 너무 따뜻해서 그만.<end>"

## 1360440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーむ、よし！<line-break>そろそろ日も傾いてくる頃じゃ、<line-break>続きは酒場で話すとしようじゃないか。<line-break>パーティーには結束が何より大事。<line-break>違うか？　<line-break><value:$28>、イオンズ殿？<end>"
EN: "Hmm, right! The sun will soon be sinking, so let us continue this tale at the tavern. Nothing matters more to a party than fellowship. Wouldn't you agree, <value:$28>? Sir Ions?<end>"
KO: "흠, 좋아! 이제 해도 슬슬 기울 때니 뒷이야기는 술집에서 하지. 파티에는 결속이 무엇보다 중요해. 안 그런가? <value:$28>, 이온즈 공?<end>"

## 1360441
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっしゃる通りで。<end>"
EN: "Just as you say.<end>"
KO: "말씀하신 대로입니다.<end>"

## 1360442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃろう！<line-break>ゆこう、そして呑み、大いに語ろうぞ！<line-break>はっはっはっは！<end>"
EN: "Exactly! Come, let us drink and talk to our hearts' content! Ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "그렇지! 가자, 그리고 마시고 마음껏 이야기하자! 하하하하!<end>"

## 1360443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても<line-break>私なんかが王女様になってしまうなんて<line-break>…複雑な気分です<end>"
EN: "Even so, to think that someone like me should become a princess... I have mixed feelings about it.<end>"
KO: "그런데 나 같은 사람이 공주님이 되어 버리다니… 복잡한 기분이에요.<end>"

## 1360444
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…、昔、王女様に<line-break>かつぎだされたことがあるんです。<end>"
EN: "I... was once put forward as a princess, long ago.<end>"
KO: "저… 예전에 공주님께 추대된 적이 있어요.<end>"

## 1360445
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セルモノー叔父様とエリス叔母様に<line-break>貴族たちが反乱を起こしたのです。<line-break>テジャワの変と言います。<line-break>私はそのときのことはわかりません。<line-break>まだ、ものごころつく前でしたから…<end>"
EN: "The nobles rose in rebellion against my uncle Selmono and my aunt Eris. It is known as the Tejava Incident. I remember nothing of it. I was too young to understand what was happening.<end>"
KO: "셀모노 숙부님과 에리스 숙모님에게 귀족들이 반란을 일으켰습니다. 데자와의 변이라고 합니다. 저는 그때 일을 몰라요. 아직 철이 들기 전이었으니까….<end>"

## 1360446
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、そのとき、私は<line-break>母と、光とを失ったんです…<end>"
EN: "But that was when I lost both my mother and the light.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그때, 저는 어머니와 빛을 잃었습니다….<end>"

## 1360447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのことを人に聞かされて、<line-break>ずいぶんエリス叔母様を恨みました。<line-break>従妹のティアナ様にも嫉妬しました。<end>"
EN: "When I was told what had happened, I came to hate my aunt Eris deeply. I was jealous of my cousin, Lady Tiana, too.<end>"
KO: "그 일을 남에게 듣고 에리스 숙모님을 많이 원망했어요. 사촌 티아나 님도 질투했고요.<end>"

## 1360448
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私の生命を助けてくれたのは<line-break>実は、エリス叔母様だったんです。<end>"
EN: "But in truth, it was my aunt Eris who saved my life.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제 목숨을 구해 준 건 사실 에리스 숙모님이었어요.<end>"

## 1360449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叛乱が叔母様の手腕によって失敗すると<line-break>母は私に毒を飲ませ、<line-break>無理心中しようとしました。<line-break>叔母様は私を必死で介抱して<line-break>瀕死の私を蘇生させてくれたんです。<line-break>ただ、視力は戻らなかったそうです。<end>"
EN: "When my aunt's skill brought the rebellion to ruin, my mother made me drink poison and tried to take me with her in death. My aunt fought desperately to save me and brought me back from the brink. But I am told my sight never returned.<end>"
KO: "반란이 숙모님의 수완으로 실패하자 어머니는 제게 독을 먹이고 동반자살하려 했습니다. 숙모님은 필사적으로 저를 간호해 죽어가던 저를 살려 주셨어요. 다만 시력은 돌아오지 않았다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1360450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも、私に光を取り戻そうとして<line-break>叔母様は、その後、ずいぶん<line-break>毒の研究をなさったそうです。<line-break>…レルラ＝ロントン様が<line-break>そうおっしゃっていました。<end>"
EN: "Even so, my aunt still hoped to restore the light to me, and afterward she devoted herself to studying poisons. That is what Lord Relra-Lonton told me.<end>"
KO: "그래도 제게 빛을 되찾아 주려고 숙모님은 그 뒤로 오랫동안 독을 연구하셨다고 합니다. …렐라=론톤 님이 그렇게 말씀하셨어요.<end>"

## 1360451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとで確認しようと<line-break>ゼネテス様にお話を伺いましたら、<line-break>はぐらかされました。<line-break>ふふ、あのはぐらかし方だと<line-break>レルラ＝ロントン様のお話は<line-break>事実ですね。<end>"
EN: "Later, I asked Lord Xenetes about it to confirm the story, but he tried to evade the question. That little evasion was answer enough. Lord Relra-Lonton's story must be true.<end>"
KO: "나중에 확인하려고 제네테스 님께 여쭤봤더니 얼버무리시더군요. 후후, 그 얼버무리는 걸 보니 렐라 론톤 님 이야기는 사실이네요.<end>"

## 1360452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レルラ＝ロントン様は<line-break>お顔が広く、情報に通じたお方です。<line-break>間違いはないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Lord Relra-Lonton has connections everywhere and is very well informed. I doubt he was mistaken.<end>"
KO: "렐라 론톤 님은 인맥이 넓고 정보에 밝으신 분입니다. 틀림없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1360453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "１度王位をねらった者である私が<line-break>生きていられたのも<line-break>それならば、うなずけます。<line-break>それなのに、私は何も見ようとせず、<line-break>暗闇の中で、随分、叔母様を恨み、<line-break>ティアナ様に嫉妬しました。<end>"
EN: "If that is true, it explains why I was allowed to live despite once being used to lay claim to the throne. And yet I refused to see anything. In my blindness, I spent so long hating my aunt and envying Lady Tiana.<end>"
KO: "한번 왕위를 노렸던 제가 살아남을 수 있었던 것도 그렇다면 이해가 됩니다. 그런데도 저는 아무것도 보려 하지 않은 채 어둠 속에서 오랫동안 숙모님을 원망하고 티아나 님을 질투했습니다.<end>"

## 1360454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も知ろうとしなかったことで<line-break>私は、自分の殻に閉じこもり、<line-break>叔母様を傷つけたことと思います。<end>"
EN: "By refusing to learn anything, I withdrew into my shell, and I think that hurt my aunt.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 알려 하지 않았기에 저는 제 껍질 속에 틀어박혀 숙모님을 상처 입혔다고 생각합니다.<end>"

## 1360455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのころは１人で暗闇に生きている<line-break>つもりでいました。いろんな人に<line-break>生かされていたことを知ろうともせず…<line-break>だから、今は、知ろうと思います。<line-break>せっかく、あなたが、私を暗闇から<line-break>引きずり出してくださったんですから。<end>"
EN: "Back then, I thought I was living alone in the darkness. I made no effort to understand how many people had kept me alive. But now, I want to know. After all, you went to the trouble of dragging me out of that darkness.<end>"
KO: "그때는 혼자 어둠 속에서 산다고 생각했어요. 여러 사람의 도움으로 살아왔다는 걸 알려고도 하지 않았죠…. 그래서 지금은 알고 싶습니다. 모처럼 당신이 저를 어둠에서 끌어내 주셨으니까요.<end>"

## 1360456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ来られた。<line-break>じゃが、もうここには<line-break>闇の王女はおられぬ。<line-break>闇の王女は傷を癒すため<line-break>地上で最も闇の深い場所…。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. But the Princess of Darkness is no longer here. To heal her wounds, she has gone to the darkest place on earth...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔구나. 하지만 이제 이곳에는 어둠의 왕녀가 계시지 않는다. 어둠의 왕녀는 상처를 치유하기 위해 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳으로….<end>"

## 1360457
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロル様の城、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かわれた。<end>"
EN: "To Lord Balor's castle, the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "발로르 님의 성, 폐성의 왕좌로 향하셨다.<end>"

## 1360458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては<line-break>闇の王女が傷を癒されるまでの<line-break>時間稼ぎにすぎぬ。<end>"
EN: "All this is merely to buy time while the Princess of Darkness heals her wounds.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 어둠의 왕녀께서 상처를 치유할 때까지의 시간 벌이에 불과하다.<end>"

## 1360459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>いかに神となったとはいえ<line-break>エルファスもあれにはかなわぬ。<line-break>賞賛するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the dark artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. Even though Elfas has become a god, he is no match for it. I commend your indomitable will.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는구나, 네메아. 어둠의 신기·파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 보통 사람이라면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 터다. 아무리 신이 되었다 해도 엘파스조차 저것에는 미치지 못하지. 그 강인한 정신을 칭찬하마.<end>"

## 1360460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紹介しよう。<line-break>エンシャントの新皇帝<line-break>ザギヴ・ディンガルだ。<line-break>ディンガル家の正統な後継者であり、<line-break>強大な闇の力を身中に宿しておる。<line-break>闇の世を治めるにふさわしき皇帝よ。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to introduce Zagiv Dyneskal, the new emperor of Ancient. The rightful heir to the Dyneskal line, with a mighty power of darkness dwelling within her. An emperor fit to rule the dark world.<end>"
KO: "소개하지. 엔샨트의 새 황제 자기브 딘갈이다. 딘갈 가문의 정통 후계자이며 강대한 어둠의 힘을 몸 안에 품고 있다. 어둠의 세상을 다스리기에 걸맞은 황제지.<end>"

## 1360461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2あなた、ザギヴ様に何をしたの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>3お主、ザギヴに何をしおった！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4ザギヴ様になんかしたわね。<line-break>いけないじじいですね、まったく。<end><if><value:$01><equal>5てめえ、ザギヴに何かしやがったな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6ザギヴに何をしたの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>7ザギヴほどの魔道士が、<line-break>操られている！？<line-break>貴様、何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>8ザギヴに潜んでいる闇に<line-break>力を与えましたね。<line-break>ひどいことを…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9んー、ザギヴちゃんが、なんか変ね。<line-break>あんた、なんかしたでしょ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12何かに操られているのか？<line-break>爺さん、あんた何をしたんだ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>13あの女、様子が変だ…。<line-break>貴様、何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、ザギヴ、変だよ。<line-break>こら、じじい！　ザギヴに何した！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14お主、ザギヴに何をしたのじゃ！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ザギヴさん…、様子が変だね。<line-break>何かされたのかな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17やーね、まったく、あの女、<line-break>自分を忘れてるじゃない。<line-break>いったいどうなってるのよー！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>、<line-break>ザギヴさん、変だよぉ！<line-break>こら、何をしたの！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20闇に操られているな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26…まずいぞ。<line-break>ソウルが闇に侵されておる。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21闇に操られているな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23闇に操られている…。<line-break>貴様、何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>24ザギヴの体内には魔人が巣くっている。<line-break>…この情報、遺憾ながら<line-break>正しかったようです。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25やっぱり、こうなってたんだ。<line-break>…気をつけて、<value:$28>。<line-break>あれはザギヴの皮を被った魔人よ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27なんという闇の気…！<line-break>いったい彼女になにをしたんだ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>192<value:$28>様…。<line-break>闇の力を感じる…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193なんだか様子がおかしいです。<line-break>ザギヴさんに何をしたんですか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…ゾフォル<line-break>…ついにザギヴを<line-break>闇のあぎとに捕らえたのだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191様子がおかしいわ。<line-break>いったい何をしたの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190様子が変だ。<line-break>お前、ザギヴに何かしたのか。<end>ディンガル新皇帝の誕生かー。<line-break>また、歴史に立ち会っちゃったな。<line-break>て、そんな場合じゃないや。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2What have you done to Lady Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3What have you done to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4You've done something to Lady Zagiv, haven't you? What a bad old man you are.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5You bastard! You've done something to Zagiv!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6What did you do to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7A mage as powerful as Zagiv is being controlled?! What have you done?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8You gave power to the darkness lurking within Zagiv. How cruel...<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Hmm, Zagiv looks a little strange. You did something, didn't you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Is something controlling her? Old man, what did you do?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Something's wrong with that woman... What have you done?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, something's wrong with Zagiv. Hey, old man! What did you do to her?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What have you done to Zagiv?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Something's wrong with Zagiv... I wonder if someone did something to her.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh, honestly! That woman has completely lost herself. What in the world is going on?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, something's wrong with Zagiv! Hey! What did you do to her?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20The darkness is controlling her.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26This is bad. Her Soul is being consumed by darkness.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21The darkness is controlling her.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23The darkness is controlling her... What have you done?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24A demon has been dwelling inside Zagiv. Regrettably, it seems this information was correct.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25So this is what happened after all. Be careful, <value:$28>. That's a demon wearing Zagiv's skin.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27What an aura of darkness... What have you done to her?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192<value:$28>... I sense the power of darkness...<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Something seems wrong. What did you do to Zagiv?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Zofor... So at last you have trapped Zagiv in the jaws of darkness.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Something's wrong with her. What did you do?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Something's wrong. Did you do something to Zagiv?<end>So this is the birth of Dyneskal's new emperor. I've witnessed history again... Wait, this is no time for that.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2당신, 자기브 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3네놈, 자기브에게 무슨 짓을 했느냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4자기브 님에게 무슨 짓 했죠. 못된 영감이네요, 정말.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5네놈, 자기브에게 무슨 짓을 했어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6자기브에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7자기브 같은 마도사가 조종당하고 있어!? 네놈, 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8자기브 안에 숨어 있는 어둠에 힘을 줬군요. 끔찍한 짓을….<end><if><value:$01><equal>9음, 자기브가 좀 이상하네. 너 뭔가 했지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12뭔가에 조종당하는 건가? 영감, 무슨 짓을 한 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13저 여자, 상태가 이상해…. 네놈, 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 자기브가 이상해. 이봐 영감! 자기브에게 무슨 짓 했어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14네놈, 자기브에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15자기브 씨… 상태가 이상해. 뭔가 당한 걸까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17정말, 저 여자 제정신이 아니잖아. 대체 어떻게 된 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>, 자기브 씨가 이상해애! 이봐, 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20어둠에 조종당하고 있군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26…위험하다. 소울이 어둠에 침식되고 있어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21어둠에 조종당하고 있군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23어둠에 조종당하고 있어…. 네놈, 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24자기브의 몸 안에는 마인이 깃들어 있다. …유감스럽지만 이 정보는 사실이었군요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25역시 이렇게 됐구나. …조심해, <value:$28>. 저건 자기브의 껍질을 쓴 마인이야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이 무슨 어둠의 기운이냐…! 대체 그녀에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192<value:$28> 님…. 어둠의 힘이 느껴집니다….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193상태가 이상해요. 자기브 씨에게 무슨 짓을 한 겁니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…조포르. …마침내 자기브를 어둠의 아가리에 붙잡았구나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191상태가 이상해. 대체 무슨 짓을 한 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190상태가 이상해. 너, 자기브에게 뭔가 한 거냐.<end>딩갈 신황제의 탄생인가. 또 역사의 순간에 서게 됐네. …아니, 이럴 때가 아니지.<end>"

## 1360462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう騒ぐでない。これも運命。<line-break>ザギヴに巣くっておった闇が、<line-break>時を得て、甦りつつあるだけのこと。<line-break>ネメアを失い、弱くなった心では、<line-break>闇を抑えきれなかったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Do not make such a fuss. This too is fate. The darkness that dwelt within Zagiv has simply found its hour and begun to awaken. Her heart was weakened by the loss of Nemea and could no longer hold it back.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 소란 피우지 마라. 이것도 운명. 자기브에게 깃들어 있던 어둠이 때를 만나 되살아나고 있을 뿐이다. 네메아를 잃고 약해진 마음으로는 어둠을 억누르지 못한 거지.<end>"

## 1360463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、目覚めよ。<line-break>ザギヴに潜みし闇よ！<end>"
EN: "Now awaken, darkness that lurks within Zagiv!<end>"
KO: "자, 깨어나라. 자기브 안에 숨은 어둠이여!<end>"

## 1360464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。<line-break>ウウ…ア…アア…アアグ…。<line-break>ガアアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Uhh... Ah... Aah... Aaagh... Gaaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "으으…. 우우… 아… 아아… 아악…. 가아아아악!<end>"

## 1360465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！　長かったぜぇ！<line-break>ネメアにやられてから、<line-break>ずっと待ってたんだよ！<line-break>この女の中に潜んで、<line-break>闇の力が強くなるのをよ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! I've waited so long! Ever since Nemea defeated me, I've been hiding inside this woman, waiting for the power of darkness to grow!<end>"
KO: "기히힛! 길었다! 네메아에게 당한 뒤로 계속 기다렸어! 이 여자 안에 숨어서 어둠의 힘이 강해지기만을 말이야!<end>"

## 1360466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついに復活したぜ！<line-break>これで本格的に活動できる！<line-break>ありがとよ。ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>まず手始めに、お前を殺す！<line-break>マゴス様の恐怖のフルコース、<line-break>とくと味わえ！　ギーヒッ！<end>"
EN: "At last, I'm back! Now I can really get to work! Thanks! Geh-heh-heh! And I'll start by killing you! Prepare to savor Lord Magos's full course of terror! Geh-heh!<end>"
KO: "드디어 부활했다! 이제 본격적으로 활동할 수 있어! 고맙다. 기히히힛! 우선 시작으로 네놈을 죽여 주마! 마고스 님의 공포 풀코스, 실컷 맛봐라! 기힛!<end>"

## 1360467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>俺様を殺してどうするつもりなんだ？　<line-break>何も変わりゃ…しないぜ…！<line-break>ザギヴって女も、<line-break>生き返りは…しないぜ。<line-break>クク…。ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! What do you think killing me will accomplish? It won't... change a thing... And that woman Zagiv... won't come back to life. Heh... Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 이 몸을 죽여서 어쩌려고? 아무것도… 변하지 않아…! 자기브라는 여자도 되살아나진… 않아. 크크… 하하하!<end>"

## 1360468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>君の切り札は簡単に<line-break>やられちゃったけど、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Well, old Zofor? Your trump card went down rather easily. What will you do now?<end>"
KO: "자, 조포르 영감. 네 비장의 수가 너무 쉽게 당해 버렸는데, 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1360469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、シャリよ。<line-break>お主らの切り札は残っておろう。<line-break>あとは任せよう。<line-break>わしは、ここらで、<line-break>わしの運命に決着をつけるつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "But Shalli, your trump card still remains. I shall leave the rest to you. It is time I settled matters with my own fate.<end>"
KO: "하지만 샤리여. 너희에겐 아직 비장의 수가 남아 있지 않느냐. 뒤는 맡기마. 나는 여기서 내 운명에 결판을 낼 생각이다.<end>"

## 1360470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。じゃあね、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>あとは、僕と王女様でやるよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Goodbye, old Zofor. The princess and I will handle the rest.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 안녕, 조포르 할아버지. 나머지는 나와 왕녀님이 할게.<end>"

## 1360471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの運命の幕を<line-break>降ろしてもらおうか。<line-break>ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, Nemea... will you bring down the curtain on my fate?<end>"
KO: "자, 내 운명의 막을 내려주겠나. 네메아여.<end>"

## 1360472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ、久しいな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. It has been a long time.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 1360473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ。<line-break>お前の予言が、私の運命を示し、<line-break>私は、その運命と戦ってきた。<end>"
EN: "Zofor. Your prophecy showed me my fate, and I have fought against it ever since.<end>"
KO: "조포르여. 네 예언이 내 운명을 가리켰고, 나는 그 운명과 싸워 왔다.<end>"

## 1360474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃったな。<line-break>じゃが、そろそろ終わりにしよう。<line-break>もう、お主も疲れたろう？<end>"
EN: "So you have. But it is time we brought this to an end. You must be tired by now.<end>"
KO: "그랬지. 하지만 이제 슬슬 끝내자꾸나. 너도 이제 지쳤겠지?<end>"

## 1360475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "受け入れられぬ運命であれば、<line-break>私はこれからも戦い続けるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If fate is unacceptable, then I will go on fighting it.<end>"
KO: "받아들일 수 없는 운명이라면 나는 앞으로도 계속 싸울 것이다.<end>"

## 1360476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうか。<line-break>ならば、最後の予言を残そうか。<line-break>それは…、<line-break>お主らの死じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Is that so? Then I shall leave you with one final prophecy: your deaths!<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그런가. 그럼 마지막 예언을 남기지. 그것은… 너희의 죽음이다!<end>"

## 1360477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、これで眠れる…。<line-break>不吉な予知を得ることも、<line-break>もうあるまいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Now I can sleep. No more ominous premonitions shall come to me.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 이걸로 잠들 수 있겠군…. 불길한 예지를 얻는 일도 이제는 없겠지.<end>"

## 1360478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>お前は最後に何を見たのだ？<line-break>我々の死では、あるまい。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What did you see at the end? It was not our deaths, was it?<end>"
KO: "조포르…. 너는 마지막에 무엇을 본 것이냐? 우리의 죽음은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1360479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "후후후….<end>"

## 1360480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ぐふっ。ムダだ…。<line-break>運命は…、変わらぬ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Gah! It is useless... Fate... does not change...<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 크흑. 소용없다…. 운명은… 바뀌지 않는다….<end>"

## 1360481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>進むことしか、私たちには、できぬ。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리에겐 나아가는 수밖에 없다.<end>"

## 1360482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た。<line-break>だが、徒労だったな。<line-break>ここには闇の王女はおらぬ。<line-break>傷を癒すべく、地上で最も闇の深い場所<line-break>かつて魔王バロルがいた恐怖の城、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かった。<end>"
EN: "You have come far, but it was all in vain. The Princess of Darkness is not here. To heal her wounds, she has gone to the darkest place on earth: the throne of the Ruined Castle, the castle of terror where Balor, Demon King, once dwelt.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다. 하지만 헛걸음했군. 여기에 어둠의 공주는 없다. 상처를 치유하기 위해 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳, 한때 마왕 발로르가 있던 공포의 성, 폐성의 옥좌로 향했다.<end>"

## 1360483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、すべては徒労に終わる。<line-break>貴様は破壊神の円卓騎士サムスンと<line-break>このザハクによって、ここで死ぬのだ。<end>"
EN: "And all your efforts shall end in vain. Here, Samson, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction, and I, Zahak, will kill you.<end>"
KO: "그리고 모든 것은 헛수고로 끝난다. 네놈은 파괴신의 원탁기사 삼슨과 이 자하크에게 여기서 죽는 것이다.<end>"

## 1360484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？<line-break>一度倒したはずだとでも言いたそうだな。<line-break>我らは魔人なれば不死身だ。<line-break>愚かな人間の召喚で何度でも具現する。<line-break>徒労だったな。<end>"
EN: "What is it? You look as though you mean to say you defeated us once before. We demons are immortal. Whenever foolish humans summon us, we take form anew. Your efforts were in vain.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지? 한 번 쓰러뜨렸다고 말하고 싶은 모양이군. 우리는 마인이니 불사신이다. 어리석은 인간의 소환으로 몇 번이고 현현하지. 헛수고였다.<end>"

## 1360485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急ごう。<line-break>我はすべてに徒労を教えねばならぬ。<line-break>すべては徒労だと教えねばならぬ。<line-break>すべてに例外なく教えねばならぬ。<line-break>思い上がった虚無の子や闇の王女、<line-break>神、そして無限のソウルにもな。<end>"
EN: "We must hurry. I must teach all things that effort is futile. I must teach them that every endeavor is in vain. Nothing shall be exempt: not the presumptuous Child of the Void, the Princess of Darkness, the god, or even the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "서두르자. 나는 모든 것에 헛수고를 가르쳐야 한다. 모든 것은 헛수고라고, 예외 없이 모두에게 가르쳐야 한다. 오만한 허무의 아이와 어둠의 왕녀, 신, 그리고 무한의 소울에게도.<end>"

## 1360486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世にあるものは絶望。<line-break>すべての営みは徒労。<line-break>貴様らにも死をもって教えてやろう。<line-break>我こそは破壊のあとに来たるべき虚無、<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士、<line-break>一切の営みをあざ笑うものザハク！<end>"
EN: "All that exists is despair. Every endeavor is in vain. I shall teach you this through death. I am the Void that must follow destruction, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction―Zahak, who mocks all endeavor!<end>"
KO: "세상에 있는 것은 절망. 모든 행위는 헛수고. 너희에게도 죽음으로 가르쳐 주마. 나는 파괴 뒤에 와야 할 허무, 파괴신의 원탁기사, 모든 행위를 비웃는 자 자하크다!<end>"

## 1360487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚者の頑迷さで絶望を否定する…か。<line-break>笑止だ。虚無は常に貴様らのそばにある。<line-break>すべては…徒労だ…。<line-break>しばらく眠らせてもら…おう…。<end>"
EN: "You deny despair with the obstinacy of fools...? Absurd. The Void is always at your side. All is... in vain... I shall sleep... for a while...<end>"
KO: "어리석은 자의 완고함으로 절망을 부정한다…인가. 우습군. 허무는 언제나 네놈들 곁에 있다. 모든 것은… 헛수고다…. 잠시 잠들도록… 하지….<end>"

## 1360488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>進むことしか、私たちには、できぬ。<end>"
EN: "...Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"
KO: "………………. 가자, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리에게는 나아가는 것밖에 할 수 없다.<end>"

## 1360489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やるのう<line-break>円卓騎士サムスンとザハクを<line-break>かたづけるとはな<end>"
EN: "Impressive. You managed to dispose of Samson and Zahak, Knights of the Round Table.<end>"
KO: "제법이구나. 원탁기사 삼손과 자하크를 쓰러뜨리다니.<end>"

## 1360490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの運命の幕を<line-break>降ろしてもらおうか。<line-break>ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, Nemea... will you bring down the curtain on my fate?<end>"
KO: "자, 내 운명의 막을 내려주겠나. 네메아여.<end>"

## 1360491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ、久しいな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. It has been a long time.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 1360492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ、<line-break>お前の予言が、私の運命を示し、<line-break>私は、その運命と戦ってきた。<end>"
EN: "Zofor. Your prophecy showed me my fate, and I have fought against it ever since.<end>"
KO: "조포르여, 네 예언은 내 운명을 보여 주었고 나는 그 운명과 싸워 왔다.<end>"

## 1360493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃったな。<line-break>じゃが、そろそろ終わりにしよう<line-break>もう、お主も疲れたろう？<end>"
EN: "So you have. But it is time we brought this to an end. You must be tired by now.<end>"
KO: "그랬지. 하지만 이제 슬슬 끝내자꾸나. 너도 이제 지쳤겠지?<end>"

## 1360494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "受け入れられぬ運命であれば、私はこれからも戦い続けるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If fate is unacceptable, then I will go on fighting it.<end>"
KO: "받아들일 수 없는 운명이라면 나는 앞으로도 계속 싸울 것이다.<end>"

## 1360495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうか。<line-break>ならば、最後の予言を残そうか。<line-break>それは…、<line-break>お主らの死！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Is that so? Then I shall leave you with one final prophecy: your deaths!<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그런가. 그렇다면 마지막 예언을 남기지. 그것은… 너희의 죽음!<end>"

## 1360496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、これで眠れる…。<line-break>不吉な予言を見ることも、<line-break>もうあるまいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Now I can sleep. No more ominous visions shall come to me.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 이제 잠들 수 있겠군…. 불길한 예언을 보는 일도 이제 없겠지.<end>"

## 1360497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>お前は最後に何を見たのだ？<line-break>我々の死では、あるまい。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What did you see at the end? It was not our deaths, was it?<end>"
KO: "조포르…. 너는 마지막에 무엇을 본 것이냐? 우리의 죽음은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1360498
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "후후후….<end>"

## 1360501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷というところで<line-break>呼び出してくれるのか。<line-break>わかった。<end>"
EN: "You'll summon me at a place called Cat Manor? Very well.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 불러주는 건가. 알겠다.<end>"

## 1360502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かと思えば…<line-break>アスティアの子ではないか。<line-break>そうか、今回の茶番の主役は貴様か。<line-break>なるほど、貴様なら<line-break>この永遠に繰り返される輪廻に<line-break>終止符を打ってくれるのかもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't Astia's child. So you're the protagonist of this farce. Perhaps you really might bring this eternally recurring cycle to an end.<end>"
KO: "누군가 했더니… 아스티아의 아이가 아닌가. 그렇군, 이번 희극의 주역은 네놈인가. 과연 네놈이라면 이 영원히 반복되는 윤회에 종지부를 찍어 줄지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1360503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、素晴らしい力だ。<line-break>アスティアはその力に何かを<line-break>感じ取ったのか…。<line-break>だが、私の存在を消すには<line-break>どうにも力不足のようだ。<end>"
EN: "I see. Magnificent power. Did Astia sense something within it...? But it seems you still lack the power to erase my existence.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 훌륭한 힘이다. 아스티아는 그 힘에서 무언가를 느꼈나…. 하지만 내 존재를 지우기엔 아무래도 힘이 부족한 모양이군.<end>"

## 1360504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これでは母の仇は討てぬ。<line-break>この程度の存在に何ができると<line-break>いうのだ？　アスティアよ。<end>"
EN: "You cannot avenge your mother like this. What could a being as feeble as this possibly accomplish? Astia...<end>"
KO: "…이래서는 어머니의 원수를 갚을 수 없어. 이 정도 존재가 무얼 할 수 있다는 거지? 아스티아여.<end>"

## 1360505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだろう…<line-break>アスティアの子よ？<end>"
EN: "Is that not so... child of Astia?<end>"
KO: "그렇겠지… 아스티아의 아이여?<end>"

## 1360506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は使命を優先させた。<line-break>この女を倒さねば私に挑むことは<line-break>できなかっただろう。<line-break>だが、それは正しかったのか？<line-break>憎しみに憎しみを重ねて得られるのは<line-break>死だけではないのか？<end>"
EN: "You placed your mission above all else. You could not have challenged me without first defeating this woman. But was that truly right? If hatred is piled upon hatred, is death not all that remains?<end>"
KO: "너는 사명을 우선했다. 이 여자를 쓰러뜨리지 않으면 나에게 도전할 수 없었겠지. 하지만 그게 옳았나? 증오 위에 증오를 쌓아 얻는 건 죽음뿐 아닌가?<end>"

## 1360507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれにせよ戦わねばならないようだ。<line-break>さあ、挑むがよい。<line-break>そのためにここまで来たのだろう？<end>"
EN: "In any case, it seems we must fight. Come, challenge me. That is why you came all this way, is it not?<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 싸워야 할 모양이군. 자, 덤벼라. 그러려고 여기까지 온 것 아니냐?<end>"

## 1360508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？　何を驚く？<line-break>我ら円卓の騎士は不滅の存在。<line-break>人の力で我らを滅することはできぬ。<line-break>我らに永久の眠りをもたらすことが<line-break>できるのはウルグ様のみであろう。<end>"
EN: "What is it? Why are you surprised? We Knights of the Round Table are immortal. No human power can destroy us. Only Lord Ulugh can grant us eternal Sleep.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지? 무엇에 놀라나? 우리 원탁기사는 불멸의 존재. 인간의 힘으로 우리를 없앨 수 없다. 우리에게 영원한 잠을 줄 수 있는 분은 울그 님뿐이겠지.<end>"

KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは！<line-break><value:$28>さんの姿が見えたので<line-break>走ってきてしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Hello! I saw you, <value:$28>, so I came running.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요! <value:$28>님이 보여서 달려왔어요.<end>"

## 1360512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0もう、ノエルったら<line-break>ひとりで走っていかないでよね。　<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、ボウヤがいたんじゃ<line-break>仕方ないかしらね？<line-break>ノエルはボウヤにぞっこんだから。<end>もう、ノエルったら<line-break>ひとりで走っていかないでよね。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、子猫ちゃんがいたんじゃ<line-break>仕方ないかしらね？<line-break>ノエルは子猫ちゃんにぞっこんだから。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Honestly, Noel, don't go running off by yourself. Well, with the boy here, I suppose you couldn't help yourself, could you? Noel's completely smitten with the boy.<end>Honestly, Noel, don't go running off by yourself. Well, with the little kitten here, I suppose you couldn't help yourself, could you? Noel's completely smitten with the little kitten.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0정말, 노엘도 참. 혼자 뛰어가지 말라니까. 뭐, 꼬마가 있었으니 어쩔 수 없었으려나? 노엘은 꼬마한테 푹 빠졌으니까.<end>정말, 노엘도 참. 혼자 뛰어가지 말라니까. 뭐, 아기고양이가 있었으니 어쩔 수 없었으려나? 노엘은 아기고양이한테 푹 빠졌으니까.<end>"

## 1360513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと、カフィン…！<end>"
EN: "C-Coffin, wait...!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐, 카핀…!<end>"

## 1360515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ウフフ…あたしもボウヤに　　　　<line-break>この前のお礼をしなきゃ<line-break>いけないんだったわね。<line-break>さぁて、どんなお礼がいいかしら？<line-break>ねぇ？<end>そうだ。あたしも子猫ちゃんに　　<line-break>この前のお礼をしなきゃ<line-break>いけないんだったわね。<line-break>さぁて、どんなお礼がいいかしら？<line-break>ねぇ？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hee hee... I still owe you a proper thank-you for the other day, boy. Now, how should I thank you? Hmm?<end>That's right. I still owe you a proper thank-you for the other day, little kitten. Now, how should I thank you? Hmm?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0후후… 나도 꼬마한테 지난번 답례를 해야 했지. 자, 어떤 답례가 좋으려나? 응?<end>그래. 나도 아기고양이한테 지난번 답례를 해야 했지. 자, 어떤 답례가 좋으려나? 응?<end>"

## 1360516

## 1360517

## 1360519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360520

## 1360521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりだね、死の羽音。<line-break>いや、今はレイヴンと<line-break>呼んだ方がいいかな？<end>"
EN: "It's been a long time, Death's Wingbeat. Or should I call you Raven now?<end>"
KO: "오랜만이군, 죽음의 날갯소리. 아니, 지금은 레이븐이라고 부르는 게 좋을까?<end>"

## 1360522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しのはおと、ですって？<line-break>…どういうこと？<end>"
EN: "Death's Wingbeat? What does that mean?<end>"
KO: "'시노하오토'라고? …무슨 뜻이지?<end>"

## 1360523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、君の後ろにいる<line-break>男はね、かつて…<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You see, the man behind you was once...<end>"
KO: "후후, 네 뒤에 있는 남자는 말이지, 예전에….<end>"

## 1360524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その男は、施文院の暗殺組織<line-break>告死天使の一員だったんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それも死の羽音と呼ばれる<line-break>屈指の暗殺者…<line-break>…いや、殺人機械だった。<end>"
EN: "That man was once a member of the Angels of Death, the Inscription Order's assassination organization. More than that, he was one of the foremost assassins, known as Death's Wingbeat... No, a killing machine.<end>"
KO: "그 남자는 시문원의 암살 조직 고사천사의 일원이었어. 그것도 죽음의 날갯소리라 불리던 손꼽히는 암살자… 아니, 살인 기계였지.<end>"

## 1360525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、彼は任務に失敗した。<line-break>殺人機械のはずの彼が、おびえという<line-break>不要な感情を持ってしまってね。<line-break>ほら、今も後ろでふるえている。<line-break>無様なものだね。フフフ。<line-break>こうなっては殺人機械も形なしだ。<end>"
EN: "But he failed his mission. A killing machine, and yet he developed the useless emotion of fear. Look at him, trembling back there even now. What a pathetic sight. Heh heh heh. So much for the killing machine.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그는 임무에 실패했지. 살인 기계여야 할 그가 두려움이라는 쓸데없는 감정을 품어 버렸거든. 봐, 지금도 뒤에서 떨고 있잖아. 참 꼴사납군. 후후후. 이래서는 살인 기계라는 이름도 무색하지.<end>"

## 1360526
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、君たちも<line-break>彼の正体がわかったからには、<line-break>放っておくわけにはいかないだろう？<line-break>彼を渡してくれないか？<line-break>処分したいのでね。<end>"
EN: "Now that you know what he really is, you can hardly leave matters as they are, can you? Would you hand him over? I'd like to dispose of him.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 너희도 그의 정체를 알았으니 그냥 둘 순 없겠지? 그를 넘겨주지 않겠나? 처리하고 싶어서 말이야.<end>"

## 1360527
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスを阻止する<end>"
EN: "Stop Elfas<end>"
KO: "엘파스를 저지한다<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360529
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンを<line-break>あなたに渡すわけにはいきません！<end>"

KO: "레이븐을 당신에게 넘길 수는 없습니다!<end>"

## 1360530
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ノ、ノエル…。<line-break>やめろ、君のかなう相手では…。<end>"
EN: "N-Noel... Don't. You can't defeat him...<end>"
KO: "…노, 노엘…. 그만둬, 네가 당해낼 상대가 아니야….<end>"

## 1360531
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンは、<line-break>わたしの大切な仲間です！<line-break>あなたの好きにはさせません！<line-break>あなたが<line-break>どうしてもと言うのなら…！<end>"
EN: "Raven is a dear friend and companion! I won't let you do as you please with him! If you insist...!<end>"
KO: "레이븐은 제 소중한 동료예요! 당신 마음대로 하게 두지 않겠습니다! 그래도 굳이 하겠다면…!<end>"

## 1360532
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…。<end>"
EN: "Noel...<end>"
KO: "노엘….<end>"

## 1360533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。無限のソウルを持つ者を<line-break>ふたり同時に相手にしてまで<line-break>処分する価値は、彼にはないよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. He isn't worth disposing of if it means facing two Bearers of the Infinite Soul at once.<end>"
KO: "후후. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자 둘을 동시에 상대하면서까지 처리할 가치는 그에게 없어.<end>"

## 1360534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴンをものみたいに言わないで！<line-break>彼には手を出させません！<end>"
EN: "Don't talk about Raven as if he were a thing! I won't let you touch him!<end>"
KO: "레이븐을 물건처럼 말하지 마! 그에게 손대게 두지 않겠습니다!<end>"

## 1360535
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…彼には手を出さないで、か…。　　<line-break>フフ、姉さんもそんなことを<line-break>言っていたかな…。<end>"
EN: "“Don't touch him,” you say... Heh heh. I seem to recall my sister saying the same thing once...<end>"
KO: "…그에게는 손대지 말라, 인가…. 후후, 누나도 그런 말을 했던가….<end>"

## 1360536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくよく見れば…<line-break>君のパーティは、君にお似合いの　<line-break>面白い顔ぶればかりだね。<line-break>世界の牧者をあがめる狂信者に、<line-break>迫害で死に損なった吸血鬼の女まで<line-break>いるのか…。<end>"
EN: "Now that I look more closely... your party is quite a curious assortment, perfectly suited to you. A fanatic who worships the Shepherd of the World, and even a female vampire who only just survived persecution...<end>"
KO: "자세히 보니… 네 파티는 너와 잘 어울리는 재미있는 얼굴들뿐이네. 세계의 목자를 떠받드는 광신도에, 박해받다 죽다 살아난 흡혈귀 여자까지 있나….<end>"

## 1360537
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml

KO: "네놈….<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "나셰스!<end>"

## 1360539
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日のところは失礼させてもらうよ。<line-break>だが、死の羽音よ、やすらぎに<line-break>ひたる権利が、君にはあるのかな？<end>"
EN: "I'll take my leave for now. But tell me, Death's Wingbeat: do you truly have the right to enjoy such peace?<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이만 실례하지. 하지만 죽음의 날갯소리여, 평온에 잠길 권리가 과연 네게 있을까?<end>"

## 1360540
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…彼の言うとおりさ。<end>"
EN: "He's right...<end>"
KO: "…그가 말한 대로야.<end>"

## 1360541

KO: "어?<end>"

## 1360542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺には…殺人機械だった俺には<line-break>安らぎにひたる権利なんて<line-break>…ない。<end>"
EN: "I was a killing machine. I have no right... to live in peace.<end>"
KO: "나에게는… 살인기계였던 나에게는 평온에 잠길 권리 같은 건… 없어.<end>"

## 1360543
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、カフィン…。<line-break>世話になった。<line-break>許してくれとは言わない。<line-break>これで、告死天使たちが<line-break>君たちの前に現れることは<line-break>なくなるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Noel, Coffin... Thank you for everything. I won't ask you to forgive me. This should keep the Angels of Death from appearing before you again.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 카핀… 신세 졌다. 용서해 달라는 말은 하지 않겠다. 이제 고사천사들이 너희 앞에 나타나는 일은 없을 거다.<end>"

## 1360544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って！　待ってください！<end>"
EN: "Wait! Please, wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 기다려 주세요!<end>"

## 1360545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしたちには…わたしには<line-break>レイヴンの力が必要なんです！<line-break>わたしたち、仲間なんですから！　　<line-break>昔のレイヴンが何をしていたのか、<line-break>わたしは知りません！　でも、<line-break>わたしには今のレイヴンが必要なの！<end>"
EN: "We... I need your strength, Raven! We're companions! I don't know what you did in the past! But I need the Raven you are now!<end>"
KO: "우리에게는… 나에게는 레이븐의 힘이 필요해요! 우리는 동료잖아요! 예전의 레이븐이 무슨 일을 했는지 저는 몰라요! 하지만 지금의 레이븐은 제게 필요해요!<end>"

## 1360546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメだ。俺がいると…<line-break>君に迷惑がかかる。<end>"

KO: "안 돼. 내가 있으면… 네게 폐를 끼쳐.<end>"

## 1360547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴン！<end>"
EN: "Raven!<end>"
KO: "레이븐!<end>"

## 1360548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ここまで<line-break>ノエルが言っているんですもの。<line-break>　<line-break>それでも出ていくって言うなら<line-break>あたしも黙っていないわ。<end>"
EN: "After everything Noel has said, if you still mean to leave, I won't stand by and let you.<end>"

## 1360549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このパーティのリーダーはお前だ。<line-break>お前がそれでいいというのなら<line-break>私はかまわんよ。<end>"
EN: "You're the leader of this party. If you're willing to have him stay, I have no objection.<end>"
KO: "이 파티의 리더는 너다. 네가 그걸로 괜찮다면 나는 상관없어.<end>"

## 1360550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです、レイヴン。<line-break>行かないで。<end>"
EN: "Please, Raven. Don't go.<end>"
KO: "부탁이야, 레이븐. 가지 마.<end>"

## 1360551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとう。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君が俺を必要とする限り、<line-break>俺は君のそばに…いるよ。<end>"
EN: "...Thank you. As long as you need me, I'll... stay by your side.<end>"
KO: "…고마워. 네가 날 필요로 하는 한, 난 네 곁에… 있을게.<end>"

## 1360552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　もしかして、<line-break>君は僕の邪魔をするのかい？<line-break>彼は暗殺者なんだよ？<end>"
EN: "Hmm? Are you going to stand in my way? He is an assassin, you know.<end>"
KO: "응? 혹시 네가 나를 방해하려는 거야? 그는 암살자라고?<end>"

## 1360553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "使命を優先させるなら、<line-break>この者はお前と戦い、<line-break>勝利せねばならなかった。<end>"
EN: "Had this person placed the mission first, fighting and defeating you would have been necessary.<end>"
KO: "사명을 우선한다면 이 자는 너와 싸워 이겨야 했다.<end>"

## 1360554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きるとは意志すること。<line-break>命とは意志して動くこと。<line-break>価値、そして夢とはその道標を持つこと。<line-break>それ以上ではない。<line-break>価値や夢がとかく独り歩きしがちだが<line-break>この者は意志することを優先させたのだ。<end>"
EN: "To live is to exert one's will. Life is will set in motion. Values and dreams serve as guideposts, nothing more. Values and dreams often take on lives of their own, but this person placed personal will first.<end>"
KO: "산다는 것은 의지를 갖는 것. 생명이란 의지를 갖고 움직이는 것. 가치와 꿈은 그 길잡이를 갖는 것. 그 이상이 아니다. 가치나 꿈이 흔히 독주하지만 이 사람은 의지를 갖는 것을 우선했다.<end>"

## 1360555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意志すること…生きることを…。<end>"
EN: "To exercise the will... to live...<end>"
KO: "의지를 갖는 것… 살아가는 것을….<end>"

## 1360556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな女よ、お前の答えはそこにある。<line-break>そしてお前はそれを絶対に理解できない。<line-break>だが、感じることくらいならできよう。<line-break>感じるためにすべきことは<line-break>自分で考えるがいい。<end>"
EN: "Foolish woman, your answer lies there. You will never be able to understand it. But perhaps you can at least feel it. Decide for yourself what you must do to feel it.<end>"
KO: "어리석은 여자여, 네 답은 바로 거기에 있다. 그리고 넌 절대로 그것을 이해하지 못할 것이다. 다만 느끼는 정도라면 가능하겠지. 느끼기 위해 무엇을 해야 할지는 스스로 생각해라.<end>"

## 1360557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアの子よ。<line-break>母の仇を討つのではなかったのか？<end>"
EN: "Child of Astia. Were you not going to avenge your mother?<end>"
KO: "아스티아의 아이여. 어머니의 원수를 갚으려던 게 아니었나?<end>"

## 1360558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "憎しみを捨て、この者の未来を選ぶ。<line-break>それがお前の進む道なのか？<line-break>フッ…<line-break>なるほど…お前の未来は見えぬな。<line-break>私には想像もつかぬ道ゆえにな。<end>"
EN: "You cast aside hatred and choose this person's future. Is that the path you mean to follow? Heh... I see. No wonder I cannot see your future. It lies along a path beyond even my imagining.<end>"
KO: "증오를 버리고 이자의 미래를 선택한다. 그것이 네가 나아갈 길인가? 훗… 과연 네 미래는 보이지 않는군. 나로서는 상상도 못 할 길이니까.<end>"

## 1360559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、行くがよい。<line-break>私は闇の門でお前を待とう。<line-break>お前の未来と私の未来が交わる時…<line-break>全ての決着がつくであろう。<end>"
EN: "Go, then. I shall await you at the Dark Gate. When your future and mine intersect... all shall be decided.<end>"
KO: "자, 가거라. 나는 어둠의 문에서 너를 기다리겠다. 네 미래와 내 미래가 만나는 순간… 모든 결판이 날 것이다.<end>"

EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、相手が<line-break>イシュバアルだったとすれば、<line-break> <line-break>さしもの<value:$28>殿も<line-break>さぞ危ない目にあわれたはず。<end>"

## 1360566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml

## 1360570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほ、本当ですか、<value:$28>殿！<line-break>では、気がかわらぬうちに、<line-break>さっそく、神殿で婚約の儀を！<end>"
EN: "R-Really, <value:$28>? Then, before you change your mind, let us hold the betrothal ceremony at the temple at once!<end>"
KO: "저, 정말입니까, <value:$28>님! 그럼 마음이 바뀌기 전에 당장 신전에서 약혼식을!<end>"

## 1360571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのー、神官さん。<end>"
EN: "Um, Priest...<end>"
KO: "저기요, 신관님.<end>"

## 1360572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういえば、忘れていた。<line-break>ご主人にはぜひ仲人として<line-break>立ち会っていただきたい！<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, I forgot. I would very much like you to attend as matchmaker!<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 잊고 있었군. 주인께서는 꼭 중매인으로 입회해 주셨으면 합니다!<end>"

## 1360573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、そうじゃなくってよ。<line-break>その…言いづれえが、俺は反対だぜ。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>が冒険者じゃ<line-break>なくなっちまうってのは、<line-break>こっちとしても大きな損失だ。<end>"
EN: "No, that's not it. Um... I hate to say it, but I'm against this. Having <value:$28> give up being an adventurer would be a great loss for us, too.<end>"
KO: "아니, 그런 게 아니라. 그… 말하기 어렵지만 난 반대야. <value:$28>이 모험자가 아니게 되는 건 우리에게도 큰 손실이야.<end>"

## 1360574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "特に、こんな荒れたご時世、<line-break><value:$28>のような大冒険者の力は<line-break>どうしても必要になる。<line-break>わかってくれ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Especially in troubled times like these, we need the strength of a great adventurer like <value:$28>. Please understand, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "특히 이런 어수선한 시대에는 <value:$28> 같은 대모험자의 힘이 꼭 필요하다. 이해해 줘, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1360575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど…言われてみれば、<line-break>そのとおりだ。ならばせめて、<line-break>これをお持ちください。<end>"
EN: "I see... Now that you put it that way, you are right. Then please, at least take this.<end>"
KO: "그렇군… 듣고 보니 네 말이 맞다. 그렇다면 적어도 이것만은 받아 주게.<end>"

## 1360576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの鉱山で鉱夫をしていた<line-break>私の先祖が伝えた品です。<end>"
EN: "This was passed down through my family by an ancestor who worked in that mine.<end>"
KO: "그 광산에서 광부였던 제 조상이 전해 준 물건입니다.<end>"

## 1360577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを持っていれば、<line-break>崩落から身を守ると同時、<line-break> <line-break>的確に鉱脈を掘り当てることが<line-break>できたといわれております。<line-break>きっと、お役に立つでしょう。<end>"
EN: "It is said to protect its bearer from cave-ins while also leading them unerringly to ore veins. I am sure it will be of use to you.<end>"
KO: "그걸 가지고 있으면 붕괴에서 몸을 지키는 동시에 정확하게 광맥을 찾아낼 수 있었다고 합니다. 분명 도움이 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 1360578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うはは、困ってるぜ、神官さん。<end>"
EN: "Hahaha, this is awkward, Priest.<end>"
KO: "우하하, 곤란해 보이는데, 신관 양반.<end>"

## 1360579
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、ううむ…そうですか。<line-break>気立てのいい子なのですが…。<line-break> <line-break>では、せめてこれを<line-break>持っていってください。<end>"
EN: "I-I see... She is such a good girl, though... Then please, at least take this with you.<end>"
KO: "으, 음… 그렇습니까. 마음씨 좋은 아이인데…. 그럼 적어도 이것만은 받아 가 주세요.<end>"

## 1360580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの鉱山で鉱夫をしていた<line-break>私の先祖が伝えた品です。<end>"
EN: "This was passed down through my family by an ancestor who worked in that mine.<end>"
KO: "그 광산에서 광부였던 제 조상이 전해 준 물건입니다.<end>"

## 1360581
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを持っていれば、<line-break>崩落から身を守ることができたと<line-break>いわれております。<line-break>きっと、お役に立つでしょう。<end>"
EN: "It is said to protect its bearer from cave-ins. I am sure it will be of use to you.<end>"
KO: "그걸 가지고 있으면 붕괴에서 몸을 지킬 수 있었다고 합니다. 분명 도움이 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 1360582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで安心して暮らせます。<line-break>アルノートゥンにも活気が戻るはず。<line-break>恩にきます、<value:$28>殿。<end>"
EN: "Now we can live without fear. Alnortun should regain its vitality as well. We are in your debt, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이제 안심하고 살 수 있겠습니다. 알노툰에도 활기가 돌아오겠지요. 은혜를 입었습니다, <value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1360587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360588

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1360589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、この程度か。<line-break>私をこの永遠に続く茶番から<line-break>解放してくれるには力が足りぬな。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Is that all? You lack the power to free me from this eternal farce.<end>"
KO: "후, 이 정도인가. 나를 이 영원한 희극에서 해방하기에는 힘이 부족하군.<end>"

## 1360590
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 1360591
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来てくれたのですね。<end>"
EN: "You came.<end>"
KO: "와 주셨군요.<end>"

## 1360592
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は巫女として、ずっと、<line-break>このウルカーンから出ずに<line-break>生きてきました…。<end>"
EN: "As a priestess, I have lived my entire life here, never once leaving Ulkarne.<end>"
KO: "저는 무녀로서 줄곧 이 울칸을 떠나지 않고 살아왔습니다….<end>"

## 1360593
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にとっての世界はここだけ…<line-break><value:$28>様の世界は<line-break>もっと大きいのでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "This is the only world I have ever known. Yours must be so much larger, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "제게 세계는 여기뿐…. <value:$28>님의 세계는 더 넓겠지요.<end>"

## 1360594
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>少しだけ、<line-break>うらやましいような気分です。<end>"
EN: "I see... I think I envy you a little.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요… 조금은 부러운 기분입니다.<end>"

## 1360595
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　私も…？<line-break>…いいえ。<line-break>私の身体は旅に耐えられないでしょう。<line-break>…そんな顔しないでください。<line-break><value:$28>様が訪ねてくださる<line-break>だけで、私は十分なのですから。<end>"
EN: "What? Me too...? No. I do not think my body could withstand the journey. Please, do not look like that. Just having you come to visit is more than enough for me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "네? 저도…? …아니요. 제 몸은 여행을 견디지 못할 겁니다. …그런 표정 짓지 마세요. <value:$28> 님이 찾아와 주시는 것만으로도 저는 충분하니까요.<end>"

## 1360597
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世に存在しているということが、<line-break>天空神のお許しを<line-break>得たということならば…<end>"
EN: "If existing in this world means I have received the Sky God's permission...<end>"
KO: "이 세상에 존재하고 있다는 것이 천공신의 허락을 받았다는 뜻이라면…<end>"

## 1360598
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>不適切なことを言いました。<end>"
EN: "...I have said something inappropriate.<end>"
KO: "………………. 부적절한 말을 했습니다.<end>"

## 1360599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "存在していることを考えるならば、<line-break>悪もまた神に許された存在ということに<line-break>なってしまいます…。<line-break>もっとも、私にとっては、<line-break>善悪など、まだまだ<line-break>遠い世界のことなのですが…。<end>"
EN: "If that is so, then evil, too, must be permitted by God to exist... Of course, questions of good and evil are still far beyond me...<end>"
KO: "존재한다는 점을 생각하면 악 또한 신에게 허락된 존재라는 뜻이 되어 버립니다…. 물론 저에게는 선악 같은 것은 아직 너무 먼 세계의 이야기지만요….<end>"

## 1360600
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、紹介が遅れたが、<line-break>エンシャントの新皇帝<line-break>ザギヴ・ディンガルだ。<line-break>ディンガル家の正統な後継者であり、<line-break>強大な闇の力を身中に宿しておる。<line-break>闇の世を治めるにふさわしき皇帝よ。<end>"
EN: "I should have made the introductions sooner. This is Zagiv Dyneskal, the new emperor of Ancient: the rightful heir to the Dyneskal line, with a mighty power of darkness dwelling within her. An emperor fit to rule the dark world.<end>"
KO: "자, 소개가 늦었군. 엔샨트의 새 황제 자기브 딩갈이다. 딩갈가의 정통 후계자이며 몸속에 강대한 어둠의 힘을 품고 있지. 어둠의 세상을 다스리기에 걸맞은 황제다.<end>"

## 1360601
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう騒ぐでない。これも運命。<line-break>ザギヴに巣くっておった闇が、<line-break>時を得て、甦りつつあるだけのこと。<line-break>ネメアを失い、弱くなった心では、<line-break>闇を抑えきれなかったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Do not make such a fuss. This too is fate. The darkness that dwelt within Zagiv has simply found its hour and begun to awaken. Her heart was weakened by the loss of Nemea, and could no longer hold it back.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 소란 피우지 마라. 이것도 운명. 자기브에게 깃들어 있던 어둠이 때를 만나 되살아나고 있을 뿐이다. 네메아를 잃고 약해진 마음으로는 어둠을 억누르지 못한 거지.<end>"

## 1360602
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、目覚めよ。<line-break>ザギヴに潜みし闇よ！<end>"
EN: "Now awaken, darkness that lurks within Zagiv!<end>"
KO: "자, 깨어나라. 자기브 안에 숨은 어둠이여!<end>"

## 1360603
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。<line-break>ウウ…ア…アア…アアグ…。<line-break>ガアアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Uhh... Ah... Aah... Aaagh... Gaaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "으으…. 우우… 아… 아아… 아악…. 가아아아악!<end>"

## 1360604
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！　長かったぜぇ！<line-break>ネメアにやられてから、<line-break>ずっと待ってたんだよ！<line-break>この女の中に潜んで、<line-break>闇の力が強くなるのをよ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! I've waited so long! Ever since Nemea defeated me, I've been hiding inside this woman, waiting for the power of darkness to grow!<end>"
KO: "기히힛! 길었다! 네메아에게 당한 뒤로 계속 기다렸어! 이 여자 안에 숨어서 어둠의 힘이 강해지기만을 말이야!<end>"

## 1360605
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついに復活したぜ！<line-break>これで本格的に活動できる！<line-break>ありがとよ。ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>まず手始めに、お前を殺す！<line-break>マゴス様の恐怖のフルコース、<line-break>とくと味わえ！　ギーヒッ！<end>"
EN: "At last, I'm back! Now I can really get to work! Thanks! Geh-heh-heh! And I'll start by killing you! Prepare to savor Lord Magos's full course of terror! Geh-heh!<end>"
KO: "드디어 부활했다! 이제 본격적으로 활동할 수 있어! 고맙다. 기히히힛! 우선 시작으로 네놈을 죽여 주마! 마고스 님의 공포 풀코스, 실컷 맛봐라! 기힛!<end>"

## 1360606
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>俺様を殺してどうするつもりなんだ？　<line-break>何も変わりゃ…しないぜ…！<line-break>ザギヴって女も、<line-break>生き返りは…しないぜ。<line-break>クク…。ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! What do you think killing me will accomplish? It won't... change a thing... And that woman Zagiv... won't come back to life. Heh... Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 이 몸을 죽여서 어쩌려고? 아무것도… 변하지 않아…! 자기브라는 여자도 되살아나진… 않아. 크크… 하하하!<end>"

## 1360607
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>君の切り札は簡単に<line-break>やられちゃったけど、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Well, old Zofor? Your trump card went down rather easily. What will you do now?<end>"
KO: "자, 조포르 영감. 네 비장의 수가 너무 쉽게 당해 버렸는데, 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1360608
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、シャリよ。<line-break>お主らの切り札は残っておろう。<line-break>あとは任せよう。<line-break>わしは、ここらで、<line-break>わしの運命に決着をつけるつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "But Shalli, your trump card still remains. I shall leave the rest to you. It is time I settled matters with my own fate.<end>"
KO: "하지만 샤리여. 너희에겐 아직 비장의 수가 남아 있지 않느냐. 뒤는 맡기마. 나는 여기서 내 운명에 결판을 낼 생각이다.<end>"

## 1360609
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。じゃあね、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>あとは、僕とバロルでやるよ。<end>"
EN: "I see. Goodbye, old Zofor. Balor and I will handle the rest.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 안녕, 조포르 할아버지. 나머지는 나와 발로르가 할게.<end>"

## 1360610
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの運命の幕を<line-break>降ろしてもらおうか。<line-break>ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, Nemea... will you bring down the curtain on my fate?<end>"
KO: "자, 내 운명의 막을 내려주겠나. 네메아여.<end>"

## 1360611
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ。<line-break>お前の予言が、私の運命を示し、<line-break>私は、その運命と戦ってきた。<end>"
EN: "Zofor. Your prophecy showed me my fate, and I have fought against it ever since.<end>"
KO: "조포르여. 네 예언이 내 운명을 가리켰고, 나는 그 운명과 싸워 왔다.<end>"

## 1360612
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃったな。<line-break>じゃが、そろそろ終わりにしよう。<line-break>もう、お主も疲れたろう？<end>"
EN: "So you have. But it is time we brought this to an end. You must be tired by now.<end>"
KO: "그랬지. 하지만 이제 슬슬 끝내자꾸나. 너도 이제 지쳤겠지?<end>"

## 1360613
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "受け入れられぬ運命であれば、<line-break>私はこれからも戦い続けるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If fate is unacceptable, then I will go on fighting it.<end>"
KO: "받아들일 수 없는 운명이라면 나는 앞으로도 계속 싸울 것이다.<end>"

## 1360614
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうか。<line-break>ならば、最後の予言を残そうか。<line-break>それは…、<line-break>お主らの死じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Is that so? Then I shall leave you with one final prophecy: your deaths!<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그런가. 그럼 마지막 예언을 남기지. 그것은… 너희의 죽음이다!<end>"

## 1360615
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、これで眠れる…。<line-break>不吉な予知を得ることも、<line-break>もうあるまいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Now I can sleep. No more ominous premonitions.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 이걸로 잠들 수 있겠군…. 불길한 예지를 얻는 일도 이제는 없겠지.<end>"

## 1360616
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>お前は最後に何を見たのだ？<line-break>我々の死では、あるまい。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What did you see at the end? It was not our deaths, was it?<end>"
KO: "조포르…. 너는 마지막에 무엇을 본 것이냐? 우리의 죽음은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1360617
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ぐふっ。ムダだ…。<line-break>運命は…、変わらぬ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Gah! It is useless... Fate... does not change...<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 크흑. 소용없다…. 운명은… 바뀌지 않는다….<end>"

## 1360618
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>進むことしか、私たちには、できぬ。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리에겐 나아가는 수밖에 없다.<end>"

## 1360619
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ来られた。<line-break>じゃが、もうここには<line-break>バロル様も闇の王女もおられぬ。<line-break>お二人は<line-break>地上で最も闇の深い場所…。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. But neither Lord Balor nor the Princess of Darkness is here now. The two have gone to the darkest place on earth...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔구나. 하지만 이제 여기에는 발로르 님도 어둠의 왕녀도 계시지 않는다. 두 분은 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳으로….<end>"

## 1360620
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロル様の城、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かわれた。<end>"
EN: "To Lord Balor's castle, the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "발로르 님의 성, 폐성의 왕좌로 향하셨다.<end>"

## 1360621
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべてはバロル様が<line-break>完全に復活されるまでの<line-break>時間稼ぎにすぎぬ。<end>"
EN: "All this is merely to buy time until Lord Balor is fully restored.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 발로르 님께서 완전히 부활하실 때까지 시간을 버는 것에 불과하다.<end>"

## 1360622
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>賞賛に値するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. That indomitable will deserves praise.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는군, 네메아. 어둠의 신기·파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 다른 자였다면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 것이다. 그 강인한 정신은 칭찬할 만하다.<end>"

## 1360623
