## 1360631
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た。<line-break>だが、徒労だったな。<line-break>ここにはバロルはおらぬ。<line-break>地上で最も闇の深い場所、<line-break>懐かしきかつての居城である、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かった。<end>"
EN: "You have come far, but it was all in vain. Balor is not here. He has gone to the darkest place on earth: his familiar old stronghold, the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다. 하지만 헛걸음했군. 여기에는 발로르가 없다. 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳, 그리운 옛 거성인 폐성의 왕좌로 향했다.<end>"

## 1360632
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、すべては徒労に終わる。<line-break>貴様は破壊神の円卓騎士サムスンと<line-break>このザハクによって、ここで死ぬのだ。<end>"
EN: "And all your efforts shall end in vain. Here, Samson, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction, and I, Zahak, will kill you.<end>"
KO: "그리고 모든 것은 헛수고로 끝난다. 네놈은 파괴신의 원탁기사 삼슨과 이 자하크에게 여기서 죽는 것이다.<end>"

## 1360633
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？<line-break>一度倒したはずだとでも言いたそうだな。<line-break>我らは魔人なれば不死身だ。<line-break>愚かな人間の召喚で何度でも具現する。<line-break>徒労だったな。<end>"
EN: "What is it? You look as though you mean to say you defeated us once before. We demons are immortal. Whenever foolish humans summon us, we take form anew. Your efforts were in vain.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지? 한 번 쓰러뜨렸다고 말하고 싶은 모양이군. 우리는 마인이니 불사신이다. 어리석은 인간의 소환으로 몇 번이고 현현하지. 헛수고였다.<end>"

## 1360634
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急ごう。<line-break>我はすべてに徒労を教えねばならぬ。<line-break>すべては徒労だと教えねばならぬ。<line-break>すべてに例外なく教えねばならぬ。<line-break>思い上がった虚無の子や闇の王女、<line-break>神、そして無限のソウルにもな。<end>"
EN: "We must hurry. I must teach all things that effort is futile. I must teach them that all endeavor is in vain. I must teach everything, without exception: the presumptuous Child of the Void, the Princess of Darkness, the god, and even the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "서두르자. 나는 모든 것에 헛수고를 가르쳐야 한다. 모든 것은 헛수고라고, 예외 없이 모두에게 가르쳐야 한다. 오만한 허무의 아이와 어둠의 왕녀, 신, 그리고 무한의 소울에게도.<end>"

## 1360635
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世にあるものは絶望。<line-break>すべての営みは徒労。<line-break>貴様らにも死をもって教えてやろう。<line-break>我こそは破壊のあとに来たるべき虚無、<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士、<line-break>一切の営みをあざ笑うものザハク！<end>"
EN: "All that exists is despair. Every endeavor is in vain. I shall teach you this through death. I am the Void that must follow destruction, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction―Zahak, who mocks all endeavor!<end>"
KO: "세상에 있는 것은 절망. 모든 행위는 헛수고. 너희에게도 죽음으로 가르쳐 주마. 나는 파괴 뒤에 와야 할 허무, 파괴신의 원탁기사, 모든 행위를 비웃는 자 자하크다!<end>"

## 1360636
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚者の頑迷さで絶望を否定する…か。<line-break>笑止だ。虚無は常に貴様らのそばにある。<line-break>すべては…徒労だ…。<line-break>しばらく眠らせてもら…おう…。<end>"
EN: "So, in the stubbornness of fools, you deny despair. How absurd. The Void is always at your side. All is... in vain... I shall sleep... for a while...<end>"
KO: "어리석은 자의 완고함으로 절망을 부정한다…인가. 우습군. 허무는 언제나 네놈들 곁에 있다. 모든 것은… 헛수고다…. 잠시 잠들도록… 하지….<end>"

## 1360637
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>進むことしか、私たちには、できぬ。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리에겐 나아가는 수밖에 없다.<end>"

## 1360638
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては<line-break>闇の王女が傷を癒されるまでの<line-break>時間稼ぎにすぎぬ。<end>"
EN: "All this is merely to buy time while the Princess of Darkness heals her wounds.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 어둠의 왕녀께서 상처를 치유할 때까지의 시간 벌이에 불과하다.<end>"

## 1360639
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>いかに神となったとはいえ<line-break>エルファスもあれにはかなわぬ。<line-break>賞賛するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. Even though Elfas has become a god, he is no match for it. I commend your indomitable will.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는구나, 네메아. 어둠의 신기·파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 보통 사람이라면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 터다. 아무리 신이 되었다 해도 엘파스조차 저것에는 미치지 못하지. 그 강인한 정신을 칭찬하마.<end>"

## 1360640
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紹介しよう。<line-break>エンシャントの新皇帝<line-break>ザギヴ・ディンガルだ。<line-break>ディンガル家の正統な後継者であり、<line-break>強大な闇の力を身中に宿しておる。<line-break>闇の世を治めるにふさわしき皇帝よ。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to introduce Zagiv Dyneskal, the new emperor of Ancient. The rightful heir to the Dyneskal line, with a mighty power of darkness dwelling within her. An emperor fit to rule the dark world.<end>"
KO: "소개하지. 엔샨트의 새 황제 자기브 딘갈이다. 딘갈 가문의 정통 후계자이며 강대한 어둠의 힘을 몸 안에 품고 있다. 어둠의 세상을 다스리기에 걸맞은 황제지.<end>"

## 1360641
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう騒ぐでない。これも運命。<line-break>ザギヴに巣くっておった闇が、<line-break>時を得て、甦りつつあるだけのこと。<line-break>ネメアを失い、弱くなった心では、<line-break>闇を抑えきれなかったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Do not make such a fuss. This too is fate. The darkness that dwelt within Zagiv has simply found its hour and begun to awaken. Her heart was weakened by the loss of Nemea, and could no longer hold it back.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 소란 피우지 마라. 이것도 운명. 자기브에게 깃들어 있던 어둠이 때를 만나 되살아나고 있을 뿐이다. 네메아를 잃고 약해진 마음으로는 어둠을 억누르지 못한 거지.<end>"

## 1360642
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、目覚めよ。<line-break>ザギヴに潜みし闇よ！<end>"
EN: "Now awaken, darkness that lurks within Zagiv!<end>"
KO: "자, 깨어나라. 자기브 안에 숨은 어둠이여!<end>"

## 1360643
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。<line-break>ウウ…ア…アア…アアグ…。<line-break>ガアアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Uhh... Ah... Aah... Aaagh... Gaaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "으으…. 우우… 아… 아아… 아악…. 가아아아악!<end>"

## 1360645
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！　長かったぜぇ！<line-break>ネメアにやられてから、<line-break>ずっと待ってたんだよ！<line-break>この女の中に潜んで、<line-break>闇の力が強くなるのをよ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! I've waited so long! Ever since Nemea defeated me, I've been hiding inside this woman, waiting for the power of darkness to grow!<end>"
KO: "기히힛! 길었다! 네메아에게 당한 뒤로 계속 기다렸어! 이 여자 안에 숨어서 어둠의 힘이 강해지기만을 말이야!<end>"

## 1360646
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついに復活したぜ！<line-break>これで本格的に活動できる！<line-break>ありがとよ。ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>まず手始めに、お前を殺す！<line-break>マゴス様の恐怖のフルコース、<line-break>とくと味わえ！　ギーヒッ！<end>"
EN: "At last, I'm back! Now I can really get to work! Thanks! Geh-heh-heh! And I'll start by killing you! Prepare to savor Lord Magos's full course of terror! Geh-heh!<end>"
KO: "드디어 부활했다! 이제 본격적으로 활동할 수 있어! 고맙다. 기히히힛! 우선 시작으로 네놈을 죽여 주마! 마고스 님의 공포 풀코스, 실컷 맛봐라! 기힛!<end>"

## 1360647
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>俺様を殺してどうするつもりなんだ？　<line-break>何も変わりゃ…しないぜ…！<line-break>ザギヴって女も、<line-break>生き返りは…しないぜ。<line-break>クク…。ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Geh-heh-heh! What do you think killing me will accomplish? It won't... change a thing... And that woman Zagiv... won't come back to life. Heh... Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 이 몸을 죽여서 어쩌려고? 아무것도… 변하지 않아…! 자기브라는 여자도 되살아나진… 않아. 크크… 하하하!<end>"

## 1360648
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>君の切り札は簡単に<line-break>やられちゃったけど、どうするの？<end>"
EN: "Well, old Zofor? Your trump card went down rather easily. What will you do now?<end>"
KO: "자, 조포르 영감. 네 비장의 수가 너무 쉽게 당해 버렸는데, 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 1360649
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、シャリよ。<line-break>お主らの切り札は残っておろう。<line-break>あとは任せよう。<line-break>わしは、ここらで、<line-break>わしの運命に決着をつけるつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "But Shalli, your trump card still remains. I shall leave the rest to you. It is time I settled matters with my own fate.<end>"
KO: "하지만 샤리여. 너희에겐 아직 비장의 수가 남아 있지 않느냐. 뒤는 맡기마. 나는 여기서 내 운명에 결판을 낼 생각이다.<end>"

## 1360650
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。じゃあね、ゾフォル爺さん。<line-break>あとは、僕と王女様でやるよ。<end>"
EN: "I see. Goodbye, old Zofor. The princess and I will handle the rest.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 안녕, 조포르 할아버지. 나머지는 나와 왕녀님이 할게.<end>"

## 1360651
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、わしの運命の幕を<line-break>降ろしてもらおうか。<line-break>ネメアよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, Nemea... will you bring down the curtain on my fate?<end>"
KO: "자, 내 운명의 막을 내려주겠나. 네메아여.<end>"

## 1360652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ。<line-break>お前の予言が、私の運命を示し、<line-break>私は、その運命と戦ってきた。<end>"
EN: "Zofor. Your prophecy showed me my fate, and I have fought against it ever since.<end>"
KO: "조포르여. 네 예언이 내 운명을 가리켰고, 나는 그 운명과 싸워 왔다.<end>"

## 1360653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃったな。<line-break>じゃが、そろそろ終わりにしよう。<line-break>もう、お主も疲れたろう？<end>"
EN: "So you have. But it is time we brought this to an end. You must be tired by now.<end>"
KO: "그랬지. 하지만 이제 슬슬 끝내자꾸나. 너도 이제 지쳤겠지?<end>"

## 1360654
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "受け入れられぬ運命であれば、<line-break>私はこれからも戦い続けるだろう。<end>"
EN: "If fate is unacceptable, then I will go on fighting it.<end>"
KO: "받아들일 수 없는 운명이라면 나는 앞으로도 계속 싸울 것이다.<end>"

## 1360655
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうか。<line-break>ならば、最後の予言を残そうか。<line-break>それは…、<line-break>お主らの死じゃ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Is that so? Then I shall leave you with one final prophecy: your deaths!<end>"
KO: "후후후, 그런가. 그럼 마지막 예언을 남기지. 그것은… 너희의 죽음이다!<end>"

## 1360656
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、これで眠れる…。<line-break>不吉な予知を得ることも、<line-break>もうあるまいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Now I can sleep. No more ominous premonitions.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 이걸로 잠들 수 있겠군…. 불길한 예지를 얻는 일도 이제는 없겠지.<end>"

## 1360657
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル…。<line-break>お前は最後に何を見たのだ？<line-break>我々の死では、あるまい。<end>"
EN: "Zofor... What did you see at the end? It was not our deaths, was it?<end>"
KO: "조포르…. 너는 마지막에 무엇을 본 것이냐? 우리의 죽음은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 1360658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッフッフ…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ぐふっ。ムダだ…。<line-break>運命は…、変わらぬ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Gah! It is useless... Fate... does not change...<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 크흑. 소용없다…. 운명은… 바뀌지 않는다….<end>"

## 1360659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>進むことしか、私たちには、できぬ。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리에겐 나아가는 수밖에 없다.<end>"

## 1360660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ来られた。<line-break>じゃが、もうここには<line-break>闇の王女はおられぬ。<line-break>闇の王女は傷を癒すため<line-break>地上で最も闇の深い場所…。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. But the Princess of Darkness is no longer here. To heal her wounds, she has gone to the darkest place on earth...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔구나. 하지만 이제 이곳에는 어둠의 왕녀가 계시지 않는다. 어둠의 왕녀는 상처를 치유하기 위해 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳으로….<end>"

## 1360661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロル様の城、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かわれた。<end>"
EN: "To Lord Balor's castle, the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "발로르 님의 성, 폐성의 왕좌로 향하셨다.<end>"

## 1360662
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては<line-break>闇の王女が傷を癒されるまでの<line-break>時間稼ぎにすぎぬ。<end>"
EN: "All this is merely to buy time while the Princess of Darkness heals her wounds.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 어둠의 왕녀께서 상처를 치유할 때까지의 시간 벌이에 불과하다.<end>"

## 1360663
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>いかに神となったとはいえ<line-break>エルファスもあれにはかなわぬ。<line-break>賞賛するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. Even though Elfas has become a god, he is no match for it. I commend your indomitable will.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는구나, 네메아. 어둠의 신기·파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 보통 사람이라면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 터다. 아무리 신이 되었다 해도 엘파스조차 저것에는 미치지 못하지. 그 강인한 정신을 칭찬하마.<end>"

## 1360664
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルよ。<line-break>お前の予言が、私の運命を示し、<line-break>私は、その運命と戦ってきた。<end>"

EN: "Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"

## 1360672
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来た。<line-break>だが、徒労だったな。<line-break>ここには闇の王女はおらぬ。<line-break>傷を癒すべく、地上で最も闇の深い場所<line-break>かつて魔王バロルがいた恐怖の城、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かった。<end>"
EN: "You have come far, but it was all in vain. The Princess of Darkness is not here. To heal her wounds, she has gone to the darkest place on earth: the castle of terror where Balor, Demon King, once dwelt―the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다. 하지만 헛걸음했군. 여기에 어둠의 공주는 없다. 상처를 치유하기 위해 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳, 한때 마왕 발로르가 있던 공포의 성, 폐성의 옥좌로 향했다.<end>"

## 1360673
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、すべては徒労に終わる。<line-break>貴様は破壊神の円卓騎士サムスンと<line-break>このザハクによって、ここで死ぬのだ。<end>"
EN: "And all your efforts shall end in vain. Here, Samson, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction, and I, Zahak, will kill you.<end>"
KO: "그리고 모든 것은 헛수고로 끝난다. 네놈은 파괴신의 원탁기사 삼슨과 이 자하크에게 여기서 죽는 것이다.<end>"

## 1360674
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？<line-break>一度倒したはずだとでも言いたそうだな。<line-break>我らは魔人なれば不死身だ。<line-break>愚かな人間の召喚で何度でも具現する。<line-break>徒労だったな。<end>"
EN: "What is it? You look as though you mean to say you defeated us once before. We demons are immortal. Whenever foolish humans summon us, we take form anew. Your efforts were in vain.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지? 한 번 쓰러뜨렸다고 말하고 싶은 모양이군. 우리는 마인이니 불사신이다. 어리석은 인간의 소환으로 몇 번이고 현현하지. 헛수고였다.<end>"

## 1360675
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急ごう。<line-break>我はすべてに徒労を教えねばならぬ。<line-break>すべては徒労だと教えねばならぬ。<line-break>すべてに例外なく教えねばならぬ。<line-break>思い上がった虚無の子や闇の王女、<line-break>神、そして無限のソウルにもな。<end>"
EN: "We must hurry. I must teach all things that effort is futile. I must teach them that all endeavor is in vain. I must teach everything, without exception: the presumptuous Child of the Void, the Princess of Darkness, the god, and even the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "서두르자. 나는 모든 것에 헛수고를 가르쳐야 한다. 모든 것은 헛수고라고, 예외 없이 모두에게 가르쳐야 한다. 오만한 허무의 아이와 어둠의 왕녀, 신, 그리고 무한의 소울에게도.<end>"

## 1360676
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世にあるものは絶望。<line-break>すべての営みは徒労。<line-break>貴様らにも死をもって教えてやろう。<line-break>我こそは破壊のあとに来たるべき虚無、<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士、<line-break>一切の営みをあざ笑うものザハク！<end>"
EN: "All that exists is despair. Every endeavor is in vain. I shall teach you this through death. I am the Void that must follow destruction, a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction―Zahak, who mocks all endeavor!<end>"
KO: "세상에 있는 것은 절망. 모든 행위는 헛수고. 너희에게도 죽음으로 가르쳐 주마. 나는 파괴 뒤에 와야 할 허무, 파괴신의 원탁기사, 모든 행위를 비웃는 자 자하크다!<end>"

## 1360677
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚者の頑迷さで絶望を否定する…か。<line-break>笑止だ。虚無は常に貴様らのそばにある。<line-break>すべては…徒労だ…。<line-break>しばらく眠らせてもら…おう…。<end>"
EN: "So, in the stubbornness of fools, you deny despair. How absurd. The Void is always at your side. All is... in vain... I shall sleep... for a while...<end>"
KO: "어리석은 자의 완고함으로 절망을 부정한다…인가. 우습군. 허무는 언제나 네놈들 곁에 있다. 모든 것은… 헛수고다…. 잠시 잠들도록… 하지….<end>"

## 1360678
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どのような運命が待とうと、<line-break>進むことしか、私たちには、できぬ。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>. Whatever fate awaits us, we can do nothing but press on.<end>"
KO: "간다, <value:$28>. 어떤 운명이 기다리든 우리에겐 나아가는 수밖에 없다.<end>"

## 1360679
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　ネメアよ、生きテいたか…。<line-break>だが、うぬハもはや…闇に落ちシ者…。<line-break>　　　我が軍門ニ降るがよい。<end>"
EN: "Nemea... so you still live. But you have already... fallen into darkness. Surrender to me.<end>"
KO: "네메아여, 살아 있었는가…. 하지만 너 또한 이미… 어둠에 떨어진 자…. 내 휘하로 들어오라.<end>"

## 1360680
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　クックックッ。ネメアよ。<line-break>　　　　　　闇ニ落ちテなお、<line-break>　　　我ト刺し違えようと…するとは…。<line-break>　　アクマデ運命神ノことのはニ従うカ。<line-break>　　　ここでうぬヲ滅ぼスノミでは<line-break>　　　我ガ憎しみハ…満足できぬ…。<line-break>　　　うぬガ我ヲ滅ぼす運命ナラバ<line-break>　我ハうぬヲ砕キ、運命ヲ飲み喰ラオウ。<line-break>　　　謁見ノ間に…来るガよイ。<line-break>　　皇帝の玉座…コタビハうぬの血で<line-break>　　朱に染メ、世界ノ運命ソノモノヲ<line-break>　　ココニ終わラセテくレヨウぞ…！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Nemea. Even after falling into darkness, you would still... take me down with you? Will you follow the words of the god of fate to the bitter end? Merely destroying you here... will not sate my hatred. If fate decrees that you are to destroy me, I shall crush you and devour fate itself. Come... to the audience chamber. This time, I shall stain the emperor's throne crimson with your blood and bring the fate of the world itself to an end here...!<end>"
KO: "크크크. 네메아여. 어둠에 떨어지고도 나와 함께 죽으려 하다니…. 끝까지 운명신의 말씀을 따르는가. 여기서 네놈을 없애는 것만으로는 내 증오가 만족하지 않는다…. 네놈이 나를 멸할 운명이라면 내가 네놈을 부수고 운명까지 삼켜 버리겠다. 알현의 방으로 와라. 황제의 옥좌를 이번에는 네 피로 붉게 물들이고 세계의 운명 그 자체를 여기서 끝내 주마…!<end>"

## 1360681
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　…我ハ再び降臨セリ。<line-break>生あるモノすべてに滅びヲ…。<line-break>　一切ノ事象ヲ無に帰さん…。<end>"
EN: "…I have descended once more. Destruction upon every living thing… I shall reduce all things to nothing…<end>"
KO: "…나는 다시 강림했노라. 살아 있는 모든 것에 멸망을…. 모든 현상을 무로 돌리리라….<end>"

## 1360682
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おー、さすがは伝説の魔王だね。<line-break>風格があるよ。頼もしいーっ！<line-break>　<line-break>闇の王女・ティアナ、<line-break>魔王バロルに仕える神聖なる闇の巫女よ<line-break>彼ならきっと君の願いを救えるよ。<line-break>さあ、そのかわいい唇で告げて？<line-break>君は、何を、望むの？<end>"
EN: "Oh, just what you'd expect from a legendary Demon King. So imposing! So reassuring! Tiana, Princess of Darkness, sacred priestess of darkness who serves Balor, Demon King―surely he can grant your wish. Now, speak it with those pretty lips of yours. What... do you... desire?<end>"
KO: "오오, 역시 전설의 마왕이네. 위엄이 있어. 든든해~! 어둠의 왕녀 티아나, 마왕 발로르를 섬기는 신성한 어둠의 무녀여. 그라면 분명 네 소원을 구해 줄 거야. 자, 그 귀여운 입술로 말해 봐. 너는 무엇을 바라는 거야?<end>"

## 1360683
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　ネメアよ、生きテいたか…。<line-break>だが、うぬハもはや…闇に落ちシ者…。<line-break>　　我が軍門ニ降るがよい。<end>"
EN: "Nemea... so you still live. But you have already... fallen into darkness. Surrender to me.<end>"
KO: "네메아여, 살아 있었는가…. 하지만 너 또한 이미… 어둠에 떨어진 자…. 내 휘하로 들어오라.<end>"

## 1360684
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　…我ハ再び降臨セリ。<line-break>　生あるモノすべてに滅びヲ…。<line-break>　一切ノ事象ヲ無に帰さん…。<end>"
EN: "...I have descended once more. Destruction upon all that lives... I shall return all things to nothingness...<end>"
KO: "…나는 다시 강림했노라. 살아 있는 모든 것에 멸망을…. 모든 현상을 무로 돌리리라….<end>"

## 1360685
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我ハ己の欲するままに…世界ヲ支配し…<line-break>　　　破壊シ…消滅させるのみ…。<end>"
EN: "I shall do as I desire: rule the world... destroy it... erase it from existence. Nothing more...<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 원하는 대로… 세계를 지배하고… 파괴하고… 소멸시킬 뿐….<end>"

## 1360686
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　クックックッ。ネメアよ。<line-break>　　　　　闇ニ落ちテなお、<line-break>　我ト刺し違えようと…するとは…。<line-break>アクマデ運命神ノことのはニ従うカ。<line-break>　　ここでうぬヲ滅ぼスノミでは<line-break>　　我ガ憎しみハ…満足できぬ…。<line-break>　　うぬガ我ヲ滅ぼす運命ナラバ<line-break>我ハうぬヲ砕キ、運命ヲ飲み喰ラオウ。<line-break>　　　謁見ノ間に…来るガよイ。<line-break>　皇帝の玉座…コタビハうぬの血で<line-break>　朱に染メ、世界ノ運命ソノモノヲ<line-break>　ココニ終わラセテくレヨウぞ…！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Nemea. Even after falling into darkness, you would still... take me down with you? Will you follow the words of the god of fate to the bitter end? Merely destroying you here... will not sate my hatred. If fate decrees that you are to destroy me, I shall crush you and devour fate itself. Come... to the audience chamber. This time, I shall stain the emperor's throne crimson with your blood and bring the fate of the world itself to an end here...!<end>"
KO: "크크크. 네메아여. 어둠에 떨어지고도 나와 함께 죽으려 하다니…. 끝까지 운명신의 말씀을 따르는가. 여기서 네놈을 없애는 것만으로는 내 증오가 만족하지 않는다…. 네놈이 나를 멸할 운명이라면 내가 네놈을 부수고 운명까지 삼켜 버리겠다. 알현의 방으로 와라. 황제의 옥좌를 이번에는 네 피로 붉게 물들이고 세계의 운명 그 자체를 여기서 끝내 주마…!<end>"

## 1360687
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのおびただしいソウルの流れ<line-break>おそらく、世界中の人間が<line-break>バロルにソウルをうばわれた…<end>"
EN: "That vast torrent of Souls... Balor must have stolen them from people all over the world.<end>"
KO: "저 엄청난 소울의 흐름…. 아마 전 세계의 인간들이 발로르에게 소울을 빼앗긴 거야….<end>"

## 1360688
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう誰にも止められないよ。<line-break>強大な闇の巨人が世界中を<line-break>襲いはじめたんだ。<line-break>でも、これはほんの序の口。<line-break>ここからが本番さ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、本番なのに弱ったな。<line-break>エルファスが君の仲間になって<line-break>舞台を降りちゃったからね。<line-break>しょうがないや、<line-break>エルファスの代役を<line-break>立てさせてもらうとするかな。<line-break>破壊の化身、魔王バロルをね。<end>"
EN: "No one can stop it now. Mighty Giants of Darkness have begun attacking all over the world. But that's only the beginning. Now the real show starts. Except I've got a problem: Elfas joined you and left the stage. Oh, well. I suppose I'll have to bring in an understudy for him―Balor, Demon King, the Embodiment of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "이제 아무도 막을 수 없어. 강대한 어둠의 거인들이 온 세계를 습격하기 시작했거든. 하지만 이건 겨우 시작이야. 이제부터가 본편이지. 그런데 본편인데 곤란하게 됐네. 엘파스가 네 동료가 돼서 무대에서 내려가 버렸잖아. 어쩔 수 없지. 엘파스 대신 대역을 세워야겠어. 파괴의 화신, 마왕 발로르를 말이야.<end>"

## 1360689
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…나는 다시 강림했노라. 살아 있는 모든 것에 멸망을…. 모든 현상을 무로 돌리리라….<end>"

## 1360690
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の巨人をこれだけ<line-break>呼び寄せることができたから<line-break>バロルも復活できた。<line-break>それもすべて、<line-break>強大な闇の力の持ち主である<line-break>闇の王女がいればこそだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Summoning this many Giants of Darkness made Balor's resurrection possible. Of course, none of it could have happened without the Princess of Darkness and her tremendous dark power.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 거인을 이만큼 불러모을 수 있었기에 발로르도 부활할 수 있었어. 그것도 모두 강대한 어둠의 힘을 지닌 어둠의 왕녀가 있었기 때문이지만.<end>"

## 1360691
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>彼女を闇に突き落とした<line-break>最大の功労者は君だよ。アハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. No one did more than you to cast her into darkness. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 그녀를 어둠에 빠뜨린 최대 공로자는 너야. 아하하하!<end>"

KO: "저 엄청난 소울의 흐름…. 아마 전 세계의 인간들이 발로르에게 소울을 빼앗긴 거야….<end>"

## 1360704
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　クックックッ…。<line-break>　　他愛のナイ…見セ物デアッタ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　ネメア…そして無限のソウルよ…<line-break>　絶望トともに…我ヘノ…供物トなレ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　これヨり…滅ビノ宴が始まル…。<line-break>　死コソ…うぬらノ逃レえぬ運命…。<line-break>　　無こそ…コノ世界ノ終焉なり…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... What a trifling... spectacle that was... Nemea... and you who bear the Infinite Soul... In your despair... become offerings unto me... Now... the feast of destruction begins... Death is the fate none of you shall escape... Nothingness shall be this world's end...<end>"
KO: "크크크…. 시시한… 구경거리였군…. 네메아… 그리고 무한의 소울이여… 절망과 함께… 나에게 바치는 제물이 되어라…. 이제… 멸망의 연회가 시작된다…. 죽음이야말로… 너희가 피할 수 없는 운명…. 무야말로… 이 세계의 종언이다….<end>"

## 1360705
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　クックックッ…。<line-break>　　他愛のナイ…見セ物デアッタ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　ネメア…そして無限のソウルよ…<line-break>　絶望トともに…我ヘノ…供物トなレ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　これヨり…滅ビノ宴が始まル…。<line-break>　　死コソ…うぬらノ逃レえぬ運命…。<line-break>　　　無こそ…コノ世界ノ終焉なり…。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... That was a paltry little spectacle... Nemea... and you, Infinite Soul... Together with your despair... become offerings to me... Now the feast of destruction begins... Death is the fate none of you can escape... Nothingness is the end of this world...<end>"
KO: "크크크…. 시시한… 구경거리였군…. 네메아… 그리고 무한의 소울이여… 절망과 함께… 나에게 바치는 제물이 되어라…. 이제… 멸망의 연회가 시작된다…. 죽음이야말로… 너희가 피할 수 없는 운명…. 무야말로… 이 세계의 종언이다….<end>"

## 1360706
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神様、やっちゃって。<end>"
EN: "Go on, God. Do it.<end>"
KO: "신님, 해버려.<end>"

## 1360707
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ！<line-break>すごい！<line-break>ちゃんと動くよ、これ！<line-break>アトレイア。<line-break>かわいそうな君にこの神様をあげるよ。<line-break>これで、君の好きな世界を作ってよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, ahaha! Amazing! It really works! Atleia, poor thing, I'll give you this god. Use it to make the world you want.<end>"
KO: "우후후 아하하! 굉장해! 제대로 움직여, 이거! 아트레이아. 불쌍한 너에게 이 신을 줄게. 이걸로 네가 좋아하는 세계를 만들어.<end>"

## 1360708
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…嫌い。<line-break>こんな世界滅べばいいんだわ。<end>"
EN: "I hate it. A world like this should be destroyed.<end>"
KO: "…싫어. 이런 세상, 멸망해 버리면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1360709
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だってさ。困っちゃうよねぇ。<line-break>勇者様、この世界を救って～！<line-break>…って感じかな。アハハ…<line-break>あとは頼んだよ、<line-break>勇者<value:$28>様。<line-break>アハハハ…！<end>"
EN: "You heard her. What a predicament. “Oh, great Hero, save the world!” Something like that, I suppose. Ahaha... I'll leave the rest to you, mighty Hero <value:$28>. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "그렇다네. 곤란하지? 용사님, 이 세계를 구해 줘~! …이런 느낌인가. 하하…. 나머지는 부탁할게, 용사 <value:$28>님. 하하하…!<end>"

## 1360710
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは私が光を求めたときにすら、<line-break>冷たくあしらった…。<line-break>世界を滅ぼす前にあなたを殺すわ。<line-break>復讐するの。<end>"
EN: "Even when I sought the light, you turned me away coldly... Before I destroy the world, I'll kill you. I'll have my revenge.<end>"
KO: "당신은 내가 빛을 원했을 때조차 차갑게 대했어…. 세계를 멸망시키기 전에 당신부터 죽일 거야. 복수하겠어.<end>"

## 1360711
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたさえ、私に光をくれなければ、<line-break>こんな事にはならなかった…。<line-break>世界を滅ぼす前にあなたを殺すわ。<line-break>復讐するの。<end>"
EN: "If only you had never given me light, none of this would have happened... Before I destroy the world, I'll kill you. I'll have my revenge.<end>"
KO: "당신이 내게 빛을 주지만 않았어도 이런 일은 없었어…. 세계를 멸망시키기 전에 당신을 죽일 거야. 복수할 거야.<end>"

## 1360712
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…、何…！？<end>"
EN: "W-What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐…, 뭐야…!?<end>"

## 1360713
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無事か、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "무사하냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1360714
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…見えなければ、よかった。<end>"
EN: "I wish I'd never been able to see.<end>"
KO: "…보이지 않았다면 좋았을 텐데.<end>"

## 1360715
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇にいるままでよかった。<line-break>闇にいた方がよかった。<line-break>何も見えないままならよかった。<line-break>なぜ、このつらい現実に光を当てた。<line-break>ずっとこんなもの見たくなかった。<line-break>苦しまずにすんだのに…。<end>"
EN: "I should have stayed in the darkness. I was better off there. I should never have seen anything. Why did you cast light on this painful reality? I never wanted to see any of it. Then I wouldn't have had to suffer...<end>"
KO: "어둠 속에 그대로 있었으면 좋았어. 어둠 속에 있는 편이 나았어. 아무것도 보이지 않았으면 좋았어. 왜 이 괴로운 현실에 빛을 비춘 거야. 계속 이런 건 보고 싶지 않았어. 괴롭지 않아도 됐는데….<end>"

## 1360716
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれなければよかった。<line-break>苦しまずにすんだのに！<end>"
EN: "I wish I'd never been born. Then I wouldn't have had to suffer!<end>"
KO: "태어나지 않았으면 좋았을 텐데. 그랬으면 괴롭지도 않았을 텐데!<end>"

## 1360717
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界の悲しみが<line-break>かなえられない願いが<line-break>どうしようもない思いが<line-break>闇の王女の暗き心に共鳴して<line-break>闇の巨人として<line-break>世界中で立ち上がる…。<end>"
EN: "The sorrow of this world, its unfulfilled wishes, its hopeless yearnings―they resonate with the dark heart of the Princess of Darkness and rise throughout the world as Giants of Darkness...<end>"
KO: "이 세계의 슬픔이, 이루어지지 않는 소원이, 어찌할 수 없는 마음이 어둠의 왕녀의 어두운 마음에 공명해 어둠의 거인으로서 세계 곳곳에 일어선다…<end>"

## 1360718
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、かわいそうに。<line-break>生まれたてだったのに。<line-break>ひどいことするなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Aw, poor thing. It had only just been born. That was cruel.<end>"
KO: "아아, 불쌍해라. 태어난 지 얼마 되지도 않았는데. 너무하잖아.<end>"

## 1360719
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いっか。<line-break>まだまだたくさん<line-break>産まれたからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, well. Plenty more have been born.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐나. 아직 많이 태어났으니까.<end>"

## 1360720
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらがっても無駄。<line-break>私の可愛い子たちが…<line-break>必ず、あなたたちに絶望を…<end>"
EN: "Struggling is useless. My precious children will be sure to plunge you into despair...<end>"
KO: "저항해도 소용없어. 내 귀여운 아이들이… 반드시 너희에게 절망을…<end>"

## 1360721
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな醜い世界…<line-break>見なければ良かった…。<line-break>知らなければ良かった…。<line-break>全てを無に還すの。<end>"
EN: "This hideous world... I wish I'd never seen it. I wish I'd never known. I'll return it all to nothingness.<end>"
KO: "이런 추악한 세상… 보지 말았어야 했어…. 몰랐어야 했어…. 모든 것을 무로 돌릴 거야.<end>"

## 1360722
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…たとえ闇に落ちようと、<line-break>私は私だ！<end>"
EN: "Even if I fall into darkness, I am still myself!<end>"
KO: "…설령 어둠에 떨어져도 나는 나다!<end>"

## 1360723
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>破滅の槍の力を解放する気？<line-break>新たなる神よ、退避するのです！<end>"
EN: "Damn! Are you going to unleash the power of the Spear of Ruin? New god, withdraw!<end>"
KO: "큭! 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방할 생각이야? 새로운 신이여, 피하십시오!<end>"

## 1360724
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必ず、いらしてください。<line-break>その死に様を見届けてさしあげます。<line-break>フフ…フフフ…アハハ…！<end>"
EN: "Do be sure to come. I shall watch you die. Hee hee... Hee hee hee... Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "꼭 와 주세요. 그 최후를 지켜봐 드리겠습니다. 후후… 후후후… 아하하…!<end>"

## 1360725
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまぬ。<line-break>手間取った。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me. I was delayed.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 시간이 좀 걸렸다.<end>"

## 1360726
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのおびただしいソウルの流れ<line-break>おそらく、世界中の人間が<line-break>エルファスにソウルをうばわれた…<line-break>いや、<line-break>救世主エルファスにすがって<line-break>ソウルを差し出したのだ。<end>"
EN: "That enormous torrent of Souls... Elfas must have stolen the Souls of people throughout the world. No―they clung to Elfas the Savior and offered their Souls willingly.<end>"
KO: "저 엄청난 소울의 흐름…. 아마 세계의 인간들이 모두 엘파스에게 소울을 빼앗긴… 아니, 구세주 엘파스에게 의지해 소울을 바친 것이다.<end>"

## 1360727
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界のすべてが<line-break>静寂に<line-break>支配されているだろう。<end>"
EN: "The entire world must now lie under the dominion of silence.<end>"
KO: "세계의 모든 것이 정적에 지배되어 있겠지.<end>"

## 1360728
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなったな、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>私も力を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "You've grown strong, <value:$28>. Let me lend you my strength as well.<end>"
KO: "…강해졌군, <value:$28>. 나도 힘을 보태지.<end>"

## 1360729
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が見えるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "What do you see?<end>"
KO: "뭐가 보이는 걸까?<end>"

## 1360730
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の心の中…？<end>"
EN: "Inside people's hearts...?<end>"
KO: "사람의 마음속…?<end>"

## 1360731
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛みが痛みを生み、<line-break>悲しみが悲しみを生む。<line-break>人の思いは人を縛ってゆく。<line-break>人は自分が逃れられない<line-break>悲しみと痛みの檻の中にいることに<line-break>いつか気づく…。そして叫ぶの…。<end>"
EN: "Pain gives birth to pain, and sorrow to sorrow. People's feelings bind one another. One day, they realize they are trapped in a prison of sorrow and pain from which they can never escape... and then they cry out.<end>"
KO: "고통이 고통을 낳고 슬픔이 슬픔을 낳는다. 사람의 마음은 사람을 얽매어 가지. 인간은 자신이 벗어날 수 없는 슬픔과 고통의 우리 안에 있다는 걸 언젠가 깨닫게 돼…. 그리고 외치는 거야….<end>"

## 1360732
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれなければよかった。<line-break>苦しまずにすんだのに…とね。<end>"
EN: "“I wish I'd never been born. Then I wouldn't have had to suffer...”<end>"
KO: "태어나지 않았으면 좋았을 텐데. 그러면 괴롭지 않았을 거라고….<end>"

## 1360733
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml

EN: "The sorrow of this world, its unfulfilled wishes, its helpless yearnings―all resonate with the darkness in the Princess of Darkness's heart, rising throughout the world as Giants of Darkness...<end>"

KO: "아아, 불쌍해라. 태어난 지 얼마 되지도 않았는데. 너무하잖아.<end>"

## 1360735

## 1360736
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ…あらがわないほうが<line-break>幸せなのに。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... You'd be happier if you stopped resisting.<end>"
KO: "후후… 거스르지 않는 편이 행복할 텐데.<end>"

## 1360737
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神様、やっちゃって。<end>"
EN: "Go on, God. Do it.<end>"

## 1360738
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ！<line-break>すごい！<line-break>ちゃんと動くよ、これ！<line-break>ティアナ。<line-break>かわいそうな君にこの神様をあげるよ。<line-break>これで、君の好きな世界を作ってよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, ahaha! Amazing! It really works! Tiana, I'll give this god to poor you. Use it to make the world you want.<end>"
