## 1360739
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界を無に帰すの…。<line-break>人の想いの網から解放してあげるの…。<line-break>すべてを…。<end>"
EN: "I will return this world to nothingness... I will free everything from the web of human feeling... Everything...<end>"
KO: "이 세계를 무로 돌리는 거야…. 사람들의 생각이라는 그물에서 해방해 주는 거야…. 모든 것을….<end>"

## 1360740
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だってさ。困っちゃうよ。ねぇ。<line-break>勇者様、この世界を救って～！<line-break>…って感じかな。アハハ…<line-break>あとは頼んだよ、<line-break>勇者<value:$28>様。<line-break>アハハハ…！<end>"
EN: "So there you have it. Quite the predicament, isn't it? “Oh, great Hero, save the world!” Something like that. Ahaha... The rest is up to you, Hero <value:$28>. Ahahaha...!<end>"
KO: "그렇다네. 곤란하지. 그렇지? 용자님, 이 세계를 구해 주세요~! …뭐 그런 느낌이려나. 아하하… 뒤는 부탁할게, 용자 <value:$28> 님. 아하하하…!<end>"

## 1360741
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがアトレイアを<line-break>助けたせいで…こうなったわ…。<line-break>すべて、あなたのせい。<line-break>いかが？<line-break>私は、あなたが作り上げた化け物よ。<line-break>美しいかしら？<end>"
EN: "This happened because you saved Atleia... It's all your fault. What do you think? I am the monster you created. Am I beautiful?<end>"
KO: "네가 아트레이아를 도운 탓에… 이렇게 됐어…. 전부 네 탓이야. 어때? 나는 네가 만들어 낸 괴물이야. 아름다워?<end>"

## 1360742
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、こうなったことを<line-break>喜んでいるのよ…。<line-break>だって…。<line-break>あなたを死の静寂で包んで<line-break>私を永遠に拒絶できないように<line-break>できるんですもの…。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad this happened... Because now I can wrap you in the silence of death, so that you can never reject me again...<end>"
KO: "나는 이렇게 된 걸 기뻐하고 있어… 왜냐하면… 당신을 죽음의 정적에 감싸 영원히 나를 거절할 수 없게 만들 수 있으니까…<end>"

## 1360743
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…、何…！？<end>"
EN: "Wh-what...!?<end>"
KO: "뭐…, 뭐야…!?<end>"

## 1360744
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無事か、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "무사하냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1360745
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…たとえ闇に落ちようと、<line-break>私は私だ！<end>"
EN: "Even if I fall into darkness, I am still myself!<end>"
KO: "…설령 어둠에 떨어져도 나는 나다!<end>"

## 1360746
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>破滅の槍の力を解放する気なの？<line-break>神よ、逃げるのよ！<end>"
EN: "Damn! Are you going to unleash the Spear of Ruin's power? God, get away!<end>"
KO: "큭! 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방할 생각이야? 신이여, 도망쳐!<end>"

## 1360747
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまぬ。<line-break>手間取った。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me. I was delayed.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 시간이 좀 걸렸다.<end>"

## 1360748
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのおびただしいソウルの流れ<line-break>おそらく、世界中の人間が<line-break>エルファスにソウルをうばわれた…<line-break>いや、<line-break>救世主エルファスにすがって<line-break>ソウルを差し出したのだ。<end>"
EN: "That immense torrent of Souls... Elfas must have stolen the Souls of people throughout the world. No―they clung to Elfas the Savior and offered up their Souls.<end>"
KO: "저 엄청난 소울의 흐름…. 아마 세계의 인간들이 모두 엘파스에게 소울을 빼앗긴… 아니, 구세주 엘파스에게 의지해 소울을 바친 것이다.<end>"

## 1360749
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界のすべてが<line-break>静寂に<line-break>支配されているだろう。<end>"
EN: "The entire world must now lie under the dominion of silence.<end>"
KO: "세계의 모든 것이 정적에 지배되어 있겠지.<end>"

## 1360750
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界のすべてが<line-break>静寂に<line-break>支配されているだろう。<end>"
EN: "The entire world must now lie under the dominion of silence.<end>"
KO: "세계의 모든 것이 정적에 지배되어 있겠지.<end>"

## 1360751
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなったな、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>私も力を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "You've grown strong, <value:$28>. Let me lend you my strength as well.<end>"
KO: "…강해졌군, <value:$28>. 나도 힘을 보태지.<end>"

## 1360752
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、来たよ。<line-break><value:$28>が。<end>"
EN: "Look who's here. It's <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "봐, 왔어. <value:$28>가.<end>"

## 1360753
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ来られた。<line-break>じゃが、もうここに<line-break>バロル様はおられぬ。<line-break>地上で最も闇の深い場所…。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. But Lord Balor is no longer here. The darkest place on earth...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔네. 하지만 이미 이곳에 발로르 님은 계시지 않아. 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳….<end>"

## 1360754
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "廃城の玉座へ向かわれた。<end>"
EN: "To the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "폐성의 왕좌로 향하셨다.<end>"

## 1360755
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべてはバロル様が<line-break>完全に復活されるまでの<line-break>時間稼ぎにすぎぬ。<end>"
EN: "All this is merely to buy time until Lord Balor is fully restored.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 발로르 님께서 완전히 부활하실 때까지 시간을 버는 것에 불과하다.<end>"

## 1360756
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>いかに神となったとはいえ<line-break>エルファスもあれにはかなわぬ。<line-break>賞賛するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. Even though Elfas has become a god, he is no match for it. I commend your indomitable will.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는구나, 네메아. 어둠의 신기·파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 보통 사람이라면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 터다. 아무리 신이 되었다 해도 엘파스조차 저것에는 미치지 못하지. 그 강인한 정신을 칭찬하마.<end>"

## 1360757
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "紹介しよう。<line-break>エンシャントの新皇帝<line-break>ザギヴ・ディンガルだ。<line-break>ディンガル家の正統な後継者であり、<line-break>強大な闇の力を身中に宿しておる。<line-break>闇の世を治めるにふさわしき皇帝よ。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to introduce Zagiv Dyneskal, the new emperor of Ancient. The rightful heir to the Dyneskal line, with a mighty power of darkness dwelling within her. An emperor fit to rule the dark world.<end>"
KO: "소개하지. 엔샨트의 새 황제 자기브 딘갈이다. 딘갈 가문의 정통 후계자이며 강대한 어둠의 힘을 몸 안에 품고 있다. 어둠의 세상을 다스리기에 걸맞은 황제지.<end>"

## 1360758
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、無茶をするものだな、ネメア。<line-break>闇の神器・破滅の槍の力を解放するとは<line-break>余人なら闇に飲まれておるところよ。<line-break>賞賛に値するぞ、その強靱な精神。<end>"
EN: "Still, that was reckless, Nemea. Anyone else who unleashed the power of the Dark Artifact, the Spear of Ruin, would have been swallowed by darkness. That indomitable will deserves praise.<end>"
KO: "하지만 무모한 짓을 하는군, 네메아. 어둠의 신기·파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하다니. 다른 자였다면 어둠에 삼켜졌을 것이다. 그 강인한 정신은 칭찬할 만하다.<end>"

## 1360759
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、来たよ。<line-break><value:$28>が。<end>"
EN: "Look who's here. It's <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "봐, 왔어. <value:$28>가.<end>"

## 1360760
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ来られた。<line-break>じゃが、もうここには<line-break>闇の王女はおられぬ。<line-break>闇の王女は傷を癒すため<line-break>地上で最も闇の深い場所…。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. But the Princess of Darkness is no longer here. To heal her wounds, she has gone to the darkest place on earth...<end>"
KO: "잘 왔구나. 하지만 이제 이곳에는 어둠의 왕녀가 계시지 않는다. 어둠의 왕녀는 상처를 치유하기 위해 지상에서 가장 어둠이 깊은 곳으로….<end>"

## 1360761
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロル様の城、<line-break>廃城の玉座へ向かわれた。<end>"
EN: "To Lord Balor's castle, the throne of the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "발로르 님의 성, 폐성의 왕좌로 향하셨다.<end>"

## 1360762
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、傷つきし神よ、我の血肉を糧に…<line-break>こ、この世に…あ、明けない闇を…<end>"
EN: "O wounded god, feed upon my flesh and blood... B-bring upon this world... d-darkness without dawn...<end>"
KO: "사, 상처 입은 신이여, 내 피와 살을 양식으로… 이, 이 세상에… 새벽이 오지 않는 어둠을…<end>"

## 1360763
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前たちの作った神…<line-break>出してもらおうか。<end>"
EN: "Bring out the god you created.<end>"
KO: "너희가 만든 신… 내놓아 봐.<end>"

## 1360764
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界は新たなる神によって滅ぼされるの。<line-break>それが運命なのよ。<end>"
EN: "The new god will destroy the world. That is fate.<end>"
KO: "세계는 새로운 신에게 멸망할 거야. 그게 운명이야.<end>"

## 1360765
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それが運命だと言うのであれば、<line-break>あらがうまで！<end>"
EN: "If that is fate, then I will defy it!<end>"
KO: "…그게 운명이라면 끝까지 거슬러 주겠어!<end>"

## 1360766
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ…。<line-break>神を斬ろうだなんて？<line-break>本当に破壊の化身なのね？<line-break>だけど、<line-break>そうは…いかない！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... You would cut down a god? You truly are the Embodiment of Destruction, aren't you? But I won't let that happen!<end>"
KO: "후후…. 신을 베겠다고? 정말 파괴의 화신이네. 하지만 그렇게는… 안 돼!<end>"

## 1360767
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、ウフフ、アハハ！<line-break>傲慢なる獅子に死を！<line-break>甦った魔王に無限のソウルを！<line-break>邪眼帝バロルは完璧になり、<line-break>そして、世界を完全な沈黙で<line-break>覆うことができる…！<end>"
EN: "Heh... hee hee... ah ha ha! Death to the arrogant lion! The Infinite Soul to the resurrected Demon King! Balor, the Evil-Eyed Emperor, will be perfect―and able to cover the world in absolute Silence!<end>"
KO: "…후후, 우후후, 아하하! 오만한 사자에게 죽음을! 되살아난 마왕에게 무한의 소울을! 사안제 발로르는 완벽해지고, 세계를 완전한 침묵으로 뒤덮을 수 있다…!<end>"

## 1360768
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、このままでは終わらない…<line-break>か、神よ。<line-break>わ、我が血肉を糧に…<end>"
EN: "Th-this is not over... G-god... Take my flesh and blood... as your sustenance...<end>"
KO: "이, 이대로 끝나지 않는다… 시, 신이여. 내, 내 피와 살을 양식으로…<end>"

## 1360769
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナの死によってできた<line-break>強力な力場を通って現れたか。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "So it emerged through the powerful force field created by Tiana's death. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "티아나의 죽음으로 생긴 강력한 역장을 뚫고 나타났나. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1360770
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec136-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ジラーク、ボリューム６終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "질라크 볼륨 6 끝<end>"

## 1370000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "오시 시작<end>"

## 1370001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、俺がこの道場の師範<line-break>オッシだが、なんか用かい？<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I'm Ossi, master of this dojo. What can I do for you?<end>"
KO: "그래, 내가 이 도장의 사범 오시다. 무슨 볼일이지?<end>"

## 1370002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ。アイリーンを知ってるのか。<line-break>アイリーンは女だが、このあたりじゃ<line-break>かなう者がない剣の使い手だ。<line-break>アイリーンの父親は騎士でな。<line-break>アイリーンが男じゃなかったことを<line-break>くやしがってたよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You know Irene? She may be a woman, but no one around here can match her with a sword. Her father was a knight, you see. He always regretted that she wasn't born a boy.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 아이린을 아는 건가. 아이린은 여자지만 이 근방에선 상대할 자가 없는 검술의 달인이야. 아이린의 아버지는 기사였는데, 아이린이 남자로 태어나지 않은 걸 아쉬워했지.<end>"

## 1370003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの法、竜綱では<line-break>女性は騎士になれないからな。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それで、あいつは騎士になるため、<line-break>この国を出たわけだが…<line-break>あいつは元気だったかい？<end>"
EN: "Under Rostorl law―the Dragon Code―a woman cannot become a knight. That's why she left the country to become one. So... was she doing well?<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 법인 용강에서는 여자는 기사가 될 수 없거든. 그래서 그 녀석은 기사가 되려고 이 나라를 떠난 거지만… 잘 지내고 있었나?<end>"

## 1370004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつぁよかった。アイリーンに会ったら<line-break>よろしく言っておいてくれ。<line-break>成長を楽しみにしてるってな。<end>"
EN: "That's good to hear. If you see Irene, give her my regards. Tell her I'm looking forward to seeing how she develops.<end>"
KO: "그거 잘됐군. 아이린을 만나면 안부 전해 줘. 성장하는 걸 기대하고 있다고.<end>"

## 1370005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。ま、用がなくてもいいやな。<line-break>ゆっくりしてくといいさ。<end>"
EN: "I see. Well, you don't need a reason to be here. Stay and rest awhile.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 뭐, 볼일이 없어도 괜찮지. 느긋하게 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 1370006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>じゃないか。<line-break>今日はアイリーンは一緒じゃないんだな。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Irene's not with you today, eh?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28> 아니냐. 오늘은 아이린과 함께가 아니군.<end>"

## 1370007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？<line-break>聖杯を取り返せないうちは<line-break>俺に合わす顔がないって言ってた？　　　<line-break>相変わらず妙なところでお堅い奴だな。<line-break>ここはただでさえ女っ気がねぇんだから、<line-break>紅一点の存在は貴重なんだがなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Huh? She said she couldn't face me until she'd got the Grail back? She's as stubbornly proper as ever about the oddest things. There's little enough female company around here as it is. We can hardly do without our only woman.<end>"
KO: "응? 성배를 되찾기 전엔 내 얼굴을 볼 면목이 없다고 했다고? 여전히 이상한 데서 고지식한 녀석이군. 여긴 안 그래도 여자가 없으니 홍일점이 귀한데 말이지.<end>"

## 1370008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度はちゃんと連れて来いよ。　　　　　<line-break>お前さんもあまり<line-break>根をつめすぎねぇようにな。<end>"
EN: "Make sure you bring her next time. And don't work yourself too hard, either.<end>"
KO: "이번엔 제대로 데리고 와. 너도 너무 무리하진 말고.<end>"

## 1370009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>じゃないか。<line-break>今日はアイリーンは一緒じゃないんだな。<line-break>また、ケンカでもやらかしたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Irene's not with you today, eh? You two have another fight?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28> 아니야. 오늘은 아이린이 같이 없네. 또 싸우기라도 했어?<end>"

## 1370010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁんだ、またその手の話かい？　　　<line-break>お前さんも苦労が絶えないねぇ。<line-break>　<line-break>生き物の半分は女なんだからよ、<line-break>色目のひとつやふたつぐらい…。<end>"
EN: "What, that again? You never do get a moment's peace, do you? Half the creatures in this world are female. A wandering eye now and then is only...<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 또 그런 얘기냐? 너도 고생이 끊이질 않는군. 생물의 절반은 여자잖아. 추파 한두 번쯤이야…<end>"

## 1370011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンの一方的な思いこみ？<line-break>言いわけか？　男らしくねぇ奴だな。　<line-break>　<line-break>いいじゃねえか。<line-break>いい女に目がいくのは男の性なんだよ。<line-break>弁解なんかするもんじゃねぇぜ。<end>"
EN: "It was all in Irene's head? Is that your excuse? Not very manly of you. What's the harm? A man can't help noticing a beautiful woman. No point making excuses for it.<end>"
KO: "아이린의 일방적인 착각이라고? 변명이냐? 남자답지 못하네. 뭐 어때. 좋은 여자에게 눈이 가는 건 남자의 본능이야. 변명 같은 건 하지 마.<end>"

## 1370012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>じゃないか。<line-break>今日はアイリーンは一緒じゃないんだな。<line-break>また、ケンカでもやらかしたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Irene's not with you today, eh? You two have another fight?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28> 아니야. 오늘은 아이린이 같이 없네. 또 싸우기라도 했어?<end>"

## 1370013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たまには私抜きで行動してみなさい　　　　　<line-break>って言われた？<line-break>わっはっは。こりゃおもしれぇ。<line-break>お前さんがどこに行くにつけても、<line-break>べったりくっついてってんのは<line-break>アイリーンの方なのにな。<end>"
EN: "She told you to try doing things without her for once? Hah! That's a good one. Irene's the one who sticks to you like glue wherever you go.<end>"
KO: "가끔은 나 없이 행동해 보라고 들었다고? 와하하. 이거 재미있군. 네가 어디를 가든 찰싹 붙어서 따라다니는 건 오히려 아이린 쪽인데 말이야.<end>"

## 1370014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、でも、お前さんも早く<line-break>一人前になってやんねぇと<line-break>アイリーンの方も気苦労が絶えねぇわな。　　<line-break>別に一人前ってのは剣の腕に限らないんだぜ。<line-break>剣でアイリーンに勝てなくても、それ以外の<line-break>何かで安心させてやればいいんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Still, you'd better learn to stand on your own soon, or Irene will never have a moment's peace. And that doesn't just mean getting better with a sword. You don't have to beat Irene at swordplay. Just find some other way to put her mind at ease.<end>"
KO: "그래도 너도 빨리 한 사람 몫을 해야 아이린도 걱정을 덜겠지. 한 사람 몫이라는 게 꼭 검 실력만 뜻하는 건 아니야. 검으로 아이린에게 이기지 못해도 다른 무언가로 안심시켜 주면 돼.<end>"

## 1370015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん、<value:$28>か？<line-break>ま、ゆっくりしていけや。<end>"
EN: "Hm? <value:$28>? Well, stay a while and make yourself comfortable.<end>"
KO: "응, <value:$28>인가? 뭐, 푹 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 1370016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンのことは気にすることないさ。<line-break>あいつならひとりでも大丈夫だろうよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それよりも、せっかくのいい機会だ。<line-break>お前さんもアイリーン抜きでやってみな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "No need to worry about Irene. She can take care of herself. More importantly, this is a fine opportunity. Try managing without her for a while.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 신경 쓰지 않아도 돼. 그 녀석이면 혼자서도 괜찮을 거야. 그보다 모처럼 좋은 기회잖아. 너도 아이린 없이 한번 해 봐.<end>"

## 1370017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、それによ、<line-break>離れてみて、お互いの価値が<line-break>もっとよくわかることだってあるんだ。　<line-break>これでお別れってんじゃないんだからよ。<line-break>今度、アイリーンに会うときに<line-break>笑われないようにしとくこったな。<end>"
EN: "Besides, sometimes a little time apart helps you appreciate each other more. It's not as if you're parting for good. Just make sure Irene has no reason to laugh at you the next time you meet.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 떨어져 있어 봐야 서로의 소중함을 더 잘 알게 되는 경우도 있잖아. 이걸로 영영 헤어지는 것도 아니고. 다음에 아이린을 만날 때 웃음거리가 되지 않게 잘해 둬.<end>"

## 1370018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは剣の筋はいいし、<line-break>しっかりした娘だ。ただ、まだ若いしな。<line-break>ま、これからってところだ。<line-break>お前さんもそうだぜ。今のうちに<line-break>いろんな物を目に焼き付けて、そして、<line-break>いろんなことを、自分で考えてみな。<end>"
EN: "Irene has a real gift for the sword, and she's a sensible girl. But she's still young. She's only getting started. The same goes for you. While you can, take in as much of the world as possible, then think it all through for yourself.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 검의 재능도 있고 야무진 아가씨야. 다만 아직 젊지. 뭐, 이제부터야. 너도 마찬가지다. 지금부터 여러 가지를 눈에 새기고, 여러 일을 스스로 생각해 봐.<end>"

## 1370019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "若いからこそできる見方、<line-break>若いからこそできる考え方ってのが　　　　　<line-break>世の中にはあるからよ。<line-break>まぁ、この手の話ってのは、<line-break>自分も年とってみないと<line-break>わかんねぇことなんだけどな。<end>"
EN: "There are ways of seeing and thinking about the world that are only possible while you're young. Though I suppose that's the sort of thing you don't understand until you've grown older yourself.<end>"
KO: "젊기 때문에 할 수 있는 관점, 젊기 때문에 할 수 있는 생각이라는 게 세상에는 있으니까. 뭐, 이런 이야기는 자기도 나이를 먹어 봐야 알게 되는 거지만 말이야.<end>"

## 1370020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ。<line-break>ここの貴族さんたちのことかい？　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What? You mean the nobles around here?<end>"
KO: "뭐야. 여기 귀족들 이야기야?<end>"

## 1370021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まぁ、貴族制に縛られているのは　<line-break>俺たちだけじゃあ、ないからな。<line-break>　<line-break>やつらには、今の俺みたいな<line-break>好き勝手な暮らしなんて<line-break>できないからねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Well, we're not the only ones bound by the aristocratic system. They can't just live however they please, the way I do now.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 귀족제에 얽매인 건 우리만이 아니니까. 그 녀석들은 지금의 나처럼 마음대로 사는 건 못 하겠지.<end>"

## 1370022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれたときからかけさせられた　　<line-break>色眼鏡をとって、世の中を<line-break>見るってことだけでも、<line-break>奴らには大きな努力が必要なんだよ。<line-break>ましてや、俺たちと同じ世界に<line-break>立つってことはな。<end>"
EN: "Even taking off the tinted spectacles they were made to wear from birth and seeing the world as it is takes a tremendous effort for them. Let alone standing in the same world as us.<end>"
KO: "태어날 때부터 씌워진 색안경을 벗고 세상을 보는 것만으로도 그들에겐 큰 노력이 필요해. 하물며 우리와 같은 세계에 서는 건 더 그렇지.<end>"

## 1370023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出世、ねえ。<line-break>あまり興味がないな。<line-break>俺にはここの道場主ってのが一番さ。<line-break>俺なんかにゃ<line-break>組織の中で名をあげるなんてのは<line-break>ただ、もう、うんざりさ。<end>"
EN: "Getting ahead, huh? Can't say I'm much interested. Being the master of this training hall suits me best. I've had more than enough of trying to make a name for myself in some organization.<end>"
KO: "출세라…. 별 관심 없어. 내겐 여기 도장주인이 최고야. 조직 안에서 이름을 올리는 건 이제 정말 지긋지긋해.<end>"

## 1370024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは自分で考えて<line-break>騎士になりたいって言うから、　　　<line-break>俺も喜んで送り出すけどよ。<line-break>ま、なんにしたって　　　　　　　　<line-break>俺はここの暮らしが<line-break>気に入っちまっててね。<end>"
EN: "Irene thought it through for herself and says she wants to become a knight, so I'm happy to send her off. Anyway, I've grown pretty fond of life here.<end>"
KO: "아이린이 스스로 생각해서 기사가 되고 싶다고 하니 나도 기꺼이 보내 주는 거지만 말이야. 뭐가 됐든 난 여기 생활이 꽤 마음에 들어 버렸어.<end>"

## 1370025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは俺がやっと見つけた<line-break>自分の居場所だからな。<line-break>ちょっと、離れられねぇや。　　　　<end>"
EN: "This is where I finally found a place for myself. I can't exactly leave now.<end>"
KO: "여긴 내가 겨우 찾아낸 내 자리라서 말이야. 잠깐이라도 떠나기가 어렵네.<end>"

## 1370026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、剣の話かい？<line-break>ところでお前さんは、<line-break>剣に何を求めているんだい？<end>"
EN: "Oh, you want to talk about swords? Tell me, what are you looking for in the sword?<end>"
KO: "오, 검 이야기인가? 그런데 너는 검에 무엇을 바라고 있지?<end>"

## 1370027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強さ<end>"
EN: "Strength<end>"
KO: "강함<end>"

## 1370028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "美しさ<end>"
EN: "Beauty<end>"
KO: "아름다움<end>"

## 1370029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくわからない<end>"
EN: "Not sure<end>"
KO: "잘 모르겠어<end>"

## 1370030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣はしょせん敵を倒すための道具さ。<line-break>お前さんがどういうつもりで<line-break>強さを求めているのか知らねぇが、<line-break>このことを知らないでいる、<line-break>いや、知ろうとしないでいる<line-break>ってのはホントに危ねぇんだ。<end>"
EN: "In the end, a sword is a tool for defeating an enemy. I don't know why you're seeking strength, but staying ignorant of that―no, refusing even to understand it―is really dangerous.<end>"
KO: "검은 결국 적을 쓰러뜨리는 도구야. 네가 무슨 생각으로 강함을 원하는지는 모르지만 이걸 모르는 채로, 아니 알려고도 하지 않는 건 정말 위험해.<end>"

## 1370031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもよ、最初はただ強さを求めていただけな　　<line-break>はずなのに、その過程で何か違うものが<line-break>見えてくるってこともあるんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Still, sometimes you start out seeking nothing but strength, only to discover something else along the way.<end>"
KO: "하지만 처음에는 그저 강함만 추구했을 뿐인데 그 과정에서 전혀 다른 무언가가 보이게 되는 일도 있는 법이야.<end>"

## 1370032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い？　それが何かだって？　　　　<line-break>そんなものは人に聞くもんじゃないんだよ。<line-break>若いんだから、怠けてねぇで自分で見つけなよ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? You want to know what that is? That's not something you ask someone else. You're young―don't be lazy. Find it for yourself.<end>"
KO: "응? 그게 뭐냐고? 그런 걸 남에게 묻는 게 아니야. 젊은데 게으름 피우지 말고 스스로 찾아.<end>"

## 1370033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…美しさ、ねぇ。<line-break>まぁ、いいさ。<line-break>剣に何を見るかは人それぞれだしな。　　　　<line-break>お前さん、これからも冒険を続けるんだろう？<line-break>物事にはいろんな面があるってことを<line-break>剣が教えてくれるかもしれねぇやな。<end>"
EN: "Beauty, huh? Well, fair enough. Everyone sees something different in the sword. You're going to keep adventuring, aren't you? Maybe the sword will teach you that there's more than one side to everything.<end>"
KO: "…아름다움이라. 뭐, 좋아. 검에서 무엇을 보느냐는 사람마다 다르니까. 너 앞으로도 모험을 계속할 거지? 사물에는 여러 면이 있다는 걸 검이 가르쳐 줄지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1370034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そうだな。こんな時代じゃ、<line-break>いちいちそんなこと考えてられねぇか。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>だがな、物事には必ず何かひとつは<line-break>学べるものがあるはずだ。そして、<line-break>誰もがそこに同じものを見出すわけじゃあない。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose so. In times like these, maybe you can't stop to think about every little thing. But there's always something to be learned, no matter what it is―and not everyone will find the same lesson in it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇지. 이런 시대에 그런 것까지 일일이 생각할 순 없겠지. 하지만 말이야, 어떤 일이든 반드시 하나쯤 배울 게 있는 법이다. 그리고 누구나 거기서 같은 것을 발견하는 건 아니지.<end>"

## 1370035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんの剣もいずれは<line-break>お前さんに何かを教えてくれるさ。　　　　　　<line-break>お前さんだけの何かをな。<line-break>…って、そんなことずっと考えて<line-break>生きてたら、気が滅入っちまうか？<line-break>ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Someday, your sword will teach you something too. Something that's yours alone. Though I suppose you'd get awfully gloomy if you spent your whole life thinking like that. Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "네 검도 언젠가는 네게 뭔가를 가르쳐 줄 거야. 너만의 무언가를. …그런 생각만 하며 살면 우울해지려나? 하하하!<end>"

## 1370036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん、<value:$28>か？<line-break>ま、ゆっくりしていけや。<end>"
EN: "Hm? <value:$28>, is that you? Well, make yourself at home.<end>"
KO: "응, <value:$28>인가? 뭐, 푹 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 1370037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは剣の筋はいいし、<line-break>しっかりした娘だ。でも、なにせまだ<line-break>若いからなぁ。ま、これからってとこだ。<line-break>お前さんも今のうちにいろんな<line-break>物を目に焼き付けておくんだ。<line-break>そして、いろんなことを考えてみな。<end>"
EN: "Irene has a real gift for the sword, and she's a levelheaded girl. But she's still young. She's got plenty of growing left to do. While you're young yourself, take a good look at all sorts of things. Give them plenty of thought, too.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 검술 자질도 좋고 야무진 애야. 하지만 아직 젊으니 이제부터지. 너도 지금부터 여러 가지를 눈에 새겨 둬. 그리고 많은 걸 생각해 보라고.<end>"

## 1370038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "若いからこそできる見方、<line-break>若いからこそできる考え方ってのが　　　　　<line-break>世の中にはあるからよ。<line-break>まぁ、この手の話ってのは<line-break>自分も年とってみないと<line-break>わかんねぇことなんだけどな。<end>"
EN: "There are ways of seeing and thinking about the world that are only possible while you're young. Though this is the sort of thing you don't understand until you get older yourself.<end>"
KO: "젊기 때문에 가능한 시각, 젊기 때문에 가능한 생각이라는 게 세상에는 있어. 뭐, 이런 이야기는 자기도 나이를 먹어 봐야 아는 일이지만.<end>"

## 1370039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "弟子にアイリ－ンっていう娘がいてな。<line-break>騎士になるんだってこのロストールを<line-break>出ていっちまったよ。<line-break>剣の腕は立つんだが、<line-break>この街じゃ女は騎士になれないんでな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I've got an apprentice named Irene. She left Rostorl to become a knight. She's good with a sword, but women can't become knights in this city.<end>"
KO: "제자 중에 아이린이라는 여자애가 있어. 기사가 되겠다며 이 로스톨을 떠나 버렸지. 검 실력은 뛰어나지만 이 도시에서는 여자가 기사가 될 수 없거든.<end>"

## 1370040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気になるかい、<line-break>アイリーンのことが？　<end>"
EN: "Interested in Irene, are you?<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰이나, 아이린이?<end>"

## 1370041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気になる<end>"
EN: "I'm interested.<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰여<end>"

## 1370042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気にならない<end>"
EN: "I'm not interested.<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰이지 않는다<end>"

## 1370043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっしっし、ま、男は女のことが<line-break>気にならなくなったら終わりだからな。　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、俺に言わせりゃだな。<line-break>アイリーンはいい女になると思うけどよ、<line-break>まだまだ発展途上ってところだな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. Well, a man's finished once he stops taking an interest in women. If you ask me, Irene will grow into a fine woman, but she's still got a long way to go.<end>"
KO: "우히히, 뭐 남자는 여자한테 관심이 없어지면 끝이니까. 내 말로 하자면 아이린은 좋은 여자가 될 것 같지만 아직 한창 성장 중인 단계지.<end>"

## 1370044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い？<line-break>そういう話じゃないのかい？　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Eh? That's not what you meant?<end>"
KO: "응? 그런 이야기가 아니었나?<end>"

## 1370045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じ女のアイリーンが気になるわけだ？<line-break>んー、見たとこ、あんたもいい感じだぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、まぁ、俺に言わせると<line-break>まだまだ、ふたりとも<line-break>発展途上ってとこだな。<end>"
EN: "So Irene interests you even though you're both women? Hmm. From what I can see, you're looking pretty good yourself. Still, if you ask me, both of you have a long way to go.<end>"
KO: "같은 여자인 아이린이 신경 쓰이나 보지? 음, 보기엔 너도 꽤 괜찮아. 그래도 내 말로는 둘 다 아직 성장 중이야.<end>"

## 1370046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あせらずじっくりと女を磨いていくんだ。<line-break>ふたりとも素材はよさそうだからよ。<line-break>あとは本人の努力次第…。<end>"
EN: "Don't rush it. Take your time becoming fine women. You've both got plenty to work with. The rest is up to each of you...<end>"
KO: "조급해하지 말고 천천히 여자로서 자신을 갈고닦아. 둘 다 바탕은 좋아 보이니까. 나머지는 본인 노력에 달렸지….<end>"

## 1370047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、そういう話じゃ　　　　　　　　<line-break>なかったっけか？<end>"
EN: "Whoops. That wasn't what you meant, was it?<end>"
KO: "어라, 그런 얘기가 아니었던가?<end>"

## 1370048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうのはいかんなあ。<line-break>無関心は向上につながらねえぜ？<end>"
EN: "That's no good. Indifference won't help you improve, you know?<end>"
KO: "그런 건 좋지 않아. 무관심은 발전으로 이어지지 않는다고?<end>"

## 1370049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、アイリーンに会ったら<line-break>よろしく言っておいてくれや。<end>"
EN: "Well, if you see Irene, give her my regards.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아이린 만나면 안부나 전해 줘.<end>"

## 1370050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ。<line-break>ここの貴族さんたちのことかい？<end>"
EN: "What, you mean the nobles around here?<end>"
KO: "뭐야. 여기 귀족들 이야기야?<end>"

## 1370051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まぁ、貴族制に縛られているのは　<line-break>俺たちだけじゃあ、ないからな。<line-break>　<line-break>奴らには、今の俺みたいな<line-break>好き勝手な暮らしなんて<line-break>できないからねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Well... we're not the only ones bound by the aristocratic system. They don't have the freedom to live however they please, the way I do now.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 귀족제에 얽매인 건 우리뿐만이 아니니까. 녀석들은 지금의 나처럼 제멋대로 사는 생활은 못 하거든.<end>"

## 1370052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれたときからかけさせられた　　<line-break>色眼鏡をとって、世の中を<line-break>見るってことだけでも、<line-break>奴らには大きな努力が必要なんだよ。<line-break>ましてや、俺たちと同じ世界に<line-break>立つってことはね。<end>"
EN: "Even taking off the tinted spectacles they were made to wear from birth and seeing the world without them takes a tremendous effort. Let alone standing in the same world as us.<end>"
KO: "태어날 때부터 씌워진 색안경을 벗고 세상을 보는 것만으로도 그들에겐 큰 노력이 필요해. 하물며 우리와 같은 세계에 서는 건 더 그렇지.<end>"

## 1370053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出世ねえ。あまり興味がないな。<line-break>俺にはここの道場主ってのが一番さ。<line-break>　<line-break>俺なんかにゃ<line-break>組織の中で名をあげるってなんてのは<line-break>ただ、もう、うんざりさ。<end>"
EN: "Getting ahead, huh? Can't say it interests me much. Being master of this dojo suits me best. I've had more than enough of trying to make a name for myself in some organization.<end>"
KO: "출세라. 별로 관심 없는데. 나한텐 이 도장의 주인 자리가 제일 좋아. 나 같은 놈이 조직 안에서 이름을 떨치는 건 이제 정말 지긋지긋하거든.<end>"

## 1370054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは自分で考えて<line-break>騎士になりたいって言うから、　　　<line-break>俺も喜んで送り出してんだけどよ。　<end>"
EN: "Irene decided for herself that she wanted to become a knight, so I was glad to send her on her way.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 스스로 생각해서 기사가 되고 싶다고 했으니 나도 기꺼이 보내 줬지.<end>"

## 1370055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、なんにしたって　　　　　　　　<line-break>俺はここの暮らしが<line-break>気に入っちまっててね。<line-break>ここは俺がやっと見つけた<line-break>自分の居場所だからな。<line-break>ちょっと、離れられねぇや。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, I've grown pretty fond of life here. It took me a long time to find a place where I belong. I can't bring myself to leave it now.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 어쨌든 난 여기 생활이 마음에 들어 버렸거든. 여긴 내가 겨우 찾은 내 자리야. 쉽게 떠날 수 없어.<end>"

## 1370056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、剣の話かい？<line-break>ところでお前さんは、<line-break>剣に何を求めているんだい？<end>"
EN: "Oh, you want to talk about swords? So tell me, what do you want from the sword?<end>"
KO: "오, 검 이야기인가? 그런데 너는 검에 무엇을 바라고 있지?<end>"

## 1370057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強さ<end>"
EN: "Strength<end>"
KO: "강함<end>"

## 1370058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "美しさ<end>"
EN: "Beauty<end>"
KO: "아름다움<end>"

## 1370059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくわからない<end>"
EN: "I'm not sure<end>"
KO: "잘 모르겠어<end>"

## 1370060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。剣はしょせん敵を倒すための道具さ。<line-break>お前さんがどういうつもりで<line-break>強さを求めているのか知らねぇが、<line-break>このことを知らないでいる、<line-break>いや、知ろうとしないでいる<line-break>…ってのはホントに危ねぇんだ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. A sword is only a tool for defeating enemies. I don't know why you're seeking strength, but not knowing that―no, not even trying to understand it―is really dangerous.<end>"
KO: "그래. 검은 결국 적을 쓰러뜨리는 도구야. 자네가 무슨 생각으로 강함을 추구하는지는 모르지만, 이 사실을 모르고 있는 것, 아니 알려고 하지 않는 건 정말 위험한 일이야.<end>"

## 1370061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもよ、最初はただ強さを求めていただけな　　<line-break>はずなのに、その過程で何か違うものが<line-break>見えてくるってこともあるんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Still, sometimes you start out seeking nothing but strength, only to discover something else along the way.<end>"
KO: "하지만 처음에는 그저 강함만 추구했을 뿐인데 그 과정에서 전혀 다른 무언가가 보이게 되는 일도 있는 법이야.<end>"

## 1370062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い？　それが何かだって？<line-break>そんなものは人に聞くもんじゃないんだよ。<line-break>若いんだから、怠けてねぇで自分で見つけなよ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? You want to know what that is? That's not something you ask someone else. You're young―don't be lazy. Find it for yourself.<end>"
KO: "응? 그게 뭐냐고? 그런 걸 남에게 묻는 게 아니야. 젊은데 게으름 피우지 말고 스스로 찾아.<end>"

## 1370063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…美しさねぇ。<line-break>まぁ、いいさ。<line-break>剣に何を見るかは人それぞれだしな。　　　　<line-break>お前さん、これからも冒険を続けるんだろう？<line-break>物事にはいろんな面があるってことを<line-break>剣が教えてくれるかもしれねぇやな。<end>"
EN: "Beauty, huh? Well, that's fine. Everyone sees something different in a sword. You're going to keep adventuring, aren't you? Maybe your sword will teach you that there's more than one side to everything.<end>"
KO: "…아름다움이라. 뭐, 좋아. 검에서 무엇을 보는지는 사람마다 다르니까. 너, 앞으로도 모험을 계속할 거지? 사물에는 여러 면이 있다는 걸 검이 가르쳐 줄지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1370064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そうだな。こんな時代じゃ、<line-break>いちいちそんなこと考えてられねぇか。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>だがな、物事には必ず何かひとつは学べる　　<line-break>ものがあるはずだ。そして、誰もがそこに<line-break>同じものを見出すわけじゃあない。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose in times like these, you can't stop to think about every little thing. But there ought to be something to learn from everything, and not everyone will find the same thing in it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇지. 이런 시대에 그런 것까지 일일이 생각할 순 없겠지. 하지만 말이야, 어떤 일이든 반드시 하나쯤 배울 게 있는 법이다. 그리고 누구나 거기서 같은 것을 발견하는 건 아니지.<end>"

## 1370065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんの剣もいずれは<line-break>お前さんに何かを教えてくれるさ。　　　　　<line-break>お前さんだけの何かをな。<end>"
EN: "Someday, your sword will teach you something too. Something that's yours alone.<end>"
KO: "네 검도 언젠가는 네게 무언가를 가르쳐 줄 거야. 너만의 무언가를 말이지.<end>"

## 1370066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…って、そんなことずっと考えて<line-break>生きてたら、気が滅入っちまうか？　　<line-break>ハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Then again, dwelling on things like that your whole life might get a bit depressing, eh? Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 그런 걸 계속 생각하며 살면 우울해지려나? 하하하!<end>"

## 1370067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、人生急ぐことはねえ。<line-break>お前さんもゆっくりしていきなよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, there's no need to rush through life. Stay and take it easy for a while.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 인생을 서두를 건 없어. 너도 천천히 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 1370068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>カルラがいよいよ攻めてくるってな。<line-break>お前さんはどうするんだい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I hear Karula's finally about to attack. What will you do?<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 카를라가 드디어 쳐들어온다더군. 넌 어떻게 할 거야?<end>"

## 1370069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は、ロストールが負けて、カルラが<line-break>この平民街に危害を加えようとしたとき、<line-break>ここを守るために戦う。命を懸けてもな。　　<end>"
EN: "If Rostorl falls and Karula moves against this commoners' quarter, I'll fight to defend it, even if it costs me my life.<end>"
KO: "나는 로스톨이 패하고 카를라가 이 평민가에 위해를 가하려 한다면, 여길 지키기 위해 싸울 거다. 목숨을 걸어서라도.<end>"

## 1370070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺にはな、ジジイになるまで長生きして、<line-break>お前やアイリーンから金をせびって<line-break>悠々自適に暮らすっていう野望がある。<line-break>その野望を達成するためにも、<line-break>今、この街を廃墟にしちまうわけには<line-break>いかないってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "You see, I have this grand ambition: to live to a ripe old age, wheedle money out of you and Irene, and spend my days in comfort. And if I'm going to achieve it, I can't very well let this town be reduced to ruins now, can I?<end>"
KO: "난 말이지, 늙은이가 될 때까지 오래 살면서 너랑 아이린한테 돈이나 뜯어내며 유유자적 사는 야망이 있어. 그 야망을 이루려면 지금 이 도시가 폐허가 되게 둘 수는 없다는 거지.<end>"

## 1370071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっはっは。ま、いつまでたっても、<line-break>お前もアイリーンも<line-break>俺の弟子なわけよ。<line-break>だからこそ、俺の弟子たちが<line-break>どのように育っているかを見定めるのも、<line-break>まぁ、師匠の務めってところかな。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! No matter how much time passes, you and Irene will always be my pupils. So I suppose seeing how you've both grown is part of a master's duty.<end>"
KO: "하하하. 뭐, 언제까지고 너도 아이린도 내 제자지. 그러니까 내 제자들이 어떻게 자라고 있는지 확인하는 것도 스승의 의무라는 거야.<end>"

## 1370072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンという若葉に、<line-break>俺はどのような光を与えてやれたのか。<line-break>そこに咲く花の色に、俺は責任があるんだ。<line-break>たとえそれが、どんな色であろうとな。<line-break>そして、<value:$28>。<line-break>それは、お前って花の色にも同じなんだ。<end>"
EN: "Irene is a tender shoot. What kind of light was I able to give her? I am responsible for the color of the flower she blooms into, whatever that color may be. And <value:$28>, the same is true of the flower that is you.<end>"
KO: "아이린이라는 새싹에게 나는 어떤 빛을 비춰 줄 수 있었을까. 그곳에 피는 꽃의 색에는 내 책임도 있어. 그게 어떤 색이든 말이지. 그리고 <value:$28>. 그건 너라는 꽃의 색에도 마찬가지다.<end>"

## 1370073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺にはな、ジジイになるまで長生きして、<line-break>弟子のアイリーンたちから金をせびって<line-break>悠々自適に暮らすっていう野望がある。<line-break>その野望を達成するためにも、<line-break>今、この街を廃墟にしちまうわけには<line-break>いかないってわけだ。<end>"
EN: "You see, I have this grand ambition: to live to a ripe old age, wheedle money out of Irene and the rest of my pupils, and spend my days in comfort. And if I'm going to achieve it, I can't very well let this town be reduced to ruins now, can I?<end>"
KO: "나한텐 말이지, 늙은이가 될 때까지 오래 살아서 제자 아이린들에게 돈을 뜯어내며 유유자적하게 사는 야망이 있어. 그 야망을 이루려면 지금 이 도시를 폐허로 만들 순 없다는 거야.<end>"

## 1370074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そう言うなよ。ディンガル軍の<line-break>騎士になろうが、アイリーンが<line-break>俺の弟子だって事実は変わらない。<line-break>だからこそ、俺の弟子が<line-break>どのように育っているかを見定めるのも、<line-break>まぁ、師匠の務めってものなのさ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, don't say that. Even if Irene becomes a knight in the Dyneskal Army, that doesn't change the fact that she's my pupil. So I suppose seeing how she's grown is part of a master's duty.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇게 말하지 마. 딩갈군의 기사가 되든 아이린이 내 제자라는 사실은 변하지 않아. 그러니까 내 제자가 어떻게 자라고 있는지 지켜보는 것도 스승의 의무라는 거지.<end>"

## 1370075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンという若葉に、<line-break>俺はどのような光を与えてやれたのか。<line-break>そこに咲く花の色に、俺は責任がある。　　<line-break>たとえそれが、<line-break>どんな色であろうとな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Irene is a tender shoot. What kind of light was I able to give her? I am responsible for the color of the flower she blooms into, whatever that color may be.<end>"
KO: "아이린이라는 새싹에게 나는 어떤 빛을 줄 수 있었을까. 거기에 피는 꽃의 색에 나는 책임이 있다. 그것이 어떤 색이든 말이지.<end>"

## 1370076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは剣士としても、<line-break>そしてひとりの人間としても、<line-break>死なせるに惜しい奴だったよ。　　　　　　<line-break>道場からいなくなってかなり経つが、<line-break>今になってバカに寂しくなった気がする。<line-break>光ってた奴だっただけに、なおさらな。<end>"
EN: "Irene was too good a swordswoman, and too fine a person, to let die. She'd been away from the dojo for a long time, but it's only now that I'm starting to feel so damned lonely. She had such a spark about her. That only makes it worse.<end>"
KO: "아이린은 검사로서도, 한 사람의 인간으로서도 죽이기 아까운 녀석이었어. 도장에서 사라진 지도 꽤 됐는데 이제 와서 유난히 쓸쓸하군. 빛나던 녀석이라 더 그런가 봐.<end>"

## 1370077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンのことを一番悲しんでるのは<line-break>彼女の母親だよ。旦那さんに続いて、<line-break>ひとり娘まで戦争にさらわれちまった。<line-break>エルメスさんの姿を見ると、<line-break>アイリーンが街を出るときにとめりゃ…<line-break>なんて考えちまってな。<end>"
EN: "The one grieving most for Irene is her mother. First her husband, and now her only daughter―the war took them both. Whenever I see Hermes, I can't help wondering if I should've stopped Irene from leaving town.<end>"
KO: "아이린 일로 가장 슬퍼하는 건 그 애 어머니야. 남편에 이어 외동딸까지 전쟁에 빼앗겼으니 말이지. 에르메스 씨를 보면 아이린이 마을을 떠날 때 말렸어야 했나… 그런 생각이 들어.<end>"

## 1370078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんは、どう思う？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What do you think?<end>"
KO: "넌 어떻게 생각해?<end>"

## 1370079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまねぇ。<line-break>お前さんに聞くことじゃなかったな。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. I shouldn't have asked you that.<end>"
KO: "미안하군. 자네한테 물을 일이 아니었어.<end>"

## 1370080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまねぇ。<line-break>今はお前さんに話してやれることはない。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. There's nothing I can tell you right now.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 지금은 네게 말해 줄 수 있는 게 없어.<end>"

## 1370081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最悪の事態は避けられたな。<line-break>お前さんにも感謝してるよ。<line-break>これで俺の老後も保証つきってもんだ。<line-break>…冗談はさておき、ほっとしたよ。<line-break>俺の弟子が、俺たちの街を壊しちまった<line-break>ってのは勘弁してほしかったからな。<end>"
EN: "We avoided the worst, and I've got you to thank for that too. Looks like my old age is secure now. Joking aside, I'm relieved. I couldn't have borne it if my own pupil had destroyed our town.<end>"
KO: "최악의 사태는 피했군. 너한테도 감사하고 있어. 이걸로 내 노후도 보장됐다는 거지. 농담은 그만하고, 정말 안심했어. 내 제자가 우리 마을을 부숴 버렸다는 결말만큼은 사양하고 싶었거든.<end>"

## 1370082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、安心しきれる状態じゃないが、<line-break>俺としては、これからはアイリーンが<line-break>カルラをとめてくれると信じたいね。　<end>"
EN: "We're not out of danger yet, but I'd like to believe Irene will keep Karula in check from now on.<end>"
KO: "아직 완전히 안심할 상황은 아니지만, 난 이제 아이린이 카를라를 막아 줄 거라고 믿고 싶어.<end>"

## 1370083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回の戦いのあと、道場に人が殺到だ。<line-break>自警団を強化しようとか言ってな。<line-break>逃げようってよりはいいんだろうがな。<line-break>だが、そんなことをしても、<line-break>ディンガル軍を刺激するだけだ。<line-break>今はおとなしくした方がいい。<end>"
EN: "People have been flooding into the dojo since the battle, talking about strengthening the town watch. I suppose that's better than running away. But it'll only provoke the Dyneskal Army. For now, we'd be wiser to lie low.<end>"
KO: "이번 싸움 뒤로 도장에 사람이 몰려들고 있어. 자경단을 강화한다면서 말이야. 도망치자는 것보단 낫겠지만, 그런다고 딘갈군을 자극할 뿐이야. 지금은 얌전히 있는 편이 낫다.<end>"

## 1370084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>俺に何か用かい？　…つっても俺にゃ、　<line-break>もう、お前さんに教えることはないな。<line-break>でも、最後にひとつ言わしてもらうか。<line-break>お前さんは、歴史を知っているだろう？<line-break>歴史をただの知識にはしないでくれ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. What can I do for you? Though I suppose there's nothing left for me to teach you. Still, let me say one last thing. You know history, don't you? Don't let it be nothing more than knowledge to you.<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 내게 무슨 볼일이야? 이제 내가 가르쳐 줄 건 없겠지만 마지막으로 하나만 말하지. 넌 역사를 알고 있지? 역사를 단순한 지식으로만 만들진 마.<end>"

## 1370085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はお前さんに、困りもんの学者や<line-break>為政者には、なってほしくはないからな。<line-break>ま、そんなところかな。<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't want you to become the kind of scholar or ruler who's nothing but trouble. Well, that's about it.<end>"
KO: "난 네가 골칫거리 학자나 위정자가 되길 바라진 않거든. 뭐, 그런 정도다.<end>"

## 1370086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>この前は世話になったな。<line-break>アイリーンの様子はどうだい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Thanks for your help the other day. How's Irene doing?<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 지난번엔 신세 졌다. 아이린은 잘 지내나?<end>"

## 1370087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？　相変わらずうるさいって？　　<line-break>いっひっひ。まだまだ青いねぇ。<line-break>相変わらずなのはお前の方だよ。<line-break>普通、ああいう展開があれば、<line-break>そのあとふたりの仲は急速に発展する<line-break>ってのがお約束なんだが…ま、いいか。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Still as loud as ever, you say? Heh heh heh. You've got a lot to learn. You're the one who hasn't changed. Usually, after something like that, the two of you are supposed to grow much closer in no time. Ah, well.<end>"
KO: "뭐? 여전히 시끄럽다고? 히히, 아직 멀었구나. 여전한 건 너 쪽이야. 보통 그런 전개가 있었으면 그 뒤 두 사람 사이가 급속히 진전되는 게 정석인데… 뭐, 됐어.<end>"

## 1370088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最悪の事態は避けられたな。<line-break>お前さんにも感謝してるよ。<line-break>これで俺の老後も保証つきってもんだ。<line-break>ま、お前さんとアイリーンっていう<line-break>立派な弟子にせいぜい世話になるさ。<line-break>…てな冗談はさておき、ほっとしたよ。<end>"
EN: "We avoided the worst, and I've got you to thank for that too. Looks like my old age is secure now. With fine pupils like you and Irene, I'm sure you'll both take excellent care of me. Joking aside, I'm relieved.<end>"
KO: "최악의 사태는 피했군. 너한테도 감사하고 있어. 이걸로 내 노후도 보장됐다는 거지. 뭐, 너와 아이린이라는 훌륭한 제자들에게 실컷 신세 지면 되겠군. 농담은 그만하고, 정말 안심했어.<end>"

## 1370089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の弟子はやっぱり正しかった…つまり<line-break>俺の目も、俺がここでしてきたことも<line-break>正しかったってことだからな。<end>"
EN: "My pupil was right after all... which means my judgment was sound, and everything I've done here was right too.<end>"
KO: "내 제자는 역시 옳았어… 즉, 내 안목도 내가 여기서 해온 일도 옳았다는 뜻이니까.<end>"

## 1370090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんたち、うまくいくと思うぜ。　<line-break>いいパートナーになってくれ。<end>"
EN: "I think you'll work well together. I hope you'll make good partners.<end>"
KO: "너희 둘 잘될 것 같군. 좋은 파트너가 되어 줘.<end>"

## 1370091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンのことは残念だが、<line-break>俺にそれ以上とやかく言う資格はないな。<line-break>戦争に出ていれば、死とは常にとなり合わせだ。<line-break>アイリーンを斬ったのは誰かなんてのは<line-break>問題じゃない。アイリーンはこの戦乱の時代を<line-break>生き抜く力がなかったというだけだ。<end>"
EN: "What happened to Irene is regrettable, but I have no right to say any more than that. When you go to war, death is always at your side. It doesn't matter who struck Irene down. She simply lacked the strength to survive this age of war.<end>"
KO: "아이린 일은 유감이지만 내가 더 왈가왈부할 자격은 없군. 전쟁터에 나가면 죽음은 늘 곁에 있다. 아이린을 누가 베었는지는 문제가 아니야. 아이린에게는 이 전란의 시대를 살아남을 힘이 없었을 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1370092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>この前は世話になったな。<line-break>お前さんがいてくれて助かった。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Thanks for your help the other day. I'm glad you were there.<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 지난번엔 신세 졌어. 네가 있어서 살았다.<end>"

## 1370093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンなら大丈夫だろう。<line-break>あいつの目から迷いがなくなっていたからな。<line-break>いい騎士になってくれると思うぜ。<line-break>あれからディンガル軍が動く気配はないしな。<line-break>俺としては、カルラの襲撃計画を<line-break>アイリーンがとめてくれたと思いたいね。<end>"
EN: "Irene will be all right. There was no hesitation left in her eyes. I think she'll make a fine knight. There has been no sign of the Dyneskal Army moving since then. I'd like to believe Irene stopped Karula's plan to attack.<end>"
KO: "아이린이라면 괜찮을 거야. 그 애 눈에서 망설임이 사라졌거든. 좋은 기사가 될 거라고 봐. 그 뒤 딩갈군이 움직일 기색도 없고. 난 아이린이 카를라의 습격 계획을 막았다고 믿고 싶네.<end>"

## 1370094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今のところディンガル軍に動きはない。<line-break>この街もどことなく不安が漂ってはいるが、<line-break>住民の生活の息づかいは元に戻りつつある。<line-break>もともとこの平民街は、ロストール王家から<line-break>援助を受けていたわけではないからな。<line-break>昔も今も、自分たちの力で生きているんだ。<end>"
EN: "So far, the Dyneskal Army has made no move. There is still an undercurrent of anxiety in this town, but the pulse of the townspeople's lives is gradually returning to normal. This commoners' quarter has never received aid from the Rostorl royal family. Then as now, its people have lived by their own strength.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 딘갈군의 움직임은 없다. 이 거리에도 어딘지 불안한 기운이 감돌지만 주민들의 생활은 차츰 예전으로 돌아가고 있다. 애초에 이 평민가는 로스톨 왕가의 지원을 받던 곳도 아니었으니까. 예전이나 지금이나 자기 힘으로 살아가는 거야.<end>"

## 1370095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その力はたくましいものだが、武力に対抗する<line-break>種類の力ではない。<line-break>だから、俺がここにいるんだ。<line-break>これからも、ディンガルがこの街を<line-break>つぶしに来ない限り、<line-break>俺の方から動くつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "It's a hardy sort of strength, but not one that can stand against military force. That's why I'm here. Unless Dyneskal comes to crush this town, I have no intention of making the first move.<end>"
KO: "그 힘은 강인하지만 무력에 맞서는 종류의 힘은 아니야. 그래서 내가 여기 있는 거다. 앞으로도 딘갈이 이 도시를 짓밟으러 오지 않는 한 내가 먼저 움직일 생각은 없어.<end>"

## 1370096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>この前は世話になったな。<line-break>アイリーンの様子はどうだい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Thanks for your help the other day. How's Irene doing?<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 지난번엔 신세 졌다. 아이린은 잘 지내나?<end>"

## 1370097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？　相変わらずうるさいって？　　　　<line-break>いっひっひ。まだまだ青いねぇ。<line-break>相変わらずなのはお前の方だよ。<line-break>普通、ああいう展開があれば、<line-break>そのあとふたりの仲は急速に発展する<line-break>ってのが、お約束なんだが…ま、いいか。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Still as loud as ever, you say? Heh heh heh. You've got a lot to learn. You're the one who hasn't changed. Usually, after something like that, the two of you are supposed to grow much closer in no time. Ah, well.<end>"
KO: "응? 여전히 시끄럽다고? 이히히, 아직 멀었군. 여전한 건 네 쪽이야. 보통 그런 전개가 있으면 그 뒤 둘의 사이는 급속히 발전하는 게 정석인데… 뭐, 됐나.<end>"

## 1370098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、見かけない顔だね。<line-break>何か用かい？<end>"
EN: "Well, you're a new face. What can I do for you?<end>"
KO: "어라, 못 보던 얼굴이군. 무슨 용건이지?<end>"

## 1370099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ふーん、<value:$28>っていうのかい？<line-break>いい名前じゃないか。<line-break>顔形も男前だし。<end>ふーん、<value:$28>っていうのかい？<line-break>いい名前じゃないか。<line-break>顔もべっぴんさんだし。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hmm, so your name's <value:$28>? That's a fine name―and a handsome face to match.<end>Hmm, so your name's <value:$28>? That's a lovely name―and a pretty face to match.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0흐음, <value:$28>라고 해? 좋은 이름이네. 얼굴도 잘생겼고.<end>흐음, <value:$28>라고 해? 좋은 이름이네. 얼굴도 예쁘고.<end>"

## 1370100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そうだ、もし急いでなかったら<line-break>うちのダンナにも会っていっておくれよ。<line-break>きっと、おんなじような反応すると思うけどね。<line-break>この町の裏山で<line-break>ひとりで木を切ってるんだ。<line-break>行きゃあわかるよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's right. If you're not in a hurry, go meet my husband while you're here. I expect he'll react much the same way. He's up in the hills behind town, cutting wood by himself. You'll find him easily enough.<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다. 급하지 않으면 우리 남편도 만나 보고 가. 아마 똑같은 반응을 할걸. 이 마을 뒷산에서 혼자 나무를 베고 있어. 가 보면 알아.<end>"

## 1370101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだい？　<line-break><value:$28>じゃないか。<line-break>いつ帰ってきたんだい？　　　　　　　　　<line-break>おや、今日はナッジは一緒じゃないんだね。<line-break>ま、せっかく来たんならうちのダンナにも<line-break>会ってってやっておくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. When did you get back? Oh, Nadge isn't with you today. Since you've come all this way, go and see my husband too, won't you?<end>"
KO: "뭐야? <value:$28>잖아. 언제 돌아온 거야? 어라, 오늘은 나지가 같이 없네. 뭐, 모처럼 왔으니 우리 남편도 만나고 가 줘.<end>"

## 1370102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちのダンナはいつも通り、裏の山で<line-break>木を切ってるはずだよ。　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "My husband should be up in the hills out back, cutting wood as usual.<end>"
KO: "우리 남편은 늘 그렇듯 뒷산에서 나무를 베고 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1370103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、<value:$28>かい。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>, is that you?<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 1370104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちのダンナは、今日も、山で仕事さ。<line-break>ま、それが今のうちの人の生きがいだからね。<line-break>　<line-break>木を伐るのが生きがいだなんて<line-break>若いあんたには<line-break>ちょっとわかんないだろう？<end>"
EN: "My husband's up in the hills working again today. Well, that's what he lives for now. I don't suppose someone as young as you can quite understand living for nothing but cutting wood, can you?<end>"
KO: "우리 남편은 오늘도 산에서 일해. 뭐, 그게 지금은 그 사람 삶의 보람이니까. 나무 베는 게 삶의 보람이라니 젊은 너는 잘 모르겠지?<end>"

## 1370105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかります<end>"
EN: "I understand.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다<end>"

## 1370106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、わからない<end>"
EN: "I don't really understand.<end>"
KO: "잘 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1370107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、そうかい？<line-break>善し悪しの問題じゃないけど<line-break>ちょっと感心だね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you do? It's not a matter of right or wrong, but I'm rather impressed.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 그래? 좋고 나쁨의 문제는 아니지만 조금 감탄했어.<end>"

## 1370108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだろうねぇ。<line-break>でもね、男が何かに打ち込んでる姿<line-break>ってのはいいもんだよ。<end>"
EN: "I suppose not. But there's something wonderful about seeing a man devote himself to something.<end>"
KO: "그렇겠지. 하지만 남자가 무언가에 몰두하는 모습은 보기 좋은 법이야.<end>"

## 1370109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "木こりかどうかなんて問題じゃないんだけどね。<line-break>私は働く男の背中ってのに弱くてね。<line-break>　<line-break>うちの人には昔からそれがあったから、<line-break>つい、くらっときちまって、<line-break>駆け落ち同然で一緒になっちまったんだよ。<end>"
EN: "It doesn't matter that he's a woodcutter. I've always had a weakness for the sight of a man hard at work. My husband had that about him even back then, and I couldn't resist. I fell for him, and we ran off together―or near enough.<end>"
KO: "나무꾼이냐 아니냐가 문제는 아니야. 난 일하는 남자의 뒷모습에 약하거든. 우리 남편에겐 예전부터 그게 있어서 홀딱 반해 거의 도망치듯 같이 살게 됐지.<end>"

## 1370110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　うちの人のことかい？　　　　　　　<line-break>なんだい、藪から棒に。<line-break>　<line-break>そうだねぇ、もちろん頼りにしてるよ。<line-break>いざっていうときには、やっぱり<line-break>男手が必要だからね。<end>"
EN: "What? My husband? Where did that come from? Well, of course I rely on him. When it comes down to it, you do need a man's help.<end>"
KO: "응? 우리 남편 말이야? 갑자기 왜 그래. 그야 당연히 의지하고 있지. 막상 일이 생기면 역시 남자 손이 필요하니까.<end>"

## 1370111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、だからって男を甘やかしちゃ　　　　<line-break>いけないよ。頼りにしてても、<line-break>それをあまり見せすぎない。<line-break>でも、まったくそれを見せない<line-break>ってのもダメさ。そのさじ加減ってのが<line-break>家を守る秘訣だからね。<end>"
EN: "But that doesn't mean you should spoil a man. You can rely on him, but don't show it too much. Mind you, never showing it at all is no good either. Striking the right balance is the secret to keeping a household together.<end>"
KO: "그래도 남자를 너무 응석받이로 만들면 안 돼. 의지하고 있어도 그걸 너무 드러내진 말고. 하지만 전혀 안 보여 주는 것도 안 되지. 그 적당한 선을 지키는 게 집안을 지키는 비결이야.<end>"

## 1370112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0おっと、男のあんたにしてみれば<line-break>こんな話はつまらないかもしれないね。　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あんたも、いずれ、<line-break>私の言ってたことの意味が<line-break>わかるだろうよ。<end>よく覚えておきなよ。<line-break>家庭を持つ女の先輩として忠告さ。<line-break>いずれ、あんたも所帯を持ったらわかるよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Oh, I suppose a man like you might find all this rather dull. Still, one day you'll understand what I meant.<end>Remember that. Take it as advice from a woman with more experience of married life. You'll understand once you have a household of your own.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0이런, 남자인 네 입장에선 이런 얘기가 시시할지도 모르겠네. 하지만 너도 언젠가 내가 한 말의 뜻을 알게 될 거야.<end>잘 기억해 둬. 가정을 꾸린 여자로서 선배의 충고야. 너도 언젠가 살림을 꾸리면 알게 될 거야.<end>"

## 1370113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度はアイリーンにも<line-break>顔出せって言っといてくれ。<line-break>よろしくな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Tell Irene to stop by next time too, will you? I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "다음엔 아이린도 얼굴 비추라고 전해 줘. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1370114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、お前さんはお前さんの道を<line-break>頑張って進みぬくこった。<line-break>そこに、見えてくるものもあるからな。<end>"
EN: "Well, you just keep to your own path and see it through. You may find something along the way.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 넌 네 길을 힘껏 계속 가 봐. 그 끝에서 보이는 것도 있을 테니까.<end>"

## 1370115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、ここにはなんにもないけどよ、<line-break>好きなだけゆっくりしていくといい。<end>"
EN: "Well, there's not much here, but stay and rest as long as you like.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 여긴 아무것도 없지만 원하는 만큼 느긋하게 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 1370116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、Ｅｖオッシ全編だ。<line-break>予備メッセージの４だぁな。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "그래, Ev 오시 전편이다. 예비 메시지 4지.<end>"

## 1370117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>…がんばれよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... stay strong.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, …힘내.<end>"

## 1370118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、今はこんな<line-break>つらい世の中だが、お前さんは<line-break>自分の行くべき道を見失うなよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, these are hard times, but don't lose sight of the path you ought to follow.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 지금은 힘든 세상이지만 네가 가야 할 길을 잃지 마라.<end>"

## 1370119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小言臭くなって悪かったな。<line-break>まぁ、街は守れてるんだ。<line-break>今はそれで、よしとしようや。<end>"
EN: "Sorry for sounding like a nag. Still, we managed to protect the town. For now, let's be content with that.<end>"
KO: "잔소리처럼 말해서 미안하다. 뭐, 마을은 지켜냈잖아. 지금은 그걸로 됐다고 하자.<end>"

EN: ""
KO: "그래, Ev 오시 전편이다. 예비 메시지 8지.<end>"
