## 1370123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0おう、小僧、なんか用か？<end>おう、お嬢ちゃん、なんか用かい？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hey there, lad. Need something?<end>Hey there, young lady. Need something?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0어이, 꼬마, 무슨 일이냐?<end>어이, 아가씨, 무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 1370124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "するってえと、なんだ。こんな田舎の<line-break>こんな山奥まで来て、こんな木こりに　<line-break>話しかけて、何も用はねえか！？<line-break>はっはっは！　こりゃいいや。　<line-break>おヒマな冒険者様じゃなきゃ、　<line-break>こうはいかねえな！<end>"
EN: "So let me get this straight. You came all the way out to the mountains in the middle of nowhere, struck up a conversation with a woodcutter like me―and you don't even want anything!? Hah hah hah! That's a good one. Only an adventurer with time to kill could pull that off!<end>"
KO: "그러니까 뭐냐, 이런 시골 이런 산골짜기까지 와서 이런 나무꾼에게 말을 걸고도 아무 용건이 없다고!? 하하하! 이거 재밌군. 한가한 모험자님이 아니면 이럴 수 없겠지!<end>"

## 1370125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、お前<line-break>名前はなんていうんだい？<end>"
EN: "Say, what's your name?<end>"
KO: "그래, 너 이름이 뭐냐?<end>"

## 1370126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>か。<line-break>いい名前だな。なんていうか、こう、　<line-break>広がりがあるっていうのか…。<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1<value:$28>か。<line-break>いい名前だな。なんていうか、こう、　<line-break>安らぎがあるっていうのか…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>, eh? That's a good name. It's got a sort of... openness to it.<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1<value:$28>, eh? That's a good name. It's got a sort of... peaceful sound to it.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>인가. 좋은 이름이군. 뭐랄까, 이렇게… 펼쳐지는 느낌이 있다고 해야 하나….<end><if><value:$29><equal>%1<value:$28>인가. 좋은 이름이군. 뭐랄까, 이렇게… 편안함이 있다고 해야 하나….<end>"

## 1370127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺にもお前さんと同い年くらいの<line-break>仲間がいてよ。そいつも旅に出たんだ。<line-break>今ごろ、どこで何をしているか…。<end>何かを探して旅してるんだろうが、　　<line-break>早くその何かが見つかるといいな。<end>"
EN: "A companion of mine, about your age, set off on a journey too. Wonder where that one is now... and doing what.<end>Must be traveling in search of something. I hope it turns up soon.<end>"
KO: "나한테도 너랑 비슷한 나이의 동료가 있었는데 그 녀석도 여행을 떠났어. 지금쯤 어디서 뭘 하고 있을지….<end>뭔가를 찾아 여행 중이겠지만 빨리 그 무언가를 찾으면 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1370128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 1370129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、人生っていうのは　　　　　　　<line-break>あと戻りできないからな。<line-break>お前は前に進むしかないんだ。<line-break>ただ、どう前に進むか。<line-break>過去が過ちなら未来をどう正すべきか。<line-break>それをいつも考えて生きてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Well, there's no going back in life. All you can do is keep moving forward. But you have to think about how you'll do that. If the past was a mistake, how will you set things right in the future? Never stop asking yourself that as you go through life.<end>"
KO: "인생이란 건 되돌릴 수 없으니까. 넌 앞으로 나아갈 수밖에 없어. 다만 어떻게 나아갈 것인가. 과거가 잘못이었다면 미래를 어떻게 바로잡을 것인가. 그걸 늘 생각하며 살아가라.<end>"

## 1370130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、お前はお前なりに<line-break>答えを見つけてきたみたいだしな。<line-break>俺も、その答え、いい答えだと思うぜ。<line-break>力ってなんなのか？　<line-break>正しさってなんなのか？<line-break>自分はどうありたいと願うのか？<end>"
EN: "Well, looks like you've found your own answer. I think it's a good one too. What is strength? What is right? What kind of person do you want to be?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 너도 나름대로 답을 찾아온 것 같군. 나도 그 답, 좋은 답이라고 생각해. 힘이란 뭔가? 올바름이란 뭔가? 나는 어떤 존재이고 싶은가?<end>"

## 1370131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前なら、これからもそうやって<line-break>自分の道に答を出していけるんだろうな。<end>"
EN: "Knowing you, I reckon you'll keep finding your own answers as you make your way through life.<end>"
KO: "너라면 앞으로도 그렇게 자기 길에 답을 내릴 수 있겠지.<end>"

## 1370133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>そういう道を選んじまったか…。　 　     <line-break>　<line-break>で、結局、ヴァンはそれで喜んでくれたか？<end>"
EN: "I see... So that's the path you chose. And in the end, was Van happy with your choice?<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 그런 길을 택했나…. 그래서 결국 반은 그걸로 기뻐했어?<end>"

## 1370134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そういう顔をするってことは          <line-break>自分が選んだ道がどういうものだったか<line-break>わかってるってこったな。<line-break>だったら、これからどう生きるかだ。<line-break>歩いてきた道よりいい道はどこにあるのか、<line-break>お前にはわかり始めてるんだろう？<end>"
EN: "Well, that look on your face tells me you understand what kind of path you chose. What matters now is how you live from here on. You're starting to see where you might find a better path than the one you've walked so far, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 표정을 짓는 걸 보니 네가 고른 길이 어떤 길이었는지는 알고 있나 보군. 그렇다면 이제부터 어떻게 살아갈지가 중요하지. 지금까지 걸어온 길보다 더 나은 길이 어디에 있는지, 너도 슬슬 알기 시작한 거 아니냐?<end>"

## 1370135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。                            　　　<line-break>決着はついたんだな。<line-break>お前もナッジも、がんばったな。　<line-break>お前たちが出した答え、<line-break>お前たちがたどり着いた強さや正しさの意味、<line-break>そのどれもが、すばらしいと俺は思うぞ。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you did settle it. You and Nadge both worked hard. The answer you two found, and what you came to understand about strength and what is right―I think all of it is wonderful.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 결판이 난 모양이구나. 너도 나지도 잘했다. 너희가 내린 답, 너희가 도달한 강함과 올바름의 의미, 그 어느 것도 훌륭하다고 나는 생각한다.<end>"

## 1370136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、男子３日あわざれば…とは<line-break>よく言ったものだな。今俺の前にいるお前は<line-break>ここを旅立った時とはまるで別人だな。<end>"
EN: "The old saying is right: a man can change in three days. You're nothing like the person who set out from here.<end>"
KO: "사흘 못 보면 사람이 달라진다는 말이 딱 맞군. 지금 내 앞에 있는 넌 여기서 떠날 때와 완전히 다른 사람이야.<end>"

## 1370137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか……。<line-break>まだ、お前の中の決着はつかないままか…。<line-break>　<line-break>それなら、もう少し頑張ってみるこった。<line-break>簡単に答の出るものなんか世の中にそうそう<line-break>あるもんじゃないからな。<end>"
EN: "I see... So you still haven't settled it within yourself. In that case, keep at it a little longer. There aren't many questions in this world that come with easy answers.<end>"
KO: "그런가……. 아직 네 안에서 결론이 나지 않았군…. 그렇다면 조금 더 힘내 봐. 쉽게 답이 나오는 일은 세상에 그리 많지 않으니까.<end>"

## 1370138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、　<line-break>…がんばれよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... keep at it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, …힘내.<end>"

## 1370139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、お前か。<line-break>ヒマな奴だな、まったく。<end>"
EN: "You again? You've got too much time on your hands, you know that?<end>"
KO: "또 너냐. 정말 한가한 녀석이군.<end>"

## 1370140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ？<line-break>元気にやってっか？<end>"
EN: "So, how've you been? Keeping well?<end>"
KO: "어때? 잘 지내고 있냐?<end>"

## 1370141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？　なんで木こりなんて<line-break>やってんのかって？<line-break>そりゃ、やっぱりこれだな。<end>"
EN: "Huh? You want to know why I'm a lumberjack? Well, this is why.<end>"
KO: "응? 왜 나무꾼 일을 하냐고? 그건 역시 이거지.<end>"

## 1370142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おら、いい音だろ。小鳥のさえずり、          <line-break>小川のせせらぎ、そして、木こりが木を伐る音。<line-break>何もかもが、心に話しかけてくるじゃねぇか。<line-break>若いから、こんな山の中の暮らしより都会の方が<line-break>いいんだろうが、忘れちゃならねぇものが<line-break>こんなにすぐそばにあるんだな。<end>"
EN: "Listen. Isn't that a lovely sound? Birds singing, the babble of a brook, and the sound of a lumberjack felling trees. Doesn't it all speak to your heart? You're young, so you probably prefer the city to life in the mountains. But there are things you mustn't forget right here beside you.<end>"
KO: "이봐, 좋은 소리지? 새들의 지저귐, 시냇물 흐르는 소리, 그리고 나무꾼이 나무 베는 소리. 모든 게 마음에 말을 거는 것 같잖아. 젊으니 이런 산속 생활보다 도시가 좋겠지만, 잊어선 안 될 것들이 이렇게 바로 곁에 있는 거야.<end>"

## 1370143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？　カミさんとはどこで知り合ったかって？<line-break>変なこと聞くやつだな。もう、昔のことだから<line-break>忘れちまったよ。<line-break>いいじゃねぇか、別に。今がちゃんとあれば<line-break>昔のことなんてよ。<end>"
EN: "Huh? Where did I meet my wife? That's an odd thing to ask. It was so long ago, I've forgotten. What does it matter? As long as things are good now, who cares about the past?<end>"
KO: "응? 아내랑 어디서 만났냐고? 이상한 걸 묻는 녀석이네. 오래된 일이라 잊어버렸어. 지금이 제대로 있다면 옛날 일쯤은 아무래도 좋잖아.<end>"

## 1370144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前もカミさんもらうんだったら、　　<line-break>メシのうまい娘にするんだぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>冒険者やってて、<line-break>宿屋に泊まったら、何か暖かみのある<line-break>食事がしてぇとか思うだろ？<line-break>"
EN: "If you're going to take a wife, make sure she's a good cook. When you're off adventuring and staying at inns, don't you ever wish you could sit down to a nice, home-cooked meal?"
KO: "너도 아내를 맞을 거라면 밥 잘하는 아가씨로 해. 모험하다가 여관에 묵으면 뭔가 따뜻한 밥을 먹고 싶다고 생각하잖아?"

## 1370145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう食事がいつも待ってるってのが<line-break>どんなもんか、わかるだろ？<end>"
EN: "Well, imagine having a meal like that waiting for you every day. You know what I mean, don't you?<end>"
KO: "그런 식사가 늘 기다리고 있다는 게 어떤 건지 알겠지?<end>"

## 1370146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、だいたいお前<line-break>料理の腕の方はどうなんだ？　　<line-break>　<line-break>いい男を捕まえるなら<line-break>剣ばっかり振りまわしてねぇで、<line-break>料理の腕も振るった方がいいぜ。<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, how good are you in the kitchen? If you want to catch yourself a good man, don't just swing that sword around―try your hand at cooking too.<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야, 너요리 솜씨는 어느 정도야? 괜찮은 남자를 잡으려면검만 휘두르지 말고, 요리 솜씨도 발휘하는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 1370147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、おめえのエモノ<line-break>見せてみな。<end>"
EN: "All right, let's have a look at that weapon of yours.<end>"
KO: "어이, 네 무기 좀 보여 봐.<end>"

## 1370148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぉ、感心だな。手入れしてある　　<line-break>じゃねぇか。俺もよ、エモノの<line-break>手入れはばっちりなんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm impressed. You've been taking good care of it. I keep my own weapon in fine shape too, you know.<end>"
KO: "오, 기특하군. 손질해 뒀잖아. 나도 말이지, 내 무기 손질은 완벽하다고.<end>"

## 1370149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前はそいつに命を懸けてんだから、<line-break>大事にしなきゃな。<line-break>　<line-break>…ま、木こりの俺には、<line-break>命のやりとりなんかは<line-break>ちょっと遠い世界だがな。<end>"
EN: "You stake your life on that thing, so you'd better treat it well. Though for a lumberjack like me, fighting for your life is a world away.<end>"
KO: "넌 거기에 목숨을 걸고 있으니 소중히 해야지. 뭐, 나무꾼인 나한텐 목숨을 걸고 싸우는 건 먼 세상 얘기지만.<end>"

## 1370150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ～て、仕事だ、仕事。<line-break>じゃあな、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>暇があんなら、<line-break>カミさんのとこにも<line-break>顔を出していってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Right, time to get back to work. See you, <value:$28>. If you've got time to spare, stop by and see my wife too.<end>"
KO: "자~ 일이다, 일. 그럼 가 볼게, <value:$28>. 시간 있으면 아내한테도 얼굴 좀 비춰 줘.<end>"

## 1370151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあな、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前も冒険、がんばれよ。<end>"
EN: "See you around, <value:$28>. Good luck on your adventures.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 가, <value:$28>. 너도 모험 열심히 해.<end>"

## 1370152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔物に襲われたケガ人なら心配ない。<line-break>今、父上が神殿まで運んでゆかれた。<end>"
EN: "There's no need to worry about the person injured by the monsters. Father has taken the victim to the temple.<end>"
KO: "마물에게 습격당한 부상자라면 걱정 마. 지금 아버지가 신전으로 데려가셨어.<end>"

## 1370153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よし、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日の稽古は、<line-break>この辺にしておこうか。<end>"
EN: "All right, <value:$28>. Let's call it a day.<end>"
KO: "…좋아, <value:$28>. 오늘 수련은 이쯤 해 둘까.<end>"

## 1370154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正直言って、まだまだだな、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break>太刀筋に迷いがある。そのせいで、<line-break>速さ強さともに本来のものが<line-break>出せなくなっているようだ。<end>"
EN: "To be honest, you still have a long way to go, <value:$28>. There's hesitation in your swordplay, and it's keeping you from bringing your true speed and strength to bear.<end>"
KO: "솔직히 아직 멀었다, <value:$28>. 검로에 망설임이 있어. 그 탓에 속도와 힘 모두 제 실력이 나오지 않는 것 같군.<end>"

## 1370155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "落胆することはない。<line-break>実戦ではなく、私との訓練だけで<line-break>ここまで来たのだ。<line-break>お前の素質はたいしたものだと<line-break>思っているよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't be discouraged. You've come this far through training with me alone, without any real combat experience. You have remarkable potential.<end>"
KO: "낙담할 건 없다. 실전도 아닌 나와의 훈련만으로 여기까지 온 거다. 네 소질은 대단하다고 생각해.<end>"

## 1370156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、そうだな。<line-break>確かに、素質さえあれば<line-break>魔物に勝てるというわけではないな。<line-break>…いいだろう。<line-break>兄である私が教えられることならば、<line-break>何でも教えよう。<end>"
EN: "Heh. True enough. Potential alone won't let you defeat monsters. Very well. As your brother, I'll teach you anything I can.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇지. 확실히 소질만 있다고 마물에게 이길 수 있는 건 아니야. …좋아. 오빠인 내가 가르칠 수 있는 거라면 뭐든 가르쳐 주마.<end>"

## 1370157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "上手な戦い方は？<end>"
EN: "How can I fight more effectively?<end>"
KO: "잘 싸우는 법은?<end>"

## 1370158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を伸ばす方法は？<end>"
EN: "How can I become stronger?<end>"
KO: "힘을 키우는 방법은?<end>"

## 1370159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も聞くことはない<end>"
EN: "I don't have any questions.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 묻지 않는다<end>"

## 1370160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっているとは思うが、<line-break>万能な戦法なんてものは存在しない。<line-break>相手が魔物であれば、なおさらだ…。<line-break>その勝敗をわけるものは、<line-break>他でもない、おまえ自身の目だ。<end>"
EN: "You probably know this already, but there is no strategy that works against every opponent, least of all monsters. What decides victory or defeat is your own judgment.<end>"
KO: "알고 있겠지만 만능 전법 같은 건 없어. 상대가 마물이라면 더더욱 그렇지…. 승패를 가르는 건 다른 무엇도 아닌 네 눈이다.<end>"

## 1370161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どの敵にも必ず弱点がある。<line-break>それを見逃さず、的確に見破れれば、<line-break>戦いをうまく運べるだろう。<end>"
EN: "Every enemy has a weakness. If you keep your eyes open and identify it correctly, you can turn the battle to your advantage.<end>"
KO: "어떤 적에게도 반드시 약점은 있어. 그걸 놓치지 않고 정확히 간파하면 싸움을 유리하게 이끌 수 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1370162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣で攻撃すればいいのか、　　<line-break>魔法が有効なのか？<line-break><line-break>火、水、土、風の、<line-break>どの属性を苦手とする敵なのか？<end>"
EN: "Should you attack with a sword, or would magic be more effective? Is the enemy weak against fire, water, earth, or wind?<end>"
KO: "검으로 공격해야 하나, 마법이 효과적인가? 불, 물, 땅, 바람 중 어떤 속성에 약한 적인가?<end>"

## 1370163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間たちとも協力してそこを攻めれば、<line-break>必ずや突破口は開けるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Work with your companions to exploit that weakness, and you'll find a way through.<end>"
KO: "동료들과 협력해 그곳을 공격하면 반드시 돌파구가 열릴 것이다.<end>"

## 1370164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他には何かあるか？<end>"
EN: "Is there anything else?<end>"
KO: "다른 건 뭐가 있지?<end>"

## 1370165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはつまり、どのように<line-break>成長していきたいかということだな？<end>"
EN: "In other words, you're asking what path you should take as you grow?<end>"
KO: "그건 곧 어떤 식으로 성장하고 싶은가라는 뜻이지?<end>"

## 1370166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦いと人生はよく似ている。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>いや、人生こそ戦いである…<line-break>名うての冒険者などは、<line-break>誰しもそう思っているはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Battle and life are much alike. No, life itself is a battle... Any renowned adventurer must feel the same.<end>"
KO: "전투와 인생은 많이 닮았다. 아니, 인생이야말로 전투지…. 이름난 모험자라면 누구나 그렇게 생각할 거다.<end>"

## 1370167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし逆に、戦士は戦士でしかなく、<line-break>魔道士は魔道士でしかないのは<line-break>どうしてだと思う？<end>"
EN: "But then why do you think a warrior remains only a warrior, and a mage only a mage?<end>"
KO: "하지만 반대로 전사는 전사일 뿐이고 마도사는 마도사일 뿐인 건 왜라고 생각하나?<end>"

## 1370168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが彼らの生き方<end>"
EN: "That's their way of life.<end>"
KO: "그게 그들의 삶의 방식이다<end>"

## 1370169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1370170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくわかっているな。<line-break>そう、それも、<line-break>彼らが自ら決めた生き方なのだ。　<line-break>世界は広い。<line-break>もちろん、魔法が得意な戦士も、<line-break>前列で戦える魔道士もいるだろう。<end>"
EN: "You understand. Yes, that too is a way of life they chose for themselves. The world is vast. Of course there are warriors skilled in magic, and mages who can fight on the front line.<end>"
KO: "잘 알고 있군. 그래, 그것도 그들이 스스로 정한 삶의 방식이다. 세계는 넓다. 물론 마법이 특기인 전사도, 전열에서 싸우는 마도사도 있겠지.<end>"

## 1370171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし彼らは、多くの場合、<line-break>第一級の戦士や魔道士ではない　　　<line-break>ことが多い。<line-break>万能であろうとすればするほど、<line-break>人よりも多い苦労を強いられるからだ。<end>"
EN: "But more often than not, they never become first-rate warriors or mages. The more they strive to master everything, the harder they must work compared with others.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그들은 많은 경우 일류 전사나 마도사가 아니다. 만능이 되려 하면 할수록 남보다 더 큰 고생을 강요받기 때문이다.<end>"

## 1370172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それゆえに価値が大きいというのは<line-break>もちろんあると思うぞ。<line-break>弱点のない仲間は、貴重だからな。<line-break>つまるところ、どうあるべきなのかは、<line-break>お前自身が決めることなのだ。<line-break>自由がある…だからこそ難しい。<end>"
EN: "Of course, that also makes such people all the more valuable. A companion with no weaknesses is a rare asset. In the end, you must decide for yourself what you wish to become. You are free to choose. That is precisely what makes it so difficult.<end>"
KO: "그렇기에 가치가 크다는 것도 물론 있겠지. 약점이 없는 동료는 귀중하니까. 결국 어떻게 해야 할지는 네가 직접 정하는 거다. 자유가 있다… 그렇기에 어려운 거지.<end>"

## 1370173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフッ、面白い答えだな。<line-break><value:$28>のそういう独創性は<line-break>大事にした方がいいと私も思うよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. That's an interesting answer. I think you should cherish that kind of originality, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 재미있는 답이군. <value:$28>의 그런 독창성은 소중히 하는 게 좋다고 나도 생각해.<end>"

## 1370174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに、パーティとして一緒に<line-break>旅をする身としては、<line-break>煮え切らない便利屋がいるよりも、<line-break>これだけは誰にも負けないという<line-break>メンバーがいた方が頼りになる。<line-break>実際に旅に出れば嫌でもわかることだ。<end>"
EN: "Certainly, when traveling together as a party, it is more reassuring to have a member who excels beyond anyone else at one thing than a half-baked jack-of-all-trades. Once you set out on a journey, you'll understand it whether you like it or not.<end>"
KO: "확실히 파티로 함께 여행하는 입장에선 애매한 만능잡이보다 이것 하나만큼은 누구에게도 안 진다는 멤버가 있는 편이 든든하지. 실제로 여행을 떠나면 싫어도 알게 될 거야.<end>"

## 1370175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その言葉、信用して大丈夫だな？<end>"
EN: "I can take you at your word, then?<end>"
KO: "그 말, 믿어도 되는 거지?<end>"

## 1370176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと待って<end>"
EN: "Actually, wait.<end>"
KO: "잠깐 기다려<end>"

## 1370177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫<end>"
EN: "You can trust me.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아<end>"

## 1370178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あわてることはない。<line-break>わからないことがあったら聞くんだぞ。<end>"
EN: "There's no need to rush. If there's anything you don't understand, just ask.<end>"
KO: "서두를 것 없다. 모르는 게 있으면 물어봐.<end>"

## 1370179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。ではまた、<line-break>機会があれば教えよう。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I'll teach you another time, if the opportunity arises.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그럼 다음에 기회가 있으면 가르쳐 주지.<end>"

## 1370180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "기운의 약이 부족해질지도 몰라. 조금 모아서 돌아가자.<end>"

## 1370182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml

## 1370186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結界か…。<line-break>モンスターを全滅させるまで<line-break>逃げられないようだな。<end>"

## 1370187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これほどの実力がありながら、<line-break>あの女はなぜ直接私たちを殺さない？<line-break>こんな…まるでもてあそぶような…。<line-break>…<value:$28>、<line-break>とにかくモンスターを全滅させよう。<line-break>胸騒ぎがする。<end>"
EN: "With power like that, why doesn't that woman kill us herself? This is almost as if she's toying with us... <value:$28>, for now, let's kill all the monsters. I have a bad feeling about this.<end>"
KO: "…이 정도 실력이 있으면서 그 여자는 왜 직접 우리를 죽이지 않는 거지? 이렇게… 마치 가지고 노는 것처럼…. …<value:$28>, 일단 몬스터를 전멸시키자. 불길한 예감이 들어.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の復活の時が来たら<line-break>魔人どもが神器をねらってくる。<line-break>　<line-break>そのときはお前も、<line-break>神器を守る一族のひとりとして<line-break>魔人や魔物と戦わねばならない。<end>"
EN: "When the time comes for the God of Destruction to return, the fiends will come for the sacred artifact. When that happens, you too must fight the fiends and monsters as a member of the clan that guards it.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 부활의 때가 오면 마인들이 신기를 노릴 거다. 그때는 너도 신기를 지키는 일족의 한 사람으로서 마인과 마물과 싸워야 한다.<end>"

## 1370201

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、今日のように、<line-break>戦いに出る機会が増えるだろう。<line-break> <line-break>自身の鍛練もそうだが、<line-break>装備も強くしておかねばならない。<end>"

## 1370231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鍛冶屋の経験がある男が、<line-break>村のどこかにいたはずだ。<line-break> <line-break>今は仕事を断っているらしいが<line-break>その錬剛石とギアを持っていって頼めば<line-break>鍛冶をしてくれるかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "There should be a man somewhere in the village with experience as a blacksmith. I hear he is refusing work at present, but if you take him that Tempering Stone and the Gea and ask him, he may agree to do some smithing for you.<end>"
KO: "대장장이 경험이 있는 남자가 마을 어딘가에 있었을 거야. 지금은 일을 거절한다지만 그 연강석과 기어를 들고 부탁하면 대장일을 해 줄지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1370232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370233

## 1370247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

Are you going to save your brother?<end>I should have expected no less from the younger sister of a man I respect. You handled yourself well. Are you going to save your brother?<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、<line-break>私が管理するはずの<line-break>大切なものをもらいに来たの。<line-break>神殿でたずねてみたけど、<line-break>誰も何も教えてくれないの。<line-break>くすくす…。ひどい話よね。<end>"
EN: "I've come to collect something precious that was supposed to be in my care. I asked around at the temple, but no one would tell me anything. Hee hee... Isn't that awful?<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 관리해야 할 소중한 것을 받으러 왔어. 신전에서 물어봤지만 아무도 아무것도 안 알려 주더라. 후후… 너무하잖아.<end>"

## 1370264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、あなた、<line-break>ウソをついていたでしょう？<line-break>知りませんだなんて。<line-break>くすくす、あれ、<line-break>あなたのおうちにあるんじゃない。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. You lied to me, didn't you? You said you didn't know. Hee hee. But it's at your house, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "맞다, 당신 거짓말했죠? 모른다니. 후후, 그거 당신 집에 있잖아요.<end>"

## 1370265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのお父様の死体から、<line-break>記憶をじかに探らせてもらったわ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I probed your father's memories directly from his corpse.<end>"
KO: "네 아버지의 시체에서 기억을 직접 읽어 냈어.<end>"

## 1370266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰に殺されるのかもわからずに<line-break>死んでいくのはかわいそうだから<line-break>教えてあげる。<line-break>私は破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士、<line-break>心をなくすものアーギルシャイア。<line-break>じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "It would be a shame for you to die without even knowing who killed you, so I'll tell you. I am Argyleshire, She Who Has Lost Her Heart, a Knight of the Round Table serving Ulugh, God of Destruction. Bye.<end>"
KO: "누구에게 죽는지도 모르고 죽는 건 불쌍하니까 알려 줄게. 나는 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사, 마음을 잃은 자 아르길샤이어. 그럼 잘 가.<end>"

## 1370267

## 1370274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "武器を収めろ。<line-break>俺はここに<line-break>旧友を訪ねてきただけだ。<line-break>殺すつもりなら<line-break>お前が目を覚ます前に<line-break>やっている。<end>"

## 1370275

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…フ、なるほどな。<line-break>お前がロイの弟か。<line-break>ロイから名前は聞いている。<line-break>俺はセラ。<line-break>ロイの友だ。<end>…フ、なるほどな。<line-break>お前がロイの妹か。<line-break>ロイから名前は聞いている。<line-break>俺はセラ。<line-break>ロイの友だ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hmph. So you're Roy's brother. He told me your name. I'm Sera, a friend of Roy's.<end>Hmph. So you're Roy's sister. He told me your name. I'm Sera, a friend of Roy's.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…후, 그렇군. 네가 로이의 남동생인가. 로이에게 이름은 들었다. 나는 세라. 로이의 친구다.<end>…후, 그렇군. 네가 로이의 여동생인가. 로이에게 이름은 들었다. 나는 세라. 로이의 친구다.<end>"

## 1370278

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml

## 1370280

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "으… 뭐야, 사람이 기분 좋게 자고 있는데.<end>"

## 1370281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん…俺があ？<line-break>鍛冶屋だったんじゃないかって？<line-break>冗談言っちゃいけねえ。<line-break>何を見てそんな…<end>"
EN: "Hmm...? Me? You think I used to be a blacksmith? Don't be ridiculous. What makes you think―<end>"
KO: "응… 내가? 대장장이였던 거 아니냐고? 농담도 마. 뭘 보고 그런….<end>"

## 1370282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっ、この指かよ。<line-break>やれやれ、<line-break>これを見られちゃしょうがねえな。　<line-break>いかにもこいつは、<line-break>長年ハンマーを振るい続けた、<line-break>鍛冶屋の手指だ。<end>"
EN: "Hah, these fingers gave me away? Well, now that you've seen them, there's no use denying it. These are a blacksmith's fingers, all right, shaped by years of swinging a hammer.<end>"
KO: "흥, 이 손가락 말이야? 이런, 이걸 들켰으니 어쩔 수 없군. 그래, 이건 오랫동안 망치를 휘둘러 온 대장장이의 손이다.<end>"

## 1370283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、俺は今、<line-break>ごらんの通りの飲んだくれだ。<line-break>何か用かい？<end>"
EN: "But now, as you can see, I'm just a drunk. What do you want?<end>"
KO: "하지만 난 지금 보다시피 술주정뱅이다. 무슨 볼일이냐?<end>"

## 1370284

KO: "일을 부탁하고 싶어<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "鍛冶屋について教えてほしい<end>"
EN: "Tell me about blacksmithing<end>"
KO: "대장간에 대해 알려 줬으면 해<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたと話をしてて、<line-break>久々にハンマーが握りたくなってきた。<line-break>俺も…やり直せるのかもな…。<line-break>ありがとよ。<end>"
EN: "Talking to you has made me want to pick up a hammer again. Maybe... maybe I can start over. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "너랑 이야기하다 보니 오랜만에 망치를 잡고 싶어졌어. 나도… 다시 시작할 수 있을지도 모르겠군…. 고맙다.<end>"

## 1370288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかくだが、そいつは無理だ…。<line-break>今の俺には自信も道具もねえ。<line-break>だが、そういってくれるのは嬉しいよ。<line-break>お前さん、見たとこそんなに<line-break>鍛冶屋のことを知らないんだろ？<line-break>俺の話を聞いて勉強していくといい。<end>"
EN: "I appreciate the offer, but I can't do it. Not now. I've got neither the confidence nor the tools. Still, I'm glad you asked. From the look of you, you don't know much about smithing, do you? Listen to what I've got to say and learn a thing or two.<end>"
KO: "모처럼이지만 그건 무리야…. 지금의 나에겐 자신도 도구도 없어. 그래도 그렇게 말해 주니 기쁘군. 보아하니 대장장이에 대해 잘 모르지? 내 이야기를 듣고 배워 가면 돼.<end>"

## 1370289

EN: "So, what do you want to hear about?<end>"

## 1370290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうどひとりで飲むのも<line-break>飽きたんで、話し相手が<line-break>欲しかったとこだ。<end>"
EN: "I was just getting tired of drinking alone and could use someone to talk to.<end>"
KO: "마침 혼자 마시는 것도 질렸고, 이야기 상대가 필요하던 참이야.<end>"

## 1370291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにお前さん、<line-break>見るからに駆け出しだよな。<line-break> <line-break>よし。<line-break>俺の話を聞いて<line-break>鍛冶屋のことを勉強しなよ。<end>"
EN: "Besides, anyone can see you're a beginner. All right. Listen closely, and I'll teach you about smithing.<end>"
KO: "게다가 자네, 보기에도 완전 초보잖아. 좋아. 내 얘기를 듣고 대장간에 대해 배워 둬.<end>"

## 1370292

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I know all of that<end>"
KO: "전부 알고 있어<end>"

## 1370298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのまんまのことだ。<line-break>武器や防具の頑丈さのことだわな。<line-break> <line-break>金属って奴は、俺ら鍛冶屋が<line-break>ぶっ叩けば叩くほど、<line-break>ねばりが出て頑丈になりやがる。<end>"
EN: "Exactly what it sounds like: how sturdy a weapon or piece of armor is. Metal gets tougher and stronger the more we blacksmiths pound on it.<end>"
KO: "말 그대로야. 무기와 방어구가 얼마나 튼튼한지를 말하지. 금속은 우리 대장장이가 두들길수록 질겨지고 단단해져.<end>"

## 1370299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強度が高いほうが、<line-break>攻撃力や防御力が増すってのは<line-break>言うまでもねえ。<line-break>で、そこに、錬剛石って特別な鉱石を<line-break>あわせて叩き直す…言うなれば<line-break>ドワーフ流が今の主流だ。<end>"
EN: "It goes without saying that greater strength means greater Attack Power or defense. These days, the usual method is to reforge the piece with a special ore called Tempering Stone―the Dwarf method, you might say.<end>"
KO: "강도가 높을수록 공격력과 방어력이 올라가는 건 말할 필요도 없지. 거기에 연강석이라는 특별한 광석을 섞어 다시 두드려 만드는… 이른바 드워프식이 지금의 주류야.<end>"

## 1370300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただし、鍛えれば鍛えるほど、<line-break>武器の重量が重くなって扱いが<line-break>難しくなることは覚悟しとけよ。<end>"
EN: "But be warned: the more you strengthen a weapon, the heavier it gets and the harder it becomes to handle.<end>"
KO: "다만 단련하면 할수록 무기의 무게가 늘어나 다루기 어려워진다는 건 각오해 둬.<end>"

## 1370301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、買ったままの武器でも<line-break>そこそこ戦えるようには鍛えてある。<line-break>だが、さらに鍛えたかったら、<line-break>錬剛石とギアがなけりゃ、<line-break>話にならねえ。<end>"
EN: "A weapon straight from the shop has already been forged well enough to hold its own. But if you want to strengthen it further, you'll need Tempering Stone and Gea. Without both, forget it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 산 그대로인 무기도 어느 정도 싸울 수 있게는 단련돼 있다. 하지만 더 단련하려면 연강석과 기어가 없으면 이야기가 안 되지.<end>"

## 1370302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ズバリ、エモノの形のことだな。<line-break>使ってる武器がいまいち好みじゃねえ<line-break>…そんな時は、鍛冶屋の出番だ。<line-break>代金さえもらえりゃ、ある程度、<line-break>お前さんの好みに従って形を<line-break>仕立て直すことが可能だ。<end>"
EN: "Put simply, it means the shape of your weapon. If the weapon you're using isn't quite to your taste, that's where a blacksmith comes in. Pay the price, and we can reshape it to suit your preferences, within reason.<end>"
KO: "딱 말하자면 무기의 형태 말이지. 쓰는 무기가 영 취향이 아니다… 그럴 땐 대장장이 차례다. 대금만 받으면 어느 정도는 네 취향에 맞춰 형태를 다시 만들 수 있어.<end>"

## 1370303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じ剣にしても、もっと細身がいいとか<line-break>まっすぐなのがいいとか、あるだろ？<line-break>そういうことだよ。<line-break>もちろん、<line-break>昔の形に戻すことだってできるぜ。<line-break>便利だろ？<end>"
EN: "Even with swords, you might want a slimmer blade or a straighter one, right? That's what I mean. And of course, we can restore its old shape too. Handy, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "같은 검이라도 좀 더 가느다란 게 좋다거나 곧은 게 좋다거나 있잖아? 그런 거야. 물론 예전 형태로 되돌리는 것도 가능해. 편리하지?<end>"

## 1370304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、効果のことな。<line-break>お前さん、<line-break>ハチに刺されたことがあるかい？<end>"
EN: "Ah, effects. Ever been stung by a bee?<end>"
KO: "아, 효과 말이지. 너 벌에 쏘여 본 적 있나?<end>"

## 1370305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かゆくなったり痛くなったり…<line-break>そうなるのは、<line-break>ハチの針に毒があるからだな。<line-break>武器に効果をつけるってのは、<line-break>例えるなら、お前さんの剣が<line-break>ハチの針みてえになるってことよ。<end>"
EN: "It itches or hurts because there's venom in the bee's stinger. Giving a weapon an effect is like turning your sword into a bee's stinger.<end>"
KO: "가렵거나 아프거나… 그런 건 벌침에 독이 있기 때문이야. 무기에 효과를 붙인다는 건 쉽게 말하면 네 검이 벌침처럼 되는 거지.<end>"

## 1370306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "刺したと同時に敵を眠らせちまう槍や、<line-break>斬ることで相手を麻痺させる剣、<line-break>なんてのも作れる。<line-break>防具はその逆だ。<line-break>石化や呪いからお前さんを<line-break>守ってくれる防具も作れる。<end>"
EN: "We can make a spear that puts an enemy to sleep the moment it strikes, or a sword that inflicts Paralysis when it cuts. Armor works the other way around: we can make armor that protects you from petrification or curses.<end>"
KO: "찌르는 동시에 적을 잠재우는 창이나, 베면 상대를 마비시키는 검 같은 것도 만들 수 있어. 방어구는 그 반대지. 석화나 저주로부터 너를 지켜 주는 방어구도 만들 수 있다.<end>"

## 1370307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつは難しい仕事だから、<line-break>それなりのギアをいただくが、<line-break>重宝するぜ。<line-break>強度や形状を変えてもまだ…<line-break>って時には、<line-break>ぜひ試してみてくれよ。<end>"
EN: "It's difficult work, so it'll cost you a fair bit of Gea, but it comes in mighty useful. If changing strength and shape still isn't enough, give it a try.<end>"
KO: "이건 어려운 일이니 그만큼 기어를 받지만 아주 쓸모 있어. 강도나 형상을 바꿔도 아직 부족하다 싶을 때 꼭 시험해 봐.<end>"

## 1370308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちにとっても、<line-break>いい稼ぎになるしな。<end>"
EN: "Makes us a tidy profit, too.<end>"
KO: "우리한테도 좋은 벌이가 되니까.<end>"

## 1370309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははは！<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "하하하!<end>"

## 1370310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊属性のことか。<line-break>こいつだけは本来、<line-break>鍛冶屋の領分じゃねえ。<line-break>神殿に言って頼むことだ。<line-break>ま、俺でも、<line-break>説明くらいはできるが…。<end>"
EN: "Spirit Attributes, eh? Strictly speaking, those aren't a blacksmith's business. You'll have to ask at a temple. Still, I can at least explain them.<end>"
KO: "정령 속성 말인가. 이건 원래 대장장이의 영역이 아니야. 신전에 가서 부탁해야 하지. 뭐, 나도 설명 정도는 할 수 있지만….<end>"

## 1370311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "精霊属性ってのはだな、<line-break>火や水といった精霊の力を<line-break>武器や防具に宿すことさ。<line-break>精霊の属性については<line-break>もう知ってるのかい？<end>"
EN: "A Spirit Attribute imbues a weapon or piece of armor with the power of a spirit, such as Fire or Water. Do you already know how Spirit Attributes work?<end>"
KO: "정령 속성이란 불이나 물 같은 정령의 힘을 무기나 방어구에 깃들게 하는 거야. 정령 속성에 대해선 이미 알고 있나?<end>"

## 1370312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知らない<end>"
EN: "No, I don't<end>"
KO: "몰라<end>"

## 1370313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知っている<end>"
EN: "Yes, I do<end>"
KO: "알고 있다<end>"

## 1370314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火、水、土、風…<line-break>この四種類が精霊の属性だ。<line-break> <line-break>聖母神がお作りになったこの四属性が<line-break>互いに干渉しあい、循環することで<line-break>世界は成り立ってるのさ。<end>"
EN: "The four Spirit Attributes are Fire, Water, Earth, and Wind. The world exists because these four attributes, created by the Mother Goddess, interact with one another in an endless cycle.<end>"
KO: "불, 물, 땅, 바람… 이 네 가지가 정령의 속성이다. 성모신이 만든 이 네 속성이 서로 간섭하고 순환하면서 세계가 이루어져 있지.<end>"

## 1370315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火は水に強く、水は土に強く、<line-break>土は風に、風は火に強い。<line-break> <line-break>わかるだろ、ぐるぐる回ってんだ。<line-break>どれが圧倒的に優位ということはなく、<line-break>うまくバランスをとってるんだな。<end>"
EN: "Fire is strong against Water, Water against Earth, Earth against Wind, and Wind against Fire. See? It goes round and round. None has an overwhelming advantage; they keep each other in balance.<end>"
KO: "불은 물에 강하고, 물은 땅에 강하며, 땅은 바람에, 바람은 불에 강해. 알겠지? 빙글빙글 도는 거야. 어느 하나가 압도적으로 유리한 게 아니라 절묘하게 균형을 이루고 있지.<end>"

## 1370316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、魔法なんかもすべて<line-break>火、水、土、風の四属性を<line-break>元にしているからな。<line-break>お前さんにそういう知識があるなら<line-break>まず問題はねえだろ。<end>"
EN: "All magic is based on those same four elements―Fire, Water, Earth, and Wind. If you know how those work, you shouldn't have much trouble.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 마법도 전부 불, 물, 땅, 바람 네 속성을 바탕으로 하니까. 네게 그런 지식이 있다면 별문제 없을 거야.<end>"

## 1370317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "装備の精霊属性を変更するってことは、<line-break>この四属性の性質をそのまま装備に<line-break>盛り込むってことだ。<line-break>例えば、お前さんが火属性の剣を持ち、<line-break>火属性の鎧を身につけたとする。<end>"
EN: "Changing equipment's Spirit Attribute means giving it the properties of one of those four elements. Say you carry a Fire-element sword and wear Fire-element armor.<end>"
KO: "장비의 정령 속성을 바꾼다는 건 이 네 속성의 성질을 그대로 장비에 담는다는 뜻이야. 예를 들어 네가 불 속성 검을 들고 불 속성 갑옷을 입었다고 하자.<end>"

## 1370318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんは水属性の相手に対して<line-break>圧倒的に有利に戦うことができ…<line-break> <line-break>逆に、火属性のダメージを<line-break>大幅に軽減することも可能だ。<line-break>なっ、こりゃすげえことだろ？<end>"
EN: "You'd have an overwhelming advantage against a Water-element foe, while also taking far less Fire-element damage. Impressive, eh?<end>"
KO: "넌 수속성 상대에게 압도적으로 유리하게 싸울 수 있고… 반대로 화속성 피해도 크게 줄일 수 있어. 대단하지?<end>"

## 1370319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらにこの精霊属性ってのには、<line-break>装備品ごと、最初から決まった強弱…<line-break>いわゆるレベルってもんがある。<line-break>同じ火属性を宿したとしても、<line-break>片手剣なら４、槍なら２、<line-break>ナックルだと６、って具合になる。<end>"
EN: "What's more, every piece of equipment has a fixed strength for its Spirit Attribute―a level, in other words. Even with the same Fire element, it might be 4 for a one-handed sword, 2 for a spear, and 6 for knuckles.<end>"
KO: "그리고 이 정령 속성에는 장비마다 처음부터 정해진 강약… 이른바 레벨이라는 게 있어. 같은 화속성을 지녀도 한손검은 4, 창은 2, 권구는 6 같은 식이지.<end>"

## 1370320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、単に属性のレベルの数値が<line-break>高けりゃいいのかっていうと、<line-break>そんな底の浅い話でもない。<line-break>忘れないでくれよ。<line-break>精霊ＬＶが高いということは、<line-break>それだけ純粋に近いという証だ。<end>"
EN: "But don't think a higher attribute level is always better. It's not that simple. Remember this: a high Spirit ＬＶ means the element is that much closer to purity.<end>"
KO: "하지만 단순히 속성 레벨 수치가 높기만 하면 좋으냐 하면 그렇게 얕은 얘기도 아니야. 잊지 마. 정령 LV가 높다는 건 그만큼 순수함에 가깝다는 증거야.<end>"

## 1370321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "火属性の剣では、同じ火属性の敵を<line-break>傷つけることは難しく、火属性の鎧は、<line-break>風属性の攻撃を、倍ににも受けちまう。<line-break>その効果が、さらに、<line-break>属性のレベルによって変動する。<line-break>…ま、臨機応変に、ってことだな。<end>"
EN: "A Fire-element sword has trouble harming a Fire-element enemy, while Fire-element armor makes you take twice as much damage from Wind-element attacks. The attribute level changes the extent of those effects too. So adapt to the situation.<end>"
KO: "불 속성 검으로는 같은 불 속성 적을 상처 입히기 어렵고, 불 속성 갑옷은 바람 속성 공격을 두 배로 받지. 그 효과는 속성 레벨에 따라서도 달라진다. …뭐, 임기응변으로 하라는 거야.<end>"

## 1370322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうでえ、酔っ払ってるとは思えねえ<line-break>さわやかな弁舌だろ？<line-break>鍛冶のことなら任せとけってんだ。<end>"
EN: "Well? Pretty lucid for a drunk, wasn't I? When it comes to smithing, you can count on me.<end>"
KO: "어때, 취했다곤 생각 못 할 만큼 상쾌한 말솜씨지? 대장장이 일이라면 맡겨 두라고.<end>"

## 1370323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に大丈夫だな？<end>"
EN: "You're sure you'll be all right?<end>"
KO: "정말 괜찮은 거지?<end>"

## 1370324

## 1370326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よしきた。<line-break>じゃ、何を話そうか？<end>"
EN: "All right, then. What shall we talk about?<end>"
KO: "좋아. 그럼 무슨 이야기를 할까?<end>"

## 1370327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほっほう、そいつはたいしたもんだ。<line-break>改めて俺に仕事を頼むときも、<line-break>迷わずに済むな。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. That's impressive. Next time you come to hire me, you won't have any trouble deciding what to ask for.<end>"
KO: "호오, 그건 대단한데. 다음에 내가 다시 일을 부탁할 때도 고민할 필요 없겠군.<end>"

## 1370328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたと話をしてて、<line-break>久々にハンマーが握りたくなってきた。<line-break>俺も…やり直せるのかもな…。<end>"
EN: "Talking to you has made me want to pick up a hammer again. Maybe... maybe I can start over.<end>"
KO: "너랑 이야기하다 보니 오랜만에 망치를 쥐고 싶어졌어. 나도… 다시 시작할 수 있을지도 모르겠군….<end>"

## 1370329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けっ、酔っ払いを<line-break>からかいたかっただけかよ。<line-break> <line-break>ひとりにしといてくれってんだ。<line-break>あばよ。<end>"
EN: "Bah. So you only wanted to make fun of a drunk. Leave me alone. See ya.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 취객을 놀리고 싶었을 뿐이냐. 혼자 있게 해 줘. 잘 가.<end>"

## 1370330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お帰りなさいませ。　　　　<line-break>おケガは？<end>"
EN: "Welcome home, <value:$28>. Are you hurt?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님, 돌아오셨군요. 다치신 곳은요?<end>"

## 1370331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご無事で何よりです。<line-break>お父上のダディアス大神官が<line-break>神殿内でお待ちです。<end>"
EN: "I'm relieved to see you safe. Your father, High Priest Dadias, is waiting for you inside the temple.<end>"
KO: "무사하셔서 다행입니다. 아버님이신 다디아스 대신관께서 신전 안에서 기다리고 계십니다.<end>"

## 1370332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3ロイ様ですか？<line-break>もう、広場か森に行かれたのでは？<end><value:$28>様もロイ様も<line-break>このミイスの村の大神官ダディアス様も、<line-break>神器の守護者と呼ばれるご一族。<line-break>闇の神器という危険な宝物を<line-break>破壊神の騎士と呼ばれる魔人どもから<line-break>守るのがお役目なのです。<end>今の地震といい、<line-break>最近うろつきだした魔物どもといい、<line-break>魔人が神器をねらっているのでしょうか？　<line-break>しかし心配はご無用ですぞ。<line-break>きっと天空神ノトゥーンの加護があります。<line-break>このじいもついておりますしな。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Master Roy? Hasn't he already gone to the square or the forest?<end>You, <value:$28>, Master Roy, and Lord Dadias, High Priest of Miis Village, belong to the family known as the Guardians of the Divine Artifact. Your duty is to protect the dangerous treasure called the Dark Artifact from the demons known as the Knights of the God of Destruction.<end>With that earthquake just now, and the monsters that have begun prowling about of late, could the demons be after the Divine Artifact? But you need not worry. The Sky God Notun will surely protect us―and this old soldier is with you as well.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3로이 님 말씀이십니까? 벌써 광장이나 숲으로 가신 것 아닐까요?<end><value:$28> 님도 로이 님도 이 미이스 마을의 대신관 다디아스 님도 신기의 수호자라 불리는 일족입니다. 어둠의 신기라는 위험한 보물을 파괴신의 기사라 불리는 마인들로부터 지키는 것이 그분들의 역할이지요.<end>방금 지진도 그렇고, 최근 돌아다니기 시작한 마물들도 그렇고, 마인들이 신기를 노리고 있는 걸까요? 하지만 걱정하실 필요 없습니다. 분명 천공신 노툰의 가호가 있을 겁니다. 이 늙은이도 곁에 있고요.<end>"

## 1370333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3神器とはどのようなものかは、<line-break>その時期が来れば、<line-break>お父上が教えてくださいますよ。　　<line-break>こればかりは神器を守る神官家にのみ<line-break>伝わる秘伝ですからな。<line-break>じいも存じませんのです。<end><value:$28>様もロイ様も<line-break>大神官ダディアス様も、<line-break>このミイスの村の神官家の方々はみな<line-break>闇の神器という危険な宝物を、<line-break>破壊神の騎士と呼ばれる魔人どもから<line-break>守る宿命を負っておられるのです。<end>今の地震といい、<line-break>最近うろつきだした魔物どもといい…、<line-break>魔人が神器をねらっているのでしょうか？　<line-break>しかし心配はご無用ですぞ。<line-break>きっと天空神ノトゥーンの加護があります。<line-break>このじいもついておりますしな。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3When the time comes, your father will tell you what the Divine Artifact is. That knowledge is a secret handed down only within the priestly family charged with guarding it. Even I do not know.<end>You, <value:$28>, Master Roy, High Priest Dadias, and all the members of Miis Village's priestly family are fated to protect the dangerous treasure called the Dark Artifact from the demons known as the Knights of the God of Destruction.<end>With that earthquake just now, and the monsters that have begun prowling about of late... could the demons be after the Divine Artifact? But you need not worry. The Sky God Notun will surely protect us―and this old soldier is with you as well.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3신기가 어떤 물건인지는 때가 되면 아버님께서 알려 주실 겁니다. 이것만큼은 신기를 지키는 신관 가문에만 전해지는 비전이라 이 늙은이도 모릅니다.<end><value:$28> 님도 로이 님도 대신관 다디아스 님도, 이 미이스 마을의 신관 가문 사람들은 모두 어둠의 신기라는 위험한 보물을 파괴신의 기사라 불리는 마인들로부터 지킬 숙명을 짊어지고 계십니다.<end>이번 지진이며 최근 돌아다니기 시작한 마물들이며… 마인이 신기를 노리는 걸까요? 하지만 걱정하실 것 없습니다. 분명 천공신 노툰의 가호가 있을 겁니다. 이 늙은이도 곁에 있고요.<end>"

## 1370334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父なる天空神ノトゥーンと<line-break>愛の神ライラネートのご加護あらんことを。<line-break><value:$28>様、よくぞご無事で。<line-break>さ、早く神殿へ。<line-break>お父上のダディアス様が<line-break>心配なさっておられますよ。<end>"
EN: "May Father Notun, the Sky God, and Lailanate, God of Love, watch over you. <value:$28>, I'm so glad you're safe. Quickly, to the temple. Your father, Lord Dadias, is worried about you.<end>"
KO: "아버지이신 천공신 노툰과 사랑의 신 라이라네트의 가호가 있기를. <value:$28> 님, 무사하셔서 다행입니다. 자, 어서 신전으로. 아버님 다디아스 님께서 걱정하고 계십니다.<end>"

## 1370335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3<value:$28>様に<line-break>愛の神ライラネートのご加護を…。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、<value:$28>様の敵に<line-break>天空神ノトゥーンの<line-break>裁きのいかづちを…。<end>このミイスの村は<line-break>闇の神器を闇のしもべから<line-break>守るための隠れ里…。<line-break>闇の神器は破壊神の復活に使われるもの。<line-break>だから、破壊神のしもべがねらうのです。<end>私もこの神殿の神器が<line-break>どのようなものかは存じません。<line-break>　<line-break>隠れて伝わる伝承では<line-break>記憶を失うかわりに<line-break>すさまじい力を手に入れられるとか…。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3May Lailanate, God of Love, watch over you, <value:$28>... and may Notun, the Sky God, strike the enemies of <value:$28> with the thunderbolt of judgment.<end>Miis Village is a hidden settlement meant to protect the Dark Artifact from the servants of darkness. The artifact can be used to revive the God of Destruction. That is why those servants seek it.<end>Even I do not know what form the Divine Artifact in this temple takes. According to a secret legend, it grants tremendous power in exchange for one's memories...<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3<value:$28> 님께 사랑의 신 라이라네트의 가호를…. 그리고 <value:$28> 님의 적에게 천공신 노툰의 심판의 번개를….<end>이 미이스 마을은 어둠의 신기를 어둠의 종복으로부터 지키기 위한 은신처…. 어둠의 신기는 파괴신의 부활에 쓰이는 것. 그래서 파괴신의 종복들이 노리는 겁니다.<end>저도 이 신전의 신기가 어떤 것인지는 모릅니다. 비밀리에 전해지는 전승에 따르면 기억을 잃는 대신 엄청난 힘을 얻을 수 있다고 합니다….<end>"

## 1370336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アンティノ商会が極秘開発中の<line-break>戦闘用モンスター…デスギガース。<line-break>…やはり、そういうことか。<end>"
EN: "Death Gigas... the combat monster being developed in utmost secrecy by the Antino Trading Company. So that is what happened.<end>"
KO: "…안티노 상회가 극비 개발 중인 전투용 몬스터… 데스기가스. …역시 그런 거였나.<end>"

## 1370337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…心配はいらん。<line-break>こいつはすでに機能停止…<line-break>つまり、死んでいる。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. It has already ceased functioning. In other words, it's dead.<end>"
KO: "…걱정 마라. 이 녀석은 이미 기능 정지… 즉 죽어 있다.<end>"

## 1370338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロイほどの手練れでも倒すのは難しい。<line-break>だが、俺がただひとり認めた<line-break>あの男以上に腕の立つ男は存在しない。<line-break>ロイがこれを倒したのだな。<end>"
EN: "Even someone as skilled as Roy would have struggled to defeat it. But no man is more skilled than that man, the only one I have ever acknowledged. Roy must have defeated it.<end>"
KO: "로이 정도의 실력자라도 쓰러뜨리긴 어렵다. 하지만 내가 유일하게 인정한 그 남자보다 뛰어난 남자는 없다. 로이가 이걸 쓰러뜨린 거군.<end>"

## 1370339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "月光に反応はない。<line-break>ロイはここにはいないようだな。<end>"
EN: "Moonlight shows no response. It seems Roy isn't here.<end>"
KO: "월광에 반응이 없다. 로이는 여기 없는 모양이군.<end>"

## 1370340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは妖刀・月光。<line-break>ロイはこれと対をなす剣を<line-break>持っている。<end>"
EN: "This is the Demon Blade “Moonlight.” Roy carries its twin.<end>"
KO: "이것은 요도 월광. 로이는 이것과 짝을 이루는 검을 가지고 있다.<end>"

## 1370341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、デスギガースを倒したあと、<line-break>あの男はなぜ姿を消した…？<end>"
EN: "But why did he disappear after defeating Death Gigas...?<end>"
KO: "하지만 데스기가스를 쓰러뜨린 뒤, 그 남자는 왜 모습을 감췄지…?<end>"

## 1370342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラという男についていく<end>"
EN: "Follow the man named Sera<end>"
KO: "세라라는 남자를 따라간다<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラという男は信用できない<end>"
EN: "The man named Sera can't be trusted<end>"
KO: "세라라는 남자는 믿을 수 없다<end>"

## 1370344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついてくるなら、<line-break>それもいいだろう。<line-break>面倒は見んがな。<end>"
EN: "If you want to follow me, so be it. But don't expect me to look after you.<end>"
KO: "따라오겠다면 그것도 좋겠지. 돌봐 주진 않겠지만.<end>"

## 1370345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロイのあとを<line-break>追いかける気はないのか？<end>"
EN: "Don't you intend to go after Roy?<end>"
KO: "로이를 뒤쫓을 생각은 없나?<end>"

## 1370346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺に不信があるなら<line-break>いずれ答えてやる。<line-break>ただし、それは今ではない。<line-break>今、俺は急ぐ。<end>"
EN: "If you have doubts about me, I'll answer them in time. But not now. I'm in a hurry.<end>"
KO: "나를 못 믿겠다면 언젠가 답해 주지. 하지만 지금은 아니다. 지금 나는 서둘러야 한다.<end>"

## 1370347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0来い。<line-break>ロイの弟よ。<end>来い<line-break>ロイの妹よ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Come with me, Roy's brother.<end>Come with me, Roy's sister.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0와라. 로이의 남동생이여.<end>와라. 로이의 여동생이여.<end>"

## 1370348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアは<line-break>俺の大事なものを奪った…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>俺は強くなる、<line-break>…どんな手段を使ってもな。<line-break>そして、俺のすべてを取り戻す。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire took something precious from me. I will become stronger, whatever it takes. And I will take back everything that is mine.<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어는 내 소중한 것을 빼앗았다…. 난 강해질 거다, …무슨 수를 써서라도. 그리고 내 모든 것을 되찾는다.<end>"

## 1370349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここがディンガル帝国の首都<line-break>エンシャントだ。<line-break>　<line-break>町の中でまで群れては歩かん。<line-break>町を出るまでは別行動が冒険者同士の<line-break>暗黙のルールだ。憶えておけ。<end>"
EN: "This is Ancient, capital of the Dyneskal Empire. Adventurers don't travel in packs inside the city. Until we leave town, we go our separate ways. It's an unwritten rule. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "여기가 딩갈 제국의 수도 엔샨트다. 도시 안에서까지 무리 지어 다니진 않는다. 도시를 나설 때까지 따로 움직이는 게 모험자들 사이의 암묵적인 규칙이다. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1370350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1370351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（村の人たちを助けに行かなきゃ…。）<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>（…そう。<line-break>　兄さんと約束したんだから…。）<end>"
EN: "(I have to go help the villagers.) (That's right. I promised my brother.)<end>"
KO: "(마을 사람들을 구하러 가야 해….) (…그래. 오빠와 약속했으니까….)<end>"

## 1370352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遅くなってすまない。<line-break>神殿で取ってくるものが<line-break>あったのでな。<end>"
EN: "Sorry I'm late. There was something I had to fetch from the temple.<end>"
KO: "늦어서 미안하다. 신전에서 가져올 것이 있었거든.<end>"

## 1370353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>父上が、私に…？<line-break>護符か。ありがたい。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Father sent this for me? An amulet... I'm grateful.<end>"
KO: "응? 아버님이 내게…? 부적이군. 고맙다.<end>"

## 1370354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>も、<line-break>ご苦労だったな。　　　　　　<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>のおかげで、<line-break>父上の気遣いの心が<line-break>よく伝わってきたよ。<end>"
EN: "You did well too, <value:$28>. Thanks to you, <value:$28>, Father's concern came through clearly.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>도 수고 많았다. <value:$28> 덕분에 아버님의 배려하는 마음이 잘 전해졌어.<end>"

## 1370355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どうした？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong?<end>"
KO: "…무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 1370356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、そんな感覚をおぼえたか。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>つまり、この護符を私に届けるという<line-break>仕事を成し遂げたことで、お前の中に<line-break>ソウルポイントが蓄積されたのだ。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you felt it. By completing the task of delivering this amulet to me, you've accumulated Soul Points within yourself.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 그런 감각을 느꼈나. 즉, 이 부적을 내게 전달하는 일을 해낸 것으로 네 안에 소울 포인트가 축적된 거다.<end>"

## 1370357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けを求める声に応じ、<line-break>窮する人々に救いの手を差し伸べる。  <line-break> <line-break>そうすることにより<line-break>ソウルポイントが得られ、<line-break>さらに優れたソウルを導き寄せる。<end>"
EN: "Answer the cries of those in need and extend a helping hand to people in distress. By doing so, you earn Soul Points and attract even greater Souls.<end>"
KO: "도움을 구하는 목소리에 응하고 곤경에 처한 사람들에게 구원의 손길을 내민다. 그러면 소울 포인트를 얻고 더 뛰어난 소울을 이끌어 낼 수 있다.<end>"

## 1370358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、では行こうか。<end>"
EN: "Good. Shall we go, then?<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 가볼까.<end>"

## 1370359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？<line-break>調査の前に<line-break>やり残したことがあるのか？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Is there something you still need to do before the investigation?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 조사 전에 아직 할 일이 남았나?<end>"

## 1370360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば行ってくるといい。<line-break>私はここで待っている。<end>"
EN: "Then go and see to it. I'll wait here.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 다녀오도록 해. 나는 여기서 기다리겠다.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう大丈夫か？<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Then let's go investigate Miis Forest.<end>"

## 1370363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>朝も早いってのに、<line-break>魔物騒ぎで大変だったな。<end><value:$28>。<line-break>無事でよかった…。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>魔物騒ぎで森に行ったってっから<line-break>俺…、寝てらんなくって…。<line-break>でも、無事でホントよかったよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hey, <value:$28>. Quite a morning you've had, dealing with those monsters at the crack of dawn.<end><value:$28>. I'm so glad you're safe... When I heard you'd gone into the forest because of the monsters, I... I couldn't sleep. I'm just really glad you're all right.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0여, <value:$28>. 아침부터 마물 소동 때문에 고생했군.<end><value:$28>. 무사해서 다행이야…. 마물 소동 때문에 숲에 갔다고 해서 나… 잠도 못 자고…. 그래도 정말 무사해서 다행이야.<end>"

## 1370364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3魔物の出現に続いて地震かよ。<line-break>いよいよ、お前たち神器を守る一族の<line-break>出番ってわけだ。<line-break>ホント大変な家に生まれちまったな。<line-break>ま、がんばれよ。<line-break>お前なら神器を守り通せるさ。<end>お前たち神官家ってさ、<line-break>このミイスにある神器を<line-break>守ってるんだよな。<line-break>…ところで神器って<line-break>なんなんだ？<line-break>どんなもんなんだ？<end>ロイ様の持ってらっしゃる剣、<line-break>カッコいいよなあ。<line-break>聖剣・日光って言うんだってな。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3First monsters, and now an earthquake. Looks like it's finally time for your family of sacred-artifact guardians to step in. You really were born into one demanding family. Well, do your best. I know you can keep the sacred artifact safe.<end>Your priestly family protects the sacred artifact here in Miis, right? So... what is a sacred artifact, anyway? What's it like?<end>That sword Lord Roy carries is so impressive. I hear it's called the Holy Sword Sunlight.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3마물이 나타난 데 이어 지진까지라니. 이제 너희 신기를 지키는 일족이 나설 차례라는 거지. 정말 힘든 집안에 태어났구나. 뭐, 힘내라. 너라면 신기를 지켜 낼 수 있을 거야.<end>너희 신관 가문은 이 미이스에 있는 신기를 지키는 거지? …그런데 신기라는 게 대체 뭐야? 어떤 물건인데?<end>로이 님이 가지고 계신 검, 멋지더라. 성검 일광이라고 한다며.<end>"

## 1370365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0知らない！！<line-break>知らないんだ、俺たちゃ！<line-break>面ってなんなんだよ！？　おい！！<end>みんな…みんな…<line-break>あの女に燃やされて…！！<line-break>ああ…、ど、どうなってるんだよ！？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0We don't know! None of us know! What mask are you talking about?! Hey!<end>Everyone... everyone was burned by that woman...! Ah... w-what's happening?!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0몰라!! 모른다고, 우리는! 가면이 뭐냐고!? 야!!<end>모두… 모두… 그 여자한테 불타서…!! 아아… 대, 대체 어떻게 된 거야!?<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1370368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>。<line-break>無事でよかった…。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>魔物騒ぎがあって、<line-break>キミが退治に行ったっていうから、<line-break>心配で朝も早いのにここに来たんだよ。<end>魔物がこんな村近くの森にまで…。<line-break>心配でゆっくり寝てられないわ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>. I'm so glad you're safe... When I heard about the monsters and that you'd gone to fight them, I was so worried I came here at the crack of dawn.<end>Monsters, even in the forest this close to the village... I'm too worried to sleep soundly.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>. 무사해서 다행이야…. 마물 소동이 있었고 네가 퇴치하러 갔다고 해서 걱정돼서 아침 일찍 여기까지 왔어.<end>마물이 이런 마을 가까운 숲에까지…. 걱정돼서 편히 잘 수가 없네.<end>"

## 1370370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…<value:$28>…な…の？<line-break>に…逃げ…て…、は…早…く…　　<line-break>　<line-break>…わ…私…もう…ダメ…だから…<line-break>キ…キミ…だけでも…<end>イ…イヤ…死にたく…ない…<line-break>か、かめ…ん…なんて…<line-break>ホント…知らな…い…<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Is... is that you, <value:$28>? R-run... hurry... I... I'm done for... so at least you...<end>N-no... I don't want to die... A m-mask...? I really... don't know anything...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…<value:$28>…인…가? 도…도망…쳐… 어…어서… …나…난… 이제… 틀렸…으니까… 너…너라도…<end>아…안 돼… 죽기 싫…어… 가, 가면… 같은 건… 정말… 몰라…<end>"

## 1370371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3今の地震といい魔物騒ぎといい…、<line-break>天空神ノトゥーンさまが<line-break>お怒りなのかのう…。<line-break>ロセンでは悪い王が<line-break>民をいじめておるからのう。<end>今の地震といい魔物騒ぎといい…、<line-break>天空神ノトゥーンさまが<line-break>お怒りなのかのう…。　<line-break>ディンガルでは皇帝が<line-break>世界を救った勇者ネメア様を<line-break>いじめておるというからのう…。<end>今の地震といい魔物騒ぎといい…、<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>世界は滅ぶのではないですかのう。　　　　<line-break>リベルダムでは魔物を改造して<line-break>戦争のための道具にしておるそうですじゃ。<line-break>これでは神様もお怒りでしょうのう。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3With that earthquake and all this trouble with monsters... perhaps the Sky God Notun is angry. A wicked king in Rocen is tormenting the people, after all.<end>With that earthquake and all this trouble with monsters... perhaps the Sky God Notun is angry. Word is, the emperor in Dyneskal is persecuting Lord Nemea, the Hero who saved the world...<end>After that earthquake and all this trouble with monsters... do you think the world is coming to an end, <value:$28>? I hear monsters are being altered in Liberdam and turned into weapons of war. No wonder the gods are angry.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3이번 지진도 그렇고 마물 소동도 그렇고… 천공신 노툰 님이 노하신 걸까…. 로센에서는 나쁜 왕이 백성을 괴롭히고 있으니 말이야.<end>이번 지진도 그렇고 마물 소동도 그렇고… 천공신 노툰 님이 노하신 걸까…. 딩갈에서는 황제가 세계를 구한 용사 네메아 님을 괴롭힌다니 말이야….<end>이번 지진도 그렇고 마물 소동도 그렇고… <value:$28> 님, 세계가 멸망하는 건 아닐까요. 리벨덤에서는 마물을 개조해 전쟁 도구로 쓴다더군요. 이래서는 신께서도 노하실 겁니다.<end>"

## 1370372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3ロイ様は修行の旅から戻られて<line-break>いっそうたくましくなられましたな。<line-break>　<line-break>少々魔物がうろついたところで、<line-break>ロイ様と<value:$28>様が<line-break>おられれば安心ですな。<end><value:$28>様もロイ様のように、<line-break>神器の守護者としての実力をつけるため<line-break>いずれは旅に出られるのでしょうなあ。<end>ロイ様の持っておられる短剣は<line-break>日光という聖剣らしいですな。<line-break>修行の旅の途中見つけられたとか。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Lord Roy has grown even stronger since returning from his training journey. A few monsters wandering about are nothing to fear with Lord Roy and <value:$28> here.<end>I suppose that one day you too will set out on a journey, <value:$28>, to hone your skills as a sacred-artifact guardian, just as Lord Roy did.<end>The dagger Lord Roy carries is apparently a holy sword called Sunlight. It seems he found it during his training journey.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3로이 님은 수행 여행에서 돌아오신 뒤 더욱 듬직해지셨습니다. 마물이 좀 돌아다녀도 로이 님과 <value:$28>님이 계시면 안심입니다.<end><value:$28>님도 로이 님처럼 신기의 수호자로서 실력을 키우기 위해 언젠가는 여행을 떠나시겠지요.<end>로이 님이 지닌 단검은 일광이라는 성검이라고 합니다. 수행 여행 도중에 찾으셨다더군요.<end>"

## 1370373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec137-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2ヘンな女が空から降りてきて…<line-break>村が…村が…炎に包まれて…。<end>火だ…火だ…火だアア！<line-break>だ、誰か、助けてくれえ！<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2A strange woman came down from the sky... and the village... the whole village went up in flames...<end>Fire... fire... FIIIRE! S-somebody, help me!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2이상한 여자가 하늘에서 내려오고…마을이… 마을이… 불길에 휩싸여서….<end>불이다… 불이다… 불이야아아! 누, 누가 좀 살려줘어!<end>"
